# Fill in the Blanks
> Total Questions: 2016

[Q1] Technology has also made it possible for us to deliver the same lessons in a much more ________ manner.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 10/12/2024 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) lumbering
(b) resourceful
(c) incompetent
(d) encumbering
[ANS] b
[SOL]
resourceful 'Resourceful' means to able to find and use different ways to achieve your goals. The given sentence states that technology has also made it possible for us to deliver the same lessons in a much more resourceful manner. Hence, 'resourceful' is the most appropriate answer. • (a) lumbering - • (b) resourceful - • (c) incompetent - • (d) encumbering -
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'resourceful'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q2] All of those things you're willing to lose are what make you ________.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 10/12/2024 (Afternoon)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) recognisable
(b) unfortunately
(c) pessimist
(d) distract
[ANS] a
[SOL]
recognisable 'Recognisable' means easy to identify or recognise. The given sentence states that all of those things you're willing to lose are what make you recognisable. Hence, 'recognisable' is the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'recognisable'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q3] Fill in the blank: "Select the most appropriate form and tense of the bracketed verb in the following sentence to fill in the blank. I'm busy today. I'd rather we ________ (has) the meeting tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 10/12/2024 (Afternoon)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had
(b) Has been
(c) had been
(d) has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
had Simple past tense is used after "would rather / 'd rather"when the subjects are different. In the given sentence, the subjects 'I' and 'we' are different. Therefore, past form(V2) of the verb will be used. Hence, 'had(V2)' is the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'had'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q4] The difference between a male and a female still ________ in our society.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 10/12/2024 (Afternoon)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) insists
(b) requires
(c) defects
(d) persists
[ANS] d
[SOL]
persists 'Persist' means to continue to exist. The given sentence states that the difference between a male and a female still persists in our society. Hence, 'persists' is the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'persists'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

(b) blockade
(c) barricade
(d) reservoir
[ANS] d
[SOL]
reservoir 'Reservoir' means a place for storing liquid. The given sentence states that a dam is a barrier that stops the flow of water and results in the creation of a reservoir. [Q14] Fill in the blank: "The rising rate of inflation is making life very hard for poor ________ .
[EXAM: SSC CHSL Tier-2 (18/11/2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) some
(b) its
(c) the
(d) all
[ANS] c
[SOL]
the When article 'the' is used before an adjective, it becomes a common noun and refers to all those denoted by the adjective. 'The poor' refers to all the people who are poor. Hence, 'the' isthe most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'the'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

(b) on
(c) onto

[Q21] Fill in the blank: "In the following passage, some words have been deleted. Read the passage carefully and Leif Erikson is a Vikingbelieved to have sailed from the Green Lands to Vinland. Vinland is now thought to be the northern tip of Canada's Newfoundland island where found evidence of Erikson's in 1960s ________ .
[EXAM: SSC MTS 07/10/2024 (Afternoon)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) sailor; excavators; packed
(b) astronomer; sculptors; colony
(c) calligrapher; historians; community
(d) explorer; archaeologists; settlement
[ANS] d
[SOL]
explorer; archaeologists; settlement 'Explorer'means someone who travels to places about which very little is known. 'Archaeologist'is a scientist who studies human history by digging up human remains and artifacts. 'Settlement' means a place where people come to live. The given sentence states that Leif Erikson is a Viking explorer. Vinland is now thought to be the northern tip of Canada's Newfoundland island where archaeologists found evidence of Erikson's settlement in 1960s. Hence, 'explorer; archaeologists; settlement' is the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'explorer; archaeologists; settlement'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q27] Fill in the blank: "There was noone left in the world whom they could call ________ .

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]

(b) their's
(c) their
(d) theirs
[ANS] d

theirs 'Theirs' is a possessive pronoun used with the pronoun 'they'. 'Their' is a possessive adjective used with a noun. As there is no noun after the blank, we cannot use possessive adjective. Hence, 'theirs' is the most appropriate answer.

are working 'Is/am/are + V-ing' is grammatically the correct structure for the given sentence which is in the present continuous tense. Also, according to the "Subject-Verb Agreement Rule", a plural subject(Mittu and Bittu)always takes a plural verb(are). Hence, 'are working(V-ing)' is the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'are working'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had you
(b) hadn't you
(c) haven't you
(d) have you
[ANS] c
[SOL]
haven't you 'According to the "Question Tag rule", if the statement is positive, the question tag will be negative '& Vice-versa. However, the sentence and the question tag must be in the same tense. Similarly, the given sentence is positive so the question tag will be negative(haven't). Hence, 'haven't you' is the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'haven't you'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

(c) Desire
(d) Desirous
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Desirous is an adjective whichmeanshavingdesireofsomething or being wishful for something. Hence, 'desirousof'willbethemostappropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'Desirous'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

(a) Oh

(c) Alas

[ANS] a
[SOL]
Oh 
• Alas - used for showing sadness about something. 
• Hurray - used for expressing great pleasure, approval. Bravo- a word that people shout to show that they have enjoyed something that
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'Oh'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q654] Fill in the blank: "He was born in a business family and has a natural aptitude business ________ .

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) at
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "Aptitude for something"is the correct structure of the sentence. It means the ability or
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'for'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q655] Fill in the blank: "The wise try to improve themselves the fools keep blaming their luck ________ .
[EXAM: SSC CPO 24/11/2020 (Evening)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) therefore
(b) whereas
(c) still
(d) whether
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Whereas is a conjunction which is used for showing a fact that is different. For example, he eats meat, whereas she's a vegetarian. Similarly in this question, the wise improves and the fool blames his luck(different fact). Hence, whereas will be the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'whereas'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q656] Fill in the blank: "The fan was ________ directly my head.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) over
(b) on
(c) at
(d) Below
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Over is a preposition which means straight above something, butnot touching it. Fans are usually on the ceiling. Hence,'fan was over my head'is the most appropriate answer.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'over'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) strong

(c) heavy
(d) weighty
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'strong' to indicate physical or professional competence/rivalry in a competitive context.

(d) disperse
[ANS] a

'Spread awareness' is the standard collocating phrase used for conveying information or awareness to the public.

[Q659] She has been suffering from fever _____ three days.

(a) from

[ANS] b
[SOL]
'For' is used with a period of time (three days), which denotes duration in the present perfect continuous tense.

(d) go down
[ANS] a

'Go through' means to read or examine something thoroughly.

[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

[Q661] Hardly had I stepped out of the house _____ it began to rain heavily.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly... when' is used to show an action happening immediately after another.
उदा. 'Hardly' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।

[Q662] The prisoner made an unsuccessful attempt to _____ from the custody of the police.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) escape

(d) flee
[ANS] a

'Escape' is the standard term used when someone gets free from confinement or custody.

[Q663] If I _____ the king of my country, I would eradicate poverty completely.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) am
(b) were
(c) was
(d) have been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In conditional sentences expressing an unreal present wish, 'were' is used with all subjects.

[RULE] Conditional Sentences / Subjunctive Mood

[Q664] His novel is remarkable _____ its vivid description of rural life.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'remarkable' is followed by the preposition 'for' to state the reason or cause.

[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

[Q665] The committee will _____ the matter thoroughly before taking any disciplinary action.

(a) look into

(d) look for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Look into' means to investigate or examine a matter.

[Q666] Neither of the two candidates _____ qualified for the post.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) are
(b) is
(c) have
(d) were
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Neither of' is followed by a plural noun/pronoun but takes a singular verb.
उदा. 'Neither of' के बाद बहुवचन संज्ञा/सर्वनाम आती है लेकिन क्रिया हमेशा एकवचन (singular) होती है।

(a) from
(b) since
(c) for
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'since' for a point of time (like morning, Monday, 2010) in a present/past perfect continuous tense.
उदा. निश्चित समय (Point of time) जैसे morning, 2010 आदि के लिए 'since' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।

[Q668] The teacher advised the students to refrain ________ making noise in the library.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) with
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'refrain' is always followed by the preposition 'from'.
उदा. 'Refrain' शब्द के बाद हमेशा निश्चित प्रीपोजिशन 'from' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Refrain from)

[Q669] Hardly had I stepped out of the house ________ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Hardly' or 'Scarcely' is always followed by the conjunction 'when' (or 'before').
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है (No sooner के साथ 'than' आता है)।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions (Hardly... when)

[Q670] Neither the manager nor his subordinates ________ present at the meeting yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it. 'Subordinates' is plural, hence 'were' is correct.
उदा. जब दो सब्जेक्ट 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो वर्ब अपने सबसे नज़दीकी सब्जेक्ट (subordinates - बहुवचन) के अनुसार आएगी।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is followed by the preposition 'in' when denoting skill in a subject.
उदा. किसी विषय में निपुणता दिखाने के लिए 'proficient' के साथ प्रीपोजिशन 'in' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Proficient in)

[Q672] If I ________ the Prime Minister of India, I would eradicate corruption.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) have been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In unreal conditional sentences expressing a wish or hypothetical state, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक इच्छा (Hypothetical sentence) को व्यक्त करने के लिए सभी सब्जेक्ट्स के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Hypothetical)

[Q673] The police ________ investigating the cybercrime case for the last two weeks.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) has
(d) was
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Police' is treated as a plural noun in English grammar and takes a plural verb.

The verb 'confess' takes the preposition 'to' when admitting a fault or crime.

'Enough' is used to express the required quantity or sufficiency before plural countable or uncountable nouns.

[Q676] She spoke in such a low voice that she was ________ audible.

(a) hardly
(b) barely

(d) easily
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Hardly audible' means almost not audible. The context requires a negative adverb meaning almost not.

[ANS] a

Here, 'curb' means to restrain or control something undesirable. The context demands checking the spread of diseases.

[Q678] She has been working on this research project _____ over a year now.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
We use 'for' with a period of time (like 'over a year') in present perfect continuous tense.
उदा. अवधि (period of time) जैसे 'over a year' के साथ 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है क्योंकि यह निश्चित समय (point of time) नहीं बल्कि समय की अवधि है।

[Q679] Hardly had I stepped out of the house _____ it started pouring heavily.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) while
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before', whereas 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।

[Q680] A committee has been set up to _____ into the causes of the recent railway accident.

(a) investigate
(b) inquire
(c) explore
(d) search
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Inquire into' is a phrasal verb meaning to formally investigate a matter. 'Investigate' does not take 'into' after it directly in this structure.
उदा. 'Inquire into' एक फ्रेजल वर्ब है जिसका अर्थ किसी मामले की जांच करना होता है। 'Investigate' के बाद सीधे 'into' का प्रयोग नहीं होता।

[Q681] If the driver had applied the brakes on time, the collision _____ avoided.

[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) can be
(b) could be
(c) could have been
(d) will be
[ANS] c
[SOL]
This is the third conditional sentence. Structure: If + Past Perfect (had applied), Subject + would/could/might + have + V3.
उदा. यह थर्ड कंडीशनल वाक्य है। बनावट: If + Past Perfect, Subject + could have + V3 का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3)

(b) with

[ANS] b
[SOL]
We use the fixed preposition 'impressed with' when someone is pleased or admires a person's traits or skills.
उदा. किसी की योग्यताओं या विशेषताओं से प्रभावित होने के लिए 'impressed with' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions (Impressed with)

[Q683] Despite his rigorous training, he could not _____ the world record in the 100-meter sprint.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) beat
(b) break
(c) win
(d) exceed
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In sports and records, we use the verb 'break' (to break a record).

[Q684] Neeta prefers reading books _____ watching television during her leisure hours.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) than
(b) to
(c) over
(d) more than
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'prefer' takes the preposition 'to' when comparing two things, not 'than'.
उदा. 'Prefer' शब्द के साथ तुलना करने के लिए 'than' नहीं बल्कि स्थिर रूप से 'to' प्रीपोजिशन का प्रयोग होता है (prefer X to Y)।

[Q685] The continuous noise from the nearby construction site was a major source of _____ for the residents.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) annoyance
(b) pleasure
(c) comfort
(d) relief
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Construction noise causes irritation or annoyance. The context requires a negative noun.
उदा. निर्माण स्थल का शोर परेशानी या झुंझلاहट (annoyance) का कारण बनता है। वाक्य के संदर्भ के अनुसार नकारात्मक संज्ञा सही है।

[Q686] Not only _____ the scholarship, but she also secured the top rank in the university.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) she won
(b) did she win
(c) she woned
(d) had she won
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a sentence begins with a negative expression like 'Not only', it takes inversion (Auxiliary verb + Subject + Main verb).
उदा. जब वाक्य की शुरुआत 'Not only' से होती है, तो उसमें इन्वर्जन (Inversion) नियम लगता है, यानी सहायक क्रिया (did) कर्ता (she) से पहले आती है।

[Q687] The manager advised his team to keep their personal biases ______ when making crucial business decisions.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) aside
(b) away
(c) along
(d) above
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'keep aside' means to save something or to ignore/disregard something temporarily for a specific purpose.
उदा. Phrasal verb 'keep aside' का अर्थ किसी चीज़ को अलग रखना या ध्यान में न लाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

[Q688] She is proficient ______ classical dance and has won several awards at the national level.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is always followed by the preposition 'in' to indicate skill or expertise in a particular domain.
उदा. Adjective 'proficient' के बाद हमेशा preposition 'in' का प्रयोग होता है जिसका अर्थ किसी कार्य में कुशल होना है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q689] Hard ______ he worked, he could not clear the rigorous entrance examination.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) as
(b) though
(c) although
(d) even
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In inverted concessive clauses, 'Adjective/Adverb + as + subject + verb' is used to show contrast. Here, 'Hard as he worked' means 'Although he worked hard'.
उदा. Concession दर्शाने के लिए 'Adjective/Adverb + as + subject + verb' संरचना का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions and Inversion

[Q690] The historical monument was badly damaged ______ the continuous pollution in the city.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) due to
(b) because
(c) owing
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Due to' acts as an adjective phrase meaning 'caused by'. It correctly modifies the noun phrase 'damaged' in this context.
उदा. 'Due to' का प्रयोग कारण बताने के लिए किया जाता है जिसका अर्थ 'के कारण' होता है।
[RULE] Prepositional Phrases

---

[Q691] Neither the direct or nor the actors ______ present at the press conference yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of subject-verb agreement, when two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it (actors, which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े होने पर verb अपने सबसे नज़दीकी subject (actors जो कि plural है) के अनुसार आएगी।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q692] The government has launched a massive campaign to ______ awareness about water conservation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) spread
(b) generate
(c) create
(d) propagate
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Collocation: We 'create awareness' or 'raise awareness'. Among the choices, 'create' best fits the collocation for awareness.
उदा. Collocation के नियम के अनुसार awareness के साथ 'create' या 'raise' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Word Collocations

---

[Q693] By the time the rescue team arrived, the trapped miners ______ under ground for over forty-eight hours.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) have been
(b) had been
(c) were
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In past tense narrative structures, when an action happened before another past action, the past perfect tense ('had been') is used with 'by the time'.
उदा. भूतकाल की दो घटनाओं में से जो पहली हुई उसके लिए Past Perfect Tense ('had been') का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Tenses

---

[Q694] The newly appointed CEO decided to ______ all the redundant policies to stream line company operations.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) abolish
(b) abandon
(c) destroy
(d) vanish
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Abolish' means to formally put an end to a system, practice, or institution. It is precisely used for rules and policies.
उदा. किसी नियम, कानून या नीति को आधिकारिक रूप से समाप्त करने के लिए 'abolish' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Synonyms

---

[Q695] Scarcely had she stepped out of her house ______ it started pouring heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before'), unlike 'No sooner', which is followed by 'than'.
उदा. Correlative conjunction नियम के अनुसार 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q696] His speech was so ______ that many members in the audience were moved to tears.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) eloquent
(b) trivial
(c) redundant
(d) harsh
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Eloquent' means fluent or persuasive in speaking or writing, which fits the context of a moving speech.
उदा. 'Eloquent' का अर्थ सुवक्ता या प्रभावशाली होता है जो दिल को छू लेने वाले भाषण के लिए उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q697] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure a job ___ the competitive market.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) into
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use the preposition 'in' when referring to enclosed spaces, larger areas, or markets.
उदा. बड़े क्षेत्रों, बाज़ारों या बंद स्थानों के लिए preposition 'in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition of place/space

---

[Q698] The manager asked me if I ___ the report by the end of the day.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will finish
(b) could finish
(c) have finished
(d) finish
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In indirect speech, when the reporting verb is in the past tense ('asked'), the future tense changes to its past equivalent ('will' becomes 'could').
उदा. Indirect speech में यदि reporting verb past tense ('asked') में हो, तो future tense ('will') पाст equivalent ('could') में बदल जाता है।
[RULE] Direct and Indirect Speech / Tense consistency

---

[Q699] She is known for her ___ nature; she always shares her belongings with the needy.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) malicious
(b) parsimonious
(c) generous
(d) frugal
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Generous' means showing a readiness to give more of something than is strictly necessary or expected, especially money or time, which fits the context of sharing with the needy.
उदा. 'Generous' का अर्थ उदार होता है जो ज़रूरतमंदों के साथ बाँटने के संदर्भ में बिल्कुल सटीक बैठता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q700] Neither the teacher nor the students ___ present in the class room.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject nearest to it. Here, 'students' is plural, so the plural verb 'were' is used.
उदा. जब subjects 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो verb अपने सबसे नज़दीकी subject के अनुसार आती है। यहाँ 'students' plural है, इसलिए plural verb 'were' आएगी।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q701] Hardly had he stepped out of the house ___ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly' or 'Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' या 'before' कंजंक्शन का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conjunction pairs (Hardly... when)

---

[Q702] The committee ___ divided in their opinions regarding the new project.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a collective noun expresses division or individual members act separately, it takes a plural verb.
उदा. जब collective noun में मतभेद (division) दर्शाया जाए, तो plural verb ('were') का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement with Collective Nouns

---

[Q703] He takes pride ___ his academic achievements.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The fixed prepositional phrase is 'to take pride in' something.
उदा. निश्चित prepositional phrase 'to take pride in' होती है, जिसका अर्थ किसी बात पर गर्व करना है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q704] If I ___ the king of this country, I would eradicate poverty.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) have been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In unreal conditional sentences expressing a hypothetical present wish, 'were' is used for all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक (hypothetical) वाक्यों में सभी subjects के साथ verb 'were' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 2)

---

[Q705] The government has taken strict measures to ___ the rising inflation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) curb
(b) curve
(c) cause
(d) create
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Curb' means to restrain or control something undesirable like inflation. 'Curve' means a bend.
उदा. 'Curb' का अर्थ नियंत्रित करना होता है, जो महंगाई (inflation) को रोकने के संदर्भ में सही है।
[RULE] Confusing Words / Vocabulary

---

[Q706] The old man was accused ___ theft by his neighbor.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) of
(d) from
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The adjective/participle 'accused' is always followed by the preposition 'of'.
उदा. 'Accused' शब्द के साथ हमेशा fixed preposition 'of' का प्रयोग होता है ('accused of theft')।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition with Adjectives/Participles

---

[Q707] The manager was highly impressed _____ the candidate's communication skills during the interview.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) with
(b) by
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use the preposition 'impressed with' when referring to being pleased or struck by someone's abilities or qualities.
उदा. Adjective 'impressed' के बाद सामान्यतः preposition 'with' का प्रयोग किया जाता है जब किसी की योग्यताओं से प्रभावित होने की बात हो।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q708] Hardly had I stepped out of the house _____ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) while
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly... when' is used to show that one action happened immediately after another.
उदा. 'Hardly/Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा कंजंक्शन 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conjunction Pair

---

[Q709] Despite working day and night, he could not _____ enough profit to sustain his family.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) gain
(b) earn
(c) win
(d) acquire
[ANS] b
[SOL]
We use 'earn' when talking about receiving money as a salary, wage, or profit for work done.
उदा. वेतन, मजदूरी या लाभ (profit) प्राप्त करने के लिए 'earn' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है, न कि 'gain' या 'win' का।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q710] If the government _____ strict laws against corruption, the situation would have been different.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) enacted
(b) had enacted
(c) enacts
(d) has enacted
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In third conditional sentences expressing unfulfilled past conditions, the 'if' clause takes the past perfect tense (had + V3), and the main clause takes 'would have + V3'.
उदा. अवास्तविक भूतकाल की शर्त (Third Conditional) में 'if' खंड में past perfect (had + V3) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q711] The committee members could not reach a consensus; therefore, they _____ their decision until next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) put off
(b) put out
(c) put on
(d) put up
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Put off' is a phrasal verb meaning to postpone or delay an event or decision.
उदा. 'Put off' एक phrasal verb है जिसका अर्थ किसी कार्य या निर्णय को स्थगित करना (postpone करना) होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q712] She is gifted _____ a remarkable talent for painting and sketching.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) with
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The passive form 'gifted with' is used to indicate that someone possesses a natural talent or quality.
उदा. प्राकृतिक प्रतिभा या गुण से संपन्न होने के लिए 'gifted with' वाक्यांश का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q713] The scientist's novel theory was met with wide spread _____ from the academic community.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) scorn
(b) applause
(c) skepticism
(d) indifference
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In scientific contexts, a novel theory is often met with doubt or 'skepticism' before being validated.
उदा. वैज्ञानिक सिद्धांतों के संदर्भ में नए विचारों पर शुरुआत में संदेह (skepticism) व्यक्त किया जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q714] Not only the principal but also the teachers _____ present at the annual function.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are connected by 'not only... but also', the verb agrees with the subject closer to it (i.e., 'the teachers', which is plural).
उदा. 'Not only... but also' से जुड़ने वाले वाक्यों में verb अपने सबसे पास वाले Subject (यहाँ 'teachers' जो कि plural है) के अनुसार आती है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q715] His speech was so _____ that the audience remained glued to their seats for over an hour.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) tedious
(b) riveting
(c) monotonous
(d) prosaic
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Riveting' means completely engrossing or compelling, which fits the context of keeping an audience engaged.
उदा. 'Riveting' का अर्थ अत्यधिक आकर्षक या मंत्रमुग्ध करने वाला होता है, जिससे श्रोता अपनी सीटों से बंधे रहते हैं।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Adjective Choice

---

[Q716] The police launched an investigation to _____ the mystery behind the sudden disappearance of valuable artifacts.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) unravel
(b) invent
(c) fabricate
(d) conceal
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Unravel' means to investigate and solve a complex or mysterious problem.
उदा. किसी जटिल रहस्य या गुत्थी को सुलझाने के लिए 'unravel' शब्द का सबसे उपयुक्त प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Collocation

---

[Q717] She has been working on this project ______ three months.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) from
(c) for
(d) in
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Use 'for' to denote a period of time (duration like three months). 'Since' is used for a point of time.
उदा. निश्चित समय (point of time) के लिए 'since' और समय की अवधि (period of time) के लिए 'for' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition of Time (For vs Since)

---

[Q718] The manager ______ the proposal before making a final decision.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) looked after
(b) looked into
(c) looked over
(d) looked down
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Look over' means to examine something carefully. 'Look into' means to investigate.
उदा. 'Look over' का अर्थ किसी दस्तावेज़ या प्रस्ताव को सरसरी तौर पर या ध्यान से जाँच करना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q719] Hardly had he stepped out of the house ______ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'hardly/scarcely' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before').
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions / Correlatives

---

[Q720] Despite his best efforts, he could not ______ the target set by the company.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) achieve
(b) attain
(c) accomplish
(d) meet
[ANS] d
[SOL]
We use the collocation 'meet a target/goal/deadline'.
उदा. लक्ष्य, डेडलाइन या आवश्यकता को पूरा करने के लिए 'meet' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है ('meet the target')।
[RULE] Collocations / Vocabulary

---

[Q721] Neither the teacher nor the students ______ present in the class yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the nearest subject (students, which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े वाक्यों में क्रिया (verb) अपने सबसे नज़दीकी कर्ता के अनुसार आती है, यहाँ 'students' बहुवचन है इसलिए 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q722] The government has taken strict measures to ______ inflation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) bring down
(b) bring up
(c) bring about
(d) bring out
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Bring down' means to reduce or lower something like prices or inflation.
उदा. 'Bring down' का अर्थ किसी चीज़ को कम करना (जैसे महँगाई या कीमतें) होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q723] Honesty is the ______ policy for long-term success.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) good
(b) better
(c) best
(d) most good
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Superlative degree ('best') is required with the definite article 'the' when comparing more than two.
उदा. यहाँ सुपरलेटिव डिग्री 'best' का प्रयोग होगा क्योंकि 'the' के बाद superlative adjective आता है।
[RULE] Degrees of Comparison

---

[Q724] If I ______ the king, I would help all the poor people.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In unreal conditional sentences expressing a wish or hypothetical situation, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक इच्छा (Hypothetical sentence) को व्यक्त करने के लिए सभी कर्ताओं के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Subjunctive Mood)

---

[Q725] The criminal was found guilty ______ murder and sentenced to life imprisonment.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) with
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'guilty' is always followed by the preposition 'of'.
उदा. 'Guilty' के बाद निश्चित रूप से preposition 'of' का प्रयोग होता है (guilty of a crime)।

---

[Q726] She is proficient ______ playing the piano.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is typically followed by the preposition 'in' (or 'at', but 'in' is standard for skills/fields).
उदा. किसी कार्य या कला में निपुण होने के लिए 'proficient in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q727] The government has launched a massive campaign to ________ awareness about road safety.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) spread
(b) spreads
(c) spreading
(d) spreed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
To-infinitive takes the base form of the verb. 'To spread' is grammatically correct after 'to'.
उदा. 'To' के बाद हमेशा verb की base form (V1) का प्रयोग होता है, इसलिए 'spread' सही है।
[RULE] Infinitive (To + V1)

---

[Q728] She is proficient ________ both French and German.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is always followed by the preposition 'in'.
उदा. Adjective 'proficient' के बाद निश्चित preposition 'in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Proficient in)

---

[Q729] Despite working hard, he could not achieve the desired ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) result
(b) results
(c) outcome
(d) success
[ANS] d
[SOL]
In this context, 'success' fits best as an uncountable noun representing the attainment of a goal.
उदा. यहाँ लक्ष्य प्राप्त करने के संदर्भ में 'success' (सफलता) सबसे उपयुक्त noun है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Contextual Usage

---

[Q730] Neither the teacher nor the students ________ present in the class today.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of subject-verb agreement, when subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the nearest subject ('students', which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े होने पर verb अपने पास वाले subject के अनुसार आती है, यहाँ 'students' plural है इसलिए plural verb 'were' आएगी।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q731] The manager asked me ________ I could complete the project by Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) that
(b) if
(c) unless
(d) until
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In indirect speech for interrogative sentences (yes/no questions), 'if' or 'whether' is used.
उदा. Yes/No type interrogative sentences के indirect speech में conjunction के रूप में 'if' या 'whether' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Indirect Speech Conjunctions

---

[Q732] The manager looked ________ the complaints submitted by the employees.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) into
(c) after
(d) over
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Look into' is a phrasal verb meaning to investigate or examine carefully.
उदा. 'Look into' एक phrasal verb है जिसका अर्थ 'जाँच पड़ताल करना' होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs (Look into)

---

[Q733] Had the driver been alert, the accident ________ averted.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) could be
(b) could have been
(c) would be
(d) will have been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence in inversion form: Had + subject + V3, subject + would/could/should + have + V3.
उदा. यह Third Conditional का inversion है, जिसमें past unreal condition के लिए 'could have + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3 Inversion)

---

[Q734] The aroma of freshly baked bread always makes my mouth ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) water
(b) watering
(c) watered
(d) waters
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The causative verb 'make' takes a bare infinitive (V1 without 'to').
उदा. Causative verb 'make' के बाद bare infinitive (बिना 'to' वाली verb) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Causative Verbs (Make + V1)

---

[Q735] She spoke in such a low voice that she was barely ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) audible
(b) eligible
(c) legible
(d) visible
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Audible' means loud enough to be heard. 'Legible' is for handwriting, and 'eligible' is for qualifications.
उदा. 'Audible' का अर्थ है जो सुनाई दे सके। 'Legible' लिखावट के लिए और 'eligible' योग्यताबद्ध के लिए होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary (One-word substitution root)

---

[Q736] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure a promotion because he lagged ________ his peers in performance.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) behind
(b) beside
(c) beyond
(d) between
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'lag behind' means to fail to maintain the same pace or progress as others.
उदा. 'Lag behind' का अर्थ दूसरों से पिछड़ जाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs (Lag behind)

---

[Q737] The government has launched a massive campaign to create ________ about the importance of water conservation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) awareness
(b) ignorance
(c) negligence
(d) carelessness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextually, a government campaign aims to spread positive knowledge, which is 'awareness'.
उदा. संदर्भ के अनुसार, सरकारी अभियान का उद्देश्य जागरूकता फैलाना है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q738] She is proficient ________ painting and has won several national awards.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is followed by the preposition 'in' when denoting skill in an activity or subject.
उदा. किसी कला या विषय में निपुणता दर्शाने के लिए 'proficient' के बाद प्रीपोजिशन 'in' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Proficient in)

---

[Q739] The manager asked the employees to ________ their differences and work together for the project.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) set aside
(b) set up
(c) set off
(d) set down
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'set aside' means to ignore or save something for a particular purpose (here, ignoring differences).
उदा. 'Set aside' का अर्थ मतभेदों को नजरअंदाज करना या भुलाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs (Set aside)

---

[Q740] Neither the teacher nor the students ________ present in the class yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of Subject-Verb Agreement, when two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the closer subject ('students', which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े वाक्यों में वर्ब पास वाले सब्जेक्ट (students) के अनुसार प्लुरल (were) होगी।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q741] The committee examined the proposal thoroughly before arriving ________ a final decision.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) at
(b) to
(c) on
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The fixed expression is 'arrive at a decision' or 'arrive at a conclusion'.
उदा. सही वाक्यांश 'arrive at a decision' (निर्णय पर पहुँचना) होता है।
[RULE] Collocation / Fixed Preposition

---

[Q742] She speaks English so fluently that it is difficult to believe she is a ________ speaker.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) non-native
(b) foreign
(c) strange
(d) remote
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In contrast to her fluency, the logical word is 'non-native' speaker.
उदा. उसकी फ्लुएन्सी के विपरीत यहाँ 'non-native' (गैर-मूल) वक्ता शब्द एकदम सटीक है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Fill

---

[Q743] If I ________ the king of the country, I would eradicate poverty completely.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) be
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In unreal conditional sentences expressing a wish or hypothetical situation, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. अवास्तविक या काल्पनिक इच्छा वाले वाक्यों में सभी विषयों के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Subjunctive Mood)

---

[Q744] The athlete was disqualified from the race because he was found using a banned ________.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) substance
(b) material
(c) matter
(d) object
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In sports terminology, performance-enhancing drugs or prohibited chemicals are referred to as 'substances'.
उदा. खेल शब्दावली में प्रतिबंधित दवाओं या रसायनों को 'banned substance' कहा जाता है।
[RULE] Collocation / Vocabulary

---

[Q745] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure the promotion because he lacked the necessary ______ for the higher post.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) qualifications
(b) qualities
(c) quantities
(d) questions
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Qualifications' refers to official credentials, educational background, or skills required for a job. Here, career promotion requires specific credentials.
उदा. किसी पद के लिए आवश्यक आधिकारिक योग्यता या क्रेडेंशियल को 'qualifications' कहा जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q746] The manager asked the employees to ______ their differences and work together as a team.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) put aside
(b) put down
(c) put off
(d) put on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Phrasal verb 'put aside' means to ignore or forget something (like differences) for a specific purpose.
उदा. 'Put aside' एक फ्रेजल वर्ब है जिसका अर्थ अपने मतभेदों को भुलाकर एक तरफ रखना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q747] She has been suffering ______ a severe headache since morning.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) with
(c) by
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'suffer' is invariably followed by the preposition 'from' when referring to an illness or pain.
उदा. बीमारी या दर्द से पीड़ित होने के लिए 'suffer' के साथ हमेशा 'from' प्रीपोजिशन का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q748] Had the authorities taken timely action, the disaster ______.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) would have been averted
(b) will be averted
(c) would avert
(d) has been averted
[ANS] a
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure (Past Unreal). Structure: If + Past Perfect (Had + V3), Subject + would/could/might + have + V3.
उदा. यह थर्ड कंडीशनल वाक्य है। यदि मुख्य खंड में 'Had + V3' हो, तो परिणाम खंड में 'would have + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q749] The committee reached a consensus after hours of ______ discussion.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) protracted
(b) promoted
(c) protected
(d) protested
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Protracted' means lasting for a long time or longer than usual, which fits the context of hours of discussion leading to consensus.
उदा. 'Protracted' का अर्थ है बहुत लंबी अवधि तक चलने वाला (लंबी चर्चा)।
[RULE] Adjective Vocabulary

---

[Q750] Hardly had I stepped out of the house ______ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly / Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before' (not 'than').
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' या 'before' कंजंक्शन का प्रयोग होता है ('than' का प्रयोग 'No sooner' के साथ होता है)।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q751] The scientist explained the complex theory in simple terms so that every one could ______ it.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) comprehend
(b) comply
(c) complete
(d) compete
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Comprehend' means to grasp mentally or understand fully. The context requires understanding a complex theory.
उदा. 'Comprehend' का अर्थ किसी बात या सिद्धांत को पूरी तरह से समझना होता है।
[RULE] Verb Vocabulary

---

[Q752] He is proficient ______ mathematics and can solve any problem within minutes.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is typically followed by the preposition 'in' when denoting skill in a subject or activity.
उदा. किसी विषय या क्षेत्र में निपुण होने के लिए 'proficient' के बाद 'in' प्रीपोजिशन का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with Adjectives

---

[Q753] Neither the teacher nor the students ______ present in the class room today.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) is
(d) has been
[ANS] a
[SOL]
According to the Subject-Verb Agreement rule, when two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it (here, 'students', which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े वाक्यों में वर्ब अपने सबसे पास वाले सब्जेक्ट (यहाँ 'students' जो कि बहुवचन है) के अनुसार आती है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q754] The government has implemented new policies to ______ economic growth in the rural sectors.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) stimulate
(b) simulate
(c) stipulate
(d) strangulate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Stimulate' means to encourage or spur activity or growth. 'Simulate' means to imitate. Context demands boosting growth.
उदा. 'Stimulate' का अर्थ किसी गतिविधि या आर्थिक विकास को प्रोत्साहित करना या बढ़ावा देना होता है।
[RULE] Confusing Words / Vocabulary

---

[Q755] The manager was ________ the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) aware of
(b) aware to
(c) aware at
(d) aware in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "be aware of" is the correct collocation.
उदाहरण: वह नई नीति के बारे में जागरूक था।
[RULE] Preposition after "aware"

---

[Q756] They have been ________ since morning.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) working
(b) work
(c) worked
(d) works
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect continuous tense requires the –ing form.
उदाहरण: वे सुबह से काम कर रहे हैं।
[RULE] Present perfect continuous verb form

---

[Q757] Please put the documents ________ the file.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The object "file" is a container; we use "in" for inside.
उदाहरण: दस्तावेज़ फ़ाइल में रखें।
[RULE] Preposition for location inside a container

---

[Q758] He is very ________ the exam.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) prepared to
(b) prepared for
(c) prepared from
(d) prepared at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Prepared for" is the correct preposition with "prepared".
उदाहरण: वह परीक्षा के लिए तैयार है।
[RULE] Preposition after "prepared"

---

[Q759] The children were ________ the class room.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) enter
(b) entered
(c) entering
(d) enters
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Present continuous requires the –ing form.
उदाहरण: बच्चे कक्षा में प्रवेश कर रहे थे।
[RULE] Present continuous verb form

---

[Q760] She has been studying English ________ five years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"For" is used with a duration of time.
उदाहरण: वह पाँच साल से अंग्रेज़ी पढ़ रही है।
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q761] The company is known ________ its excellent customer service.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Known for" is the correct collocation.
उदाहरण: कंपनी अपनी उत्कृष्ट ग्राहक सेवा के लिए जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Preposition after "known"

---

[Q762] He was accused ________ stealing the money.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) for
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "accused of" is the correct collocation.
उदाहरण: उसे पैसे चुराने के लिए आरोपित किया गया।
[RULE] Preposition after "accused"

---

[Q763] The painting is ________ 19th century.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) of
(c) in
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"From" indicates the period of origin.
उदाहरण: पेंटिंग 19वीं शताब्दी से है।
[RULE] Preposition for period of origin

---

[Q764] The report will be submitted ________ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) on
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By" indicates the deadline.
उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट शुक्रवार तक जमा की जानी है।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'by'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition for deadline

---

[Q765] She has been living ____ Germany for five years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "in" is used with countries and cities. In English: She has been living **in Germany**.
हिंदी: "in" शब्द का प्रयोग देशों के साथ होता है।
[RULE] Preposition after 'live in'

---

[Q766] He ____ a book when I called him.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was reading
(b) read
(c) has read
(d) reading
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past continuous tense is used for an action that was in progress at a specific time. In English: He **was reading** a book.
हिंदी: 'was reading' का प्रयोग जारी क्रिया के लिए किया जाता है।
[RULE] Past continuous tense

---

[Q767] The company ______ the new product last month.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) launched
(b) launch
(c) launches
(d) launching
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past simple form is needed for a completed action in the past. In English: The company **launched** the new product.
हिंदी: भूतकाल के लिए 'launched' सही है।
[RULE] Past simple tense

---

[Q768] The verdict was reached ____ the panel's recommendation.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "on" is used after 'reach' when referring to a recommendation or advice. In English: The verdict was reached **on** the panel's recommendation.
हिंदी: 'reach on' का प्रयोग सुझाव के लिये होता है।
[RULE] Preposition after 'reach'

---

[Q769] There are ____ people in the auditorium.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a lot
(b) lots
(c) much
(d) many
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The quantifier "many" is used with countable nouns. In English: There are **many** people in the auditorium.
हिंदी: गिनती योग्य संज्ञा के लिए "many" प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Quantifier for countable nouns

---

[Q770] The book is worth ____ dollars.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) some
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When a noun is singular and countable, use the indefinite article "a". In English: The book is worth **a** dollar.
हिंदी: एकवचन संज्ञा के लिये "a" का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Use of indefinite article 'a'

---

[Q771] The team will be ready ____ the night.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By" indicates a deadline. In English: The team will be ready **by** the night.
हिंदी: समय सीमा बताने के लिये "by" का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' for deadline

---

[Q772] The law was passed ____ the parliament.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) by
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"By" is used after 'pass' to indicate the entity that enacted the law. In English: The law was passed **by** the parliament.
हिंदी: 'pass by' का प्रयोग विधेय के लिये होता है।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' after 'pass'

---

[Q773] She is known ____ her generosity.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) by
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Known for" expresses the reason or attribute. In English: She is known **for** her generosity.
हिंदी: किसी गुण के लिये "known for" का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition after 'known'

---

[Q774] We need to finish the project ____ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) on
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By Friday" denotes a deadline. In English: We need to finish the project **by** Friday.
हिंदी: समय सीमा बताने के लिये "by" का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' for deadline

---

[Q775] He has been working here ____ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) over
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Since" is used with a specific point in time. In English: He has been working here **since** 2010.
हिंदी: किसी विशिष्ट समय के लिये "since" प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Use of 'since' for a point in time

---

[Q776] The student was praised ____ his hard work.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Praised for" indicates the reason of praise. In English: The student was praised **for** his hard work.
हिंदी: प्रशंसा के कारण बताने के लिये "praised for" इस्तेमाल होता है।
[RULE] Preposition after 'praised'

---

[Q777] He has been studying English ___________ he was 10 years old.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) until
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'since' is used with the present perfect tense to indicate the starting point of an action. उदाहरण: He has been studying English since he was 10 years old.
[RULE] Since + present perfect

---

[Q778] She was ___________ her friends when she received the good news.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) meeting
(b) meet
(c) to meet
(d) met
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The present participle 'meeting' is used after 'was' to form a past continuous tense. उदाहरण: She was meeting her friends when she received the good news.
[RULE] Past continuous: was + present participle

---

[Q779] The manager asked the employees to submit their reports ___________ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) in
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'by' is used to indicate a deadline. उदाहरण: Submit your reports by Friday.
[RULE] Deadline: by + time

---

[Q780] The article was published in the journal ___________ the year 2020.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) by
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'in' is used with years. उदाहरण: Published in 2020.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'in'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Year + in

---

[Q781] We should be careful ___________ the fragile vase.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) near
(b) to
(c) toward
(d) of
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase 'be careful to' is used to indicate precaution. उदाहरण: Be careful to avoid breaking the vase.
[RULE] 'be careful to' + verb

---

[Q782] The team won the match by a margin of ___________ points.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) several
(b) many
(c) much
(d) some
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'several' is used to indicate more than two but not many. उदाहरण: The team won by several points.
[RULE] Adjective for countable nouns

---

[Q783] He has never seen a _________ bird.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) beautiful
(b) beauty
(c) beautifully
(d) beautifull
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjectives like 'beautiful' are used before nouns. उदाहरण: He has never seen a beautiful bird.
[RULE] Adjective + noun

---

[Q784] The student is _________ to finish the assignment before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eager
(b) eagerly
(c) eagerness
(d) eagered
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'eager' describes a feeling; the adverb form is 'eagerly' (used with verbs). उदाहरण: She is eager to finish the assignment.
[RULE] Adjective before noun or verb phrase

---

[Q785] I will ___________ you at the station tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) meet
(b) meets
(c) meeting
(d) met
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The simple future 'will' is followed by the base form of the verb. उदाहरण: I will meet you tomorrow.
[RULE] Will + base form of verb

---

[Q786] The teacher asked the students to write their names ___________ the paper.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'on' is used for writing on a surface. उदाहरण: Write your name on the paper.
[RULE] Write on + surface

---

[Q787] The doctor advised him to stop smoking ___________ he feels ill again.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) if
(b) when
(c) while
(d) as
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The conjunction 'when' indicates a future time or condition. उदाहरण: Stop smoking when you feel ill again.
[RULE] Conditional future + when

---

[Q788] The government has launched a massive campaign to ___ awareness about road safety.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) spread
(b) spreads
(c) spreading
(d) spreed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
To-infinitive 'to' is followed by the base form of the verb (V1). Here, 'to spread' is correct.
उदा. Infinitives (to + V1) में verb की पहली फॉर्म का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Infinitive (to + V1)

---

[Q789] She is proficient ___ French and can translate this document easily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is always followed by the preposition 'in' when talking about skills or languages.
उदा. 'Proficient' के बाद हमेशा preposition 'in' का प्रयोग होता है (जैसे- proficient in English)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Proficient in)

---

[Q790] Hardly had I stepped out of the house ___ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before').
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के जोड़े के रूप में हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions (Hardly... when)

---

[Q791] Despite working hard, he could not achieve the desired ___.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) result
(b) results
(c) resulted
(d) resulting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The determiner 'the' followed by an adjective 'desired' requires a singular or plural noun. Here, 'result' fits the context as a countable noun.
उदा. Article 'the' और adjective के बाद noun का प्रयोग होता है। Context के अनुसार 'result' सही है।
[RULE] Noun Form Usage

---

[Q792] The manager asked the employee to explain the reason ___ he was late.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) why
(b) because
(c) that
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When 'reason' is mentioned, we use 'why' to introduce the clause explaining the cause, avoiding redundant use of 'reason why because'.
उदा. 'Reason' के बाद clause को जोड़ने के लिए 'why' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Relative Adverb (Reason why)

---

[Q793] Amit is accustomed ___ working for long hours under pressure.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) with
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'accustomed to' means being familiar with something, and it is followed by a gerund (V+ing).
उदा. 'Accustomed' के साथ fixed preposition 'to' आता है और उसके बाद gerund (V1+ing) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Accustomed to)

---

[Q794] Had the captain informed us earlier, we ___ attended the strategy meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) would have
(b) will have
(c) would
(d) had
[ANS] a
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure: If + Past Perfect (Had + V3), Subject + would have + V3.
उदा. Third conditional में यदि वाक्य 'Had + Subject + V3' से शुरू हो, तो दूसरे clause में 'would have + V3' आता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3)

---

[Q795] The teacher advised the students to refrain ___ making noise in the corridor.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) against
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'refrain' is always followed by the preposition 'from' + gerund.
उदा. 'Refrain' (दूर रहना या बचना) के साथ हमेशा preposition 'from' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Refrain from)

---

[Q796] She spoke in such a low voice that she was barely ___.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) audible
(b) eligible
(c) legible
(d) visible
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: 'Audible' means loud enough to be heard. Since she spoke in a low voice, she was barely audible.
उदा. 'Audible' का अर्थ है जो सुनने योग्य हो। कम आवाज़ में बोलने के कारण वह मुश्किल से सुनाई दे रही थी।
[RULE] Vocabulary / One-word Context

---

[Q797] The manager _____ the proposal before submitting it to the board of directors.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) looked into
(b) looked up
(c) looked over
(d) looked after
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Look over' means to examine something carefully. Here, the manager examined the proposal before submission.
उदा. 'Look over' का अर्थ किसी चीज़ की सावधानीपूर्वक जांच करना होता है। प्रस्ताव को जमा करने से पहले उसकी जांच की गई।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q798] She is proficient _____ mathematics and solves complex problems with ease.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is always followed by the preposition 'in'.
उदा. विशेषण 'proficient' (निपुण) के साथ हमेशा पूर्वसर्ग 'in' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q799] Had the authorities taken timely action, the disaster _____ prevented.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) could be
(b) could have been
(c) would be
(d) will have been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence in inverted form (Had + subject + V3). The main clause must take 'would/could/might + have + V3'.
उदा. यह थर्ड कंडीशनल वाक्य है। मुख्य खंड में 'could have been' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q800] The committee members were _____ in their decision, as no one opposed the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) unanimous
(b) anonymous
(c) ambiguous
(d) unanimously
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We need an adjective here to describe the committee members. 'Unanimous' means fully in agreement.
उदा. यहाँ समिति के सदस्यों का वर्णन करने के लिए विशेषण चाहिए। 'Unanimous' का अर्थ 'सर्वसम्मति से' होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Adjectives

---

[Q801] Despite his best efforts, he could not _____ his point across to the audience.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) bring
(b) get
(c) take
(d) put
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Get across' is a phrasal verb meaning to successfully communicate an idea or message.
उदा. 'Get across' एक फ्रेजल वर्ब है जिसका अर्थ अपने विचार या संदेश को सफलतापूक समझाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q802] Neither the teacher nor the students _____ present in the class room when I arrived.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the closer subject. Here, 'students' is plural, so 'were' is correct.
उदा. जब दो कर्ता 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो क्रिया निकटतम कर्ता के अनुसार आती है। 'students' बहुवचन है, अतः 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q803] The startup company made a huge profit by capitalizing _____ the new market trends.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) upon
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb/idiomatic expression is 'capitalize on', meaning to use a situation to gain an advantage.
उदा. 'Capitalize on' का सही उपयोग होता है, जिसका अर्थ किसी स्थिति का लाभ उठाना है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions / Idiomatic Usage

---

[Q804] His speech was so _____ that many people in the audience were moved to tears.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) trenchant
(b) poignant
(c) redundant
(d) insolent
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Poignant' means evoking a keen sense of sadness or regret, fitting the context of being moved to tears.
उदा. 'Poignant' का अर्थ मार्मिक या दिल को छू लेने वाला होता है, जो आंसुओं तक पहुँचाने के संदर्भ में उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q805] The police _____ investigating the robbery case for over a week now.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) has been
(b) have been
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Police' is treated as a plural noun in English grammar, and 'for over a week now' indicates the present perfect continuous tense.
उदा. अंग्रेजी व्याकरण में 'Police' को बहुवचन संज्ञा माना जाता है, और समय अवधि के कारण 'have been' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Tenses & Noun Number

---

[Q806] She has been working in this organization ________ five years.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'for' to denote a period of time (duration) like 'five years'. 'Since' is used for a point of time.
उदा. निश्चित समय (point of time) के लिए 'since' और अनिश्चित अवधि (period of time) के लिए 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है। यहाँ 'five years' एक अवधि है, अतः 'for' आएगा।
[RULE] Prepositions of Time (Since vs For)

---

[Q807] The teacher advised the students to ________ attention to what was being taught.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) bring
(b) pay
(c) give
(d) make
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The standard collocation is 'pay attention', meaning to apply your mind to something.
उदा. स्थिर शब्द-युग्म (collocation) के अनुसार 'attention' के साथ क्रिया 'pay' का प्रयोग किया जाता है ('pay attention' यानी ध्यान देना)।
[RULE] Collocations / Fixed Phrases

---

[Q808] If I ________ the king of this country, I would eradicate poverty completely.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In unreal conditional sentences expressing a hypothetical wish, 'were' is used for all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक इच्छा (Hypothetical condition) को दर्शाने वाले वाक्यों में सभी कर्ताओं के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, चाहे कर्ता singular हो या plural।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Subjunctive Mood)

---

[Q809] The novel was so interesting that I could not put it ________.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) down
(b) up
(c) off
(d) away
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Put down' is a phrasal verb meaning to stop holding or reading something.
उदा. 'Put down' एक फ्रेजल वर्ब है जिसका अर्थ होता है किसी चीज़ को नीचे रखना या पढ़ना बंद करना। उपन्यास दिलचस्प था इसलिए मैं उसे रख नहीं पाया।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q810] The police are looking ________ the matter to find the culprit.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) at
(b) into
(c) for
(d) after
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Look into' means to investigate or examine a problem or situation.
उदा. 'Look into' एक फ्रेजल वर्ब है जिसका अर्थ 'जांच करना' (investigate) होता है। पुलिस मामले की जांच कर रही है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs (Prepositional)

---

[Q811] Ambition must be ________ by virtue if one wants to achieve true greatness.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) tempered
(b) tempted
(c) tailored
(d) tackled
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Tempered' means moderated or toned down by a counteracting force. Here, ambition needs to be balanced with virtue.
उदा. 'Tempered' का अर्थ किसी चीज़ को संतुलित या नियंत्रित करना होता है। सही महानता पाने के लिए महत्वाकांक्षा को सदाचार से नियंत्रित किया जाना चाहिए।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q812] Rohan prefers reading books ________ watching television in his leisure time.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) than
(b) to
(c) over
(d) more than
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'prefer' is always followed by the preposition 'to', not 'than'.
उदा. 'Prefer' शब्द के बाद हमेशा 'to' प्रीपोजिशन का प्रयोग होता है, 'than' का नहीं।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions with Adjectives

---

[Q813] Not only the principal but also the teachers ________ responsible for the school's success.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) has
(d) was
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When subjects are connected by 'not only... but also', the verb agrees with the closer subject ('teachers', which is plural).
उदा. 'Not only... but also' से जुड़े वाक्यों में क्रिया पास वाले कर्ता के अनुसार आती है। 'teachers' बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'are' सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q814] The manager refused to ______ the employee's request for leave.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) accede to
(b) accidently
(c) accept with
(d) access to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Accede to' means to agree to a request or demand. 
उदा. 'Accede to' एक फिक्स प्रपोजीशन वाला फ्रेज है जिसका अर्थ अनुरोध या मांग स्वीकार करना होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition / Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q815] Despite his best efforts, he could not ______ the examination.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) clear
(b) pass through
(c) pass out
(d) succeed into
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We say 'pass an examination' or 'clear an examination'. 'Pass out' means to faint or graduate from a military academy. 
उदा. परीक्षा उत्तीर्ण करने के लिए 'pass' या 'clear' का प्रयोग होता है। 'Pass out' का अर्थ बेहोश होना होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Collocations

---

[Q816] It is high time the government ______ strict action against corruption.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) takes
(b) took
(c) has taken
(d) had taken
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Phrases like 'It is time', 'It is high time' are followed by a verb in the Past Indefinite (V2) tense. 
उदा. 'It is high time' के बाद हमेशा Past Indefinite Tense (V2 - took) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood / Tenses

---

[Q817] The old man is suffering ______ a rare blood disorder.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) with
(c) of
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct collocation is 'suffering from'. 
उदा. बीमारी से पीड़ित होने के लिए हमेशा 'suffering from' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with Verbs

---

[Q818] Only by working consistently ______ you be able to crack this exam.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) will
(b) can
(c) would
(d) should
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Inversion rule: A sentence starting with a negative or restrictive adverbial expression ('Only by...') takes inversion (Auxiliary verb + Subject + Main verb). 
उदा. 'Only by' से वाक्य शुरू होने पर इनवर्जन नियम लगता है, जिससे भविष्य की संभावना के लिए 'will' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Inversion and Modals

---

[Q819] A large number of trees ______ cut down for the highway expansion.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) has
(d) is
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'A large number of' takes a plural noun and a plural verb. 
उदा. 'A large number of' के साथ हमेशा बहुवचन संज्ञा और बहुवचन क्रिया ('were') आती है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement (Quantifiers)

---

[Q820] She spoke in such a low voice that she was barely ______.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) audible
(b) visible
(c) legible
(d) eligible
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Audible' means able to be heard. Since it's about voice, 'audible' is the correct vocabulary word. 
उदा. आवाज सुनने योग्य होने के लिए 'audible' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / One-Word Substitutes in Context

---

[Q821] He ___ to complete the assignment by the end of the week.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was obliged
(b) was obliged to
(c) obliged
(d) obliging
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use of the infinitive form after the adjective *obliged* when indicating a required action.
उदाहरण: वह असाइनमेंट को सप्ताह के अंत तक पूरा करने के लिए बाध्य था।
[RULE] Adjective + infinitive

---

[Q822] The committee demands that the project ______________ within six months.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) be completed
(b) complete
(c) completed
(d) completes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Subjunctive mood after *demand* requires the infinitive with *be*.
उदाहरण: समिति मांगती है कि परियोजना छह महीनों के भीतर पूर्ण हो।
[RULE] Subjunctive + be

---

[Q823] She has been ___ a new job for the past three months.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) searching
(b) to search
(c) searched
(d) search
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present perfect continuous is used for an action that started in the past and continues to the present.
उदाहरण: वह तीन महीनों से नई नौकरी की तलाश में है।
[RULE] Present perfect continuous

---

[Q824] The new policy will not come into force until ___ 1 January 2025.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the
(b) a
(c) on
(d) in
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Fixed preposition *on* is used with dates.
उदाहरण: नई नीति 1 जनवरी 2025 तक प्रभावी नहीं होगी।
[RULE] Preposition with date

---

[Q825] The manager was praised ___ his team’s efficiency.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) at
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used after *praised* to indicate the reason.
उदाहरण: प्रबंधक को टीम की दक्षता के लिए प्रशंसा की गई।
[RULE] Preposition after praise

---

[Q826] If you ___ the instructions carefully, you will not make any mistakes.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) read
(b) reading
(c) readed
(d) to read
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Zero conditional uses simple present in both clauses.
उदाहरण: यदि आप निर्देशों को ध्यान से पढ़ते हैं, तो आप कोई गलती नहीं करेंगे।
[RULE] Zero conditional

---

[Q827] The students are expected ___ the report by Friday.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to submit
(b) submitting
(c) submit
(d) submitted
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive form after *expected*.
उदाहरण: छात्रों से रिपोर्ट शुक्रवार तक जमा करने की अपेक्षा है।
[RULE] Expected + infinitive

---

[Q828] He has been working ___ 10 hours a day for the past month.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the preposition *for* with duration.
उदाहरण: वह पिछले महीने से हर दिन 10 घंटे काम कर रहा है।
[RULE] Preposition with duration

---

[Q829] She was ___ the best student in her batch.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) considered
(b) to consider
(c) consider
(d) considering
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle form after *was* to express passive.
उदाहरण: उसे अपनी कक्षा में सबसे अच्छे छात्र के रूप में माना गया।
[RULE] Passive with past participle

---

[Q830] The company plans to launch the new product ___ next quarter.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *in* with a general time period.
उदाहरण: कंपनी अगले तिमाही में नया उत्पाद लॉन्च करने की योजना बना रही है।
[RULE] Preposition with time period

---

[Q831] The committee was _______ to settle the dispute.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) responsible
(b) responsibility
(c) responsibly
(d) responsiblely
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a linking verb such as 'was', an adjective is used to describe the subject.
उदाहरण: The committee was responsible to settle the dispute.
[RULE] Adjective used as complement after linking verb

---

[Q832] He was born _______ 1990.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'in' is used with years.
उदाहरण: He was born in 1990.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'in'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition with year

---

[Q833] The project was completed _______ the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) before
(b) during
(c) after
(d) by
[ANS] d
[SOL]
'by' indicates up to the specified time.
उदाहरण: The project was completed by the deadline.
[RULE] Preposition with deadline

---

[Q834] She has been working here _______ 5 years.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'for' expresses duration.
उदाहरण: She has been working here for 5 years.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'for'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition with duration

---

[Q835] The scientist was awarded the prize _______ his contribution to medicine.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'for' is used to indicate the reason.
उदाहरण: The scientist was awarded the prize for his contribution to medicine.
[RULE] Preposition with award

---

[Q836] Please make sure to submit the report _______ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'on' is used with days.
उदाहरण: Please make sure to submit the report on Friday.
[RULE] Preposition with day

---

[Q837] The new policy will take effect _______ next month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'in' is used with months.
उदाहरण: The new policy will take effect in next month.
[RULE] Preposition with month

---

[Q838] He _______ the problem after a long discussion.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) solved
(b) solving
(c) solve
(d) solvedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past tense of 'solve' is 'solved'.
उदाहरण: He solved the problem after a long discussion.
[RULE] Past tense verb form

---

[Q839] The book was written _______ a renowned author.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'by' indicates authorship.
उदाहरण: The book was written by a renowned author.
[RULE] Preposition with author

---

[Q840] The child was _______ to play with the toy.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) allowed
(b) allowing
(c) allows
(d) allow
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'was', the past participle 'allowed' is used.
उदाहरण: The child was allowed to play with the toy.
[RULE] Past participle as complement after linking verb

---

[Q841] The committee decided ___ to postpone the meeting until next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) of
(c) for
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the verb 'decided', an infinitive form is used. The correct preposition is 'to' to form the infinitive 'to postpone'.
उदाहरण: उन्होंने बैठक को अगले हफ्ते तक स्थगित करने के लिए निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Infinitive after decision verbs

---

[Q842] She has been working here ___ five years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'for' is used with periods of time. It indicates the duration of her employment.
उदाहरण: वह यहाँ पाँच साल से काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q843] The children were excited ___ the surprise party.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) about
(b) of
(c) for
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Excited' is followed by 'about' to express the object of excitement.
उदाहरण: बच्चे आश्चर्यजनक पार्टी के बारे में उत्साहित थे।
[RULE] Preposition after 'excited'

---

[Q844] His decision to quit the job was unexpected ___ every one.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) to
(d) than
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'unexpected ___ everyone' uses 'by' to indicate that everyone was surprised.
उदाहरण: उसकी नौकरी छोड़ने का निर्णय सभी को आश्चर्यचकित कर गया।
[RULE] Preposition by in passive sense

---

[Q845] The manager was praised ___ his outstanding performance.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'for' is used after 'praised' to indicate the reason.
उदाहरण: प्रबंधक को उनकी उत्कृष्ट प्रदर्शन के लिए प्रशंसा मिली।
[RULE] Preposition after praise

---

[Q846] The book was written ___ an unknown author.
