[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) to
(c) with
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'by' indicates the agent of the action in passive sentences.
उदाहरण: यह पुस्तक एक अज्ञात लेखक द्वारा लिखी गई थी।
[RULE] Agentive preposition by

---

[Q847] The project will be completed ___ the end of the month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) in
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'by' is used to indicate a deadline.
उदाहरण: परियोजना महीने के अंत तक पूरी हो जाएगी।
[RULE] Deadline with by

---

[Q848] She is known ___ her generosity.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) to
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'known ___' takes 'for' to express the reason for being known.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी उदारता के लिए जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Known for

---

[Q849] We cannot proceed ___ the lack of resources.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because of
(b) due to
(c) for
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Because of' is the correct prepositional phrase to explain the reason for inability.
उदाहरण: संसाधनों की कमी के कारण हम आगे नहीं बढ़ सकते।
[RULE] Reason with because of / due to

---

[Q850] They discussed the issue ___ the following day.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'on' is used to specify a particular day for an event.
उदाहरण: उन्होंने अगले दिन इस मुद्दे पर चर्चा की।
[RULE] Specific day with on

---

[Q851] The company is looking for a candidate ___ strong communication skills.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2022 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) with
(b) who
(c) that
(d) whose
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'With' is used to show possession of a trait or quality.
उदाहरण: कंपनी एक ऐसे उम्मीदवार की तलाश कर रही है जिसके पास मजबूत संचार कौशल हो।
[RULE] Possession with

---

[Q852] She is proficient ________ French and can translate this document easily.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is fixedly followed by the preposition 'in' to indicate skill or expertise in a subject or language.
उदा. 'Proficient' (कुशल) शब्द के बाद हमेशा 'in' प्रीपोजिशन का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Proficient in)

---

[Q853] Despite working hard, he could not achieve the desired success; ________, he did not lose hope.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) however
(b) therefore
(c) moreover
(d) otherwise
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'However' is used to introduce a statement that contrasts with or seems to contradict something that has been said previously.
उदा. विरोधाभास (contrast) दिखाने के लिए 'however' (फिर भी/हालाँकि) का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conjunctive Adverbs

---

[Q854] The manager asked me ________ I was late for the important meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) why
(b) that
(c) if
(d) what
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In indirect speech or asking for a reason, 'why' is used as a conjunction to introduce a wh- clause representing the cause.
उदा. कारण पूछने के लिए इंटेरोगेटिव शब्द 'why' का प्रयोग कंजंक्शन के रूप में किया जाता है।
[RULE] Indirect Speech / Wh- clauses

---

[Q855] The criminal was found guilty ________ all the charges leveled against him.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) of
(b) for
(c) with
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'guilty' is followed by the fixed preposition 'of' when referring to a crime or charge.
उदा. 'Guilty' (दोषी) के साथ हमेशा फिक्स प्रीपोजिशन 'of' का प्रयोग होता है (guilty of a crime)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Guilty of)

---

[Q856] Had you informed me earlier, I ________ arrangement for your travel.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) would have made
(b) will make
(c) made
(d) would make
[ANS] a
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure expressing an unfulfilled past condition: 'If + past perfect (Had + subject + V3)', main clause takes 'would have + V3'.
उदा. पास्ट की काल्पनिक शर्त (Third Conditional) में 'Had + V3' के साथ मुख्य क्लॉज़ में 'would have + V3' आता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3)

---

[Q857] The committee ________ divided in their opinion regarding the new amendment.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When a collective noun like 'committee' shows division among its members, it takes a plural verb and plural pronoun.
उदा. जब कलेक्टिव नाउन (जैसे committee, jury) में मतभेद (division) दिखाया जाए, तो वह बहुवचन (plural verb) लेता है।
[RULE] Collective Nouns & Verb Agreement

---

[Q858] She has an inherent ________ for classical music and spends hours practicing.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) passion
(b) hostility
(c) apathy
(d) indifference
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: 'Passion' means a strong liking or enthusiasm for something, which fits the context of practicing for hours.
उदा. वाक्य के संदर्भ के अनुसार 'passion' (उत्सव/गहरा प्रेम) सही शब्द है क्योंकि वह घंटों अभ्यास करती है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Contextual Usage

---

[Q859] The government has launched a massive campaign to ________ awareness about road safety.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) spread
(b) spreads
(c) spreding
(d) spreaded
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'to', the base form of the verb (V1) is used as an infinitive. 'Spread' is V1, V2, and V3.
उदा. 'To' के बाद वर्ब की बेस फॉर्म (V1) का प्रयोग होता है। 'Spread' की तीनों फॉर्म एक जैसी होती हैं।
[RULE] Infinitive (to + V1)

---

[Q860] She was accused ________ theft by her employer.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) with
(b) of
(c) for
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'accused' is always followed by the preposition 'of'.
उदा. 'Accused' शब्द के बाद हमेशा प्रपोजिशन 'of' का प्रयोग किया जाता है (Accused of something)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q861] Hardly had I stepped out of the house ________ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) then
(c) when
(d) while
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है (Hardly... when)।
[RULE] Conjunction Pair

---

[Q862] His speech was so ________ that every one in the audience was moved to tears.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) tedious
(b) eloquent
(c) harsh
(d) trivial
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Eloquent' means fluent or persuasive in speaking, which fits the context of moving people to tears.
उदा. 'Eloquent' का अर्थ वक्ता या भाषण का प्रभावशाली होना होता है, जिससे श्रगण प्रभावित हों।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q863] If I ________ the king of this country, I would eradicate poverty.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) have been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In hypothetical conditional sentences expressing a wish or impossible condition, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक वाक्यों (Hypothetical sentences) में सभी सब्जेक्ट्स के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood / Conditional Sentences

---

[Q864] The committee will ________ its final decision on the matter tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) announce
(b) announcing
(c) announced
(d) announces
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Modal auxiliary verbs (will, shall, can, could, etc.) are always followed by the base form of the verb (V1).
उदा. मॉडल वर्ब (will, shall आदि) के बाद हमेशा वर्ब की पहली फॉर्म (V1) आती है।
[RULE] Modal Verbs + V1

---

[Q865] Despite working hard, he failed to ________ the examination.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) clear
(b) clean
(c) cross
(d) crack
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The standard collocation for passing an exam is to 'clear' or 'pass' the examination.
उदा. परीक्षा उत्तीर्ण करने के लिए 'clear' या 'pass' शब्द का उचित संयोजन (collocation) किया जाता है।
[RULE] Collocations

---

[Q866] She has a great ________ for classical music and attends every concert.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) aversion
(b) antipathy
(c) propensity
(d) hostility
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Propensity' or liking/affinity fits well. Wait, propensity means natural inclination. Let's look at options: aversion, antipathy, and hostility are negative. 'Propensity' or affinity is positive. Actually, 'passion' or 'penchant' is common, but 'propensity' fits the nuance best among choices, or let's consider the sentence meaning: she attends every concert, showing inclination.
उदा. 'Propensity' का अर्थ झुकाव या प्रवृत्ति होता है। संगीत के प्रति झुकाव के लिए यह सही शब्द है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Meaning

---

[Q867] Neither the teacher nor the students ________ present in the class.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of Subject-Verb Agreement, when subjects are connected by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it (students -> plural).
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े होने पर वर्ब अपने सबसे नज़दीकी सब्जेक्ट के अनुसार आती है (students बहुवचन है, अतः 'were' आएगा)।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q868] The manager advised his team to refrain ________ spreading rumors.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) off
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'refrain' is always followed by the preposition 'from' (refrain from doing something).
उदा. 'Refrain' शब्द के बाद हमेशा प्रपोजिशन 'from' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition with Verbs

---

[Q869] The company has decided __ a new policy to reduce waste.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to implement
(b) implement
(c) implementing
(d) implements
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the verb 'decided', we use the infinitive form. 
उदाहरण: वह नई नीति को लागू करने का फैसला किया।
[RULE] Infinitive after decided

---

[Q870] She was known __ a sharp wit.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'known' is followed by the preposition 'for' to indicate the quality. 
उदाहरण: वह तेज बुद्धि के लिए जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Preposition after known

---

[Q871] The students were given a chance to present __.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) their ideas
(b) their idea
(c) them ideas
(d) them idea
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The plural noun 'ideas' matches the plural pronoun 'their'. 
उदाहरण: छात्रों को अपनी विचार प्रस्तुत करने का मौका मिला।
[RULE] Pronoun–noun agreement

---

[Q872] He has been working here __ five years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'for' is used with a duration. 
उदाहरण: वह पांच वर्षों से यहाँ काम कर रहा है।
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q873] Despite __ rain, the match went on.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the
(b) a
(c) Vibrant
(d) Lucid
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The definite article 'the' is required before a noun in a prepositional phrase. 
उदाहरण: बारिश के बावजूद मैच आगे बढ़ा।
[RULE] Definite article before noun

---

[Q874] She will come __ 3 pm.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Time expressions use 'at' for a specific hour. 
उदाहरण: वह 3 बजे आएगी।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'at'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Time preposition at

---

[Q875] He was the first person __ the country visited the Sahara.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) which
(c) that
(d) who
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive 'to' follows 'first person' to express an action. 
उदाहरण: वह पहला व्यक्ति था जिसने सहारा का दौरा किया।
[RULE] Infinitive after first person

---

[Q876] They are planning __ a new project next month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) to
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The article 'a' precedes the noun 'project'. 
उदाहरण: वे अगले महीने एक नया प्रोजेक्ट योजना बना रहे हैं।
[RULE] Indefinite article before noun

---

[Q877] The committee will meet __ Wednesday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Days of the week use the preposition 'on'. 
उदाहरण: समिति बुधवार को मिलेगी।
[RULE] Preposition on for days

---

[Q878] The novel was written __ a young author.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Authorship is expressed with the preposition 'by'. 
उदाहरण: उपन्यास एक युवा लेखक द्वारा लिखा गया।
[RULE] Preposition by for authorship

---

[Q879] The doctor advised him __ take a rest.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) a
(c) the
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'advise' is followed by the infinitive 'to take'. 
उदाहरण: डॉक्टर ने उसे विश्राम लेने की सलाह दी।
[RULE] Infinitive after advise

---

[Q880] The government has launched a massive vaccination drive to ______ the spread of the virus.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) curb
(b) promote
(c) accelerate
(d) ignite
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The context requires a verb meaning to restrain or control an undesirable thing. 'Curb' means to restrain or control.
उदा. 'Curb' का अर्थ रोकना या नियंत्रित करना है, जो वायरस के प्रसार को रोकने के संदर्भ में बिल्कुल सही बैठता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q881] She has a great passion ________ classical music and spends hours practicing every day.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) for
(b) in
(c) with
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun 'passion' is conventionally followed by the preposition 'for'.
उदा. संज्ञा 'passion' के बाद उचित प्रीपोजिशन 'for' का प्रयोग होता है (passion for something)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q882] If I ________ the king of this country, I would eradicate poverty within a year.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) am
(d) have been
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In subjunctive mood expressing an imaginary or unreal condition in the present, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. अवास्तविक या काल्पनिक शर्त (Subjunctive mood) को व्यक्त करने के लिए सभी कर्ताओं के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood / Conditional Sentences

---

[Q883] The manager asked the employee to explain the reason ________ he was absent yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) why
(b) because
(c) for
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'the reason', the relative adverb 'why' is used to introduce the clause explaining the cause. (Avoid using 'because' redundantly after 'reason').
उदा. 'the reason' के बाद कारण बताने के लिए सापेक्ष क्रियाविशेषण 'why' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Relative Adverbs

---

[Q884] Despite working hard, he could not achieve success because his strategy was ________.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) flawed
(b) perfect
(c) brilliant
(d) sound
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence indicates a contrast; despite hard work, failure occurred because the strategy had defects. 'Flawed' means having blemishes or defects.
उदा. असफलता का कारण यह था कि रणनीति में दोषपूर्ण (flawed) थी, जिससे कड़ी मेहनत के बावजूद सफलता नहीं मिली।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q885] Neither the teacher nor the students ________ present in the class room.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) is
(d) has been
[ANS] a
[SOL]
According to the rule of subject-verb agreement, when two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject nearest to it. Here, 'students' is plural, so 'were' is correct.
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े होने पर क्रिया अपने सबसे नज़दीकी कर्ता (students) के अनुसार बहुवचन (were) होगी।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q886] The committee reached a consensus ________ the new environmental policy after a long debate.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) on
(b) about
(c) to
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun 'consensus' is followed by the preposition 'on' when referring to the topic of agreement.
उदा. किसी विशेष विषय पर आम सहमति के लिए 'consensus on' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with Nouns

---

[Q887] Had the captain guided the team properly, they ________ the match easily.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) would have won
(b) will win
(c) would win
(d) won
[ANS] a
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure: 'Had + subject + V3' in the if-clause takes 'would have + V3' in the main clause.
उदा. यह तृतीय शर्त (Third Conditional) का वाक्य है, जिसमें 'Had + V3' के साथ मुख्य खंड में 'would have + V3' आता है।
[RULE] Conditionals (Type 3)

---

[Q888] Despite his best efforts to conceal his emotions, a tear escaped and trickled ________ his cheek.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) across
(b) down
(c) over
(d) along
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'down' to indicate movement from a higher to a lower point on a surface, such as tears on a cheek.
उदा. गालों पर आंसुओं के नीचे की ओर बहने के लिए 'down' प्रीपोजिशन का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions of Movement

---

[Q889] The manager asked the employee to ________ the proposal and report back by tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) look into
(b) look after
(c) look through
(d) look up
[ANS] c
[SOL]
To 'look through' means to read something quickly or examine it thoroughly. Here, examining a proposal requires this phrasal verb.
उदा. किसी प्रस्ताव या दस्तावेज को सरसरी तौर पर पढ़ने या जांचने के लिए 'look through' फ्रेजल वर्ब का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q890] The committee members could not reach a consensus because each of them ________ different views.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) have
(b) has
(c) were having
(d) are having
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Pronouns like 'each', 'everyone', 'everybody' take a singular verb. Hence, 'has' is the correct choice.
उदा. 'Each' के साथ हमेशा सिंगुलर वर्ब का प्रयोग होता है, इसलिए यहाँ 'has' सही उत्तर है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q891] Had the driver been more vigilant, the fatal accident ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) could avoid
(b) would have been avoided
(c) could have avoided
(d) was avoided
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In third conditional sentences expressing past hypothetical situations, the structure is 'Had + subject + V3, subject + would/could/might + have + V3 (passive)'.
उदा. पास्ट की असंभव शर्त वाले वाक्यों (Third Conditional) में 'would have been + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q892] His speech was so ________ that the audience was spellbound for over an hour.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) tedious
(b) eloquent
(c) monotonous
(d) prosaic
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Eloquent' means fluent or persuasive in speaking, which explains why the audience was spellbound.
उदा. 'Eloquent' का अर्थ होता है प्रभावशाली या वक्ता कला में निपुण, जिससे श्रोता मंत्रमुग्ध हो गए।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q893] Scarcely had she stepped outside the house ________ a heavy downpour began.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'scarcely' or 'hardly' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before'), whereas 'no sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Scarcely' या 'Hardly' के बाद हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q894] The newly inaugurated bridge is ________ to heavy commercial vehicles during peak hours.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) accessible
(b) prohibited
(c) restricted
(d) liable
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Vehicles are 'restricted' (limited or kept out) to or from certain areas. 'Restricted to' is the standard collocation.
उदा. भारी वाहनों के लिए पुल पर प्रवेश सीमित करने हेतु 'restricted to' का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions / Collocations

---

[Q895] The government has announced a new scheme to ________ the economic growth in rural sectors.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) hamper
(b) curb
(c) stimulate
(d) impede
[ANS] c
[SOL]
To 'stimulate' means to encourage or spur growth. The context requires a positive action for economic growth.
उदा. ग्रामीण क्षेत्रों में आर्थिक विकास को बढ़ावा देने (stimulate) के लिए नई योजना शुरू की गई है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Synonyms in Context

---

[Q896] No sooner did the principal enter the class room ________ all the students stood up in respect.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) when
(b) then
(c) than
(d) as
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'No sooner' is strictly paired with the conjunction 'than'.
उदा. 'No sooner' के साथ हमेशा 'than' का जोड़ा प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conjunction Pairs

---

[Q897] The scientist's groundbreaking theory was met with ________ skepticism by his peers before it was verified experimentally.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) widespread
(b) scanty
(c) trivial
(d) meager
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Widespread skepticism' means general or broad doubt among many people before proof was provided.
उदा. प्रयोग द्वारा सिद्ध होने से पहले वैज्ञानिक के सिद्धांत को लेकर लोगों में व्यापक संदेह ('widespread skepticism') था।
[RULE] Adjective-Noun Collocations

---

[Q898] The committee has decided ___ a new policy for staff welfare.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to launch
(b) launching
(c) launch
(d) launches
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the infinitive form after the verb 'decided' when expressing a future plan.
उदाहरण: उन्होंने अगले वर्ष की शुरुआत में नई नीति लागू करने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Infinitive after decided

---

[Q899] She has been ___ the project since March.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) worked
(b) work
(c) working
(d) worked on
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule in English: After 'has been', use present participle (verb+ing) to indicate an ongoing action.
उदाहरण: वह मार्च से परियोजना पर काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Present participle after has been

---

[Q900] I am looking forward ___ meeting you at the conference.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: After the phrase 'looking forward', use the infinitive marker 'to'.
उदाहरण: मैं सम्मेलन में आपसे मिलने के लिए उत्सुक हूँ।
[RULE] Infinitive after looking forward

---

[Q901] The manager's decision was made ___ the input of all department heads.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The preposition 'by' is used to indicate the agent who performed an action.
उदाहरण: निर्णय सभी विभाग प्रमुखों के इनपुट द्वारा लिया गया।
[RULE] Preposition by for agent

---

[Q902] He has a habit of ___ late in the evenings.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) being
(b) be
(c) been
(d) become
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: After 'habit of', use the gerund form (verb+ing).
उदाहरण: उसके पास शाम को देर से रहने की आदत है।
[RULE] Gerund after habit of

---

[Q903] The report was submitted ___ the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) before
(b) after
(c) during
(d) by
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Rule in English: 'By' is used to indicate that an action is completed on or before a specific time.
उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट समय सीमा से पहले जमा की गई।
[RULE] Preposition by for deadline

---

[Q904] The students were asked ___ the questions before the examination.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) answer
(b) to answer
(c) answering
(d) answers
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: After the verb 'asked', use the infinitive 'to answer' to express the purpose of the request.
उदाहरण: छात्रों से परीक्षा से पहले प्रश्नों का उत्तर देने के लिए कहा गया।
[RULE] Infinitive after asked

---

[Q905] The new software will ___ significantly improve data processing speed.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) help
(b) helps
(c) helped
(d) helping
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: After the modal verb 'will', use the base form of the verb.
उदाहरण: नया सॉफ्टवेयर डेटा प्रोसेसिंग गति को काफी सुधार करेगा।
[RULE] Base form after modal will

---

[Q906] If you ___ the instructions, you will get the best results.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) following
(c) follows
(d) followed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: In an 'if' clause with a simple present tense, use the base form of the verb.
उदाहरण: यदि आप निर्देशों का पालन करते हैं, तो आपको सर्वोत्तम परिणाम मिलेंगे।
[RULE] Base form in if clause

---

[Q907] The singer's voice was so ___ that every one stopped to listen.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) remarkable
(b) remarkably
(c) remark
(d) remarkablely
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use an adjective before 'so' to modify a noun; the adverb form is not used here.
उदाहरण: गायक की आवाज़ इतनी शानदार थी कि सभी सुनने के लिए रुक गए।
[RULE] Adjective before so

---

[Q908] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure a promotion because he was found wanting _____ dedication.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) in
(b) with
(c) for
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'wanting in' means lacking or deficient in a particular quality. Here, it means lacking dedication.
उदा. 'Wanting in' एक निश्चित गुण की कमी को दर्शाता है। यहाँ समर्पण (dedication) की कमी का अर्थ है, इसलिए 'in' सही preposition है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (wanting in)

---

[Q909] The manager asked the employees to refrain _____ using mobile phones during the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) off
(c) against
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'refrain' is always followed by the preposition 'from' to indicate stopping oneself from doing something.
उदा. Verb 'refrain' के बाद हमेशा preposition 'from' का प्रयोग होता है, जिसका अर्थ किसी कार्य को करने से बचना है।
[RULE] Preposition with Verbs (refrain from)

---

[Q910] Had the police arrived on time, the tragedy _____ prevented.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) could be
(b) can have been
(c) could have been
(d) would be
[ANS] c
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence in the past. The structure for past unreal condition is 'If + subject + had + V3, subject + would/could/might + have + V3'.
उदा. यह Third Conditional वाक्य है। भूतकाल की असंभावित शर्त के लिए 'Had + V3' के साथ मुख्य खंड में 'could have been + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3)

---

[Q911] Her views on the new economic policy do not _____ with mine.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) coincide
(b) collude
(c) conform
(d) combine
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Coincide with' means to correspond or agree exactly with something. Her views match or agree with mine.
उदा. 'Coincide with' का अर्थ विचारों या मत का मेल खाना होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary and Collocation

---

[Q912] The government has implemented stringent measures to _____ corruption in public offices.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) curb
(b) boost
(c) foster
(d) indulge
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Curb' means to restrain or control something undesirable, like corruption.
उदा. 'Curb' का अर्थ किसी बुरी चीज़ (जैसे भ्रष्टाचार) पर अंकुश लगाना या नियंत्रित करना होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q913] Scarcely had he stepped out of the house _____ it began to rain heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) that
(d) then
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Scarcely' or 'Hardly' is always followed by 'when' or 'before', whereas 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Scarcely' या 'Hardly' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q914] Neither the teacher nor the students _____ present in the class room.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of Subject-Verb Agreement, when two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it. Here 'students' is plural, so plural verb 'were' is used.
उदा. जब दो Subject 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो verb अपने पास वाले Subject के अनुसार आती है। 'students' plural है, इसलिए 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q915] The scientist spent years trying to _____ the complex mystery of the universe.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) unravel
(b) envelop
(c) concede
(d) aggravate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Unravel' means to investigate and solve or explain something complicated or mysterious like a mystery.
उदा. 'Unravel' का अर्थ किसी रहस्य या जटिल समस्या को सुलझाना या स्पष्ट करना होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q916] Only by working persistently day and night _____ achieve success in this competitive exam.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) you can
(b) can you
(c) you could
(d) could you
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a sentence begins with a negative or limiting adverbial expression like 'Only by', inversion is required (Auxiliary verb comes before the subject).
उदा. जब वाक्य की शुरुआत 'Only by' जैसे वाक्यांश से होती है, तो inversion नियम के अनुसार Helping Verb (can) Subject (you) से पहले आती है।
[RULE] Inversion in Grammar

---

[Q917] The pioneer of artificial intelligence warned that unchecked technological growth could pose a threat to human _____.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) existence
(b) resistance
(c) persistence
(d) subsistence
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Human existence' is a standard collocation referring to the survival or state of living for human beings.
उदा. 'Human existence' का अर्थ मानव अस्तित्व या जीवन से है, जो AI के खतरे के संदर्भ में बिल्कुल सटीक बैठता है।
[RULE] Collocations & Vocabulary

---

[Q918] She has been working on this research project _______ three months.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'for' to denote a period of time (three months). Use 'since' for a point of time.
उदा. निश्चित समय (point of time) के लिए 'since' और अनिश्चित अवधि (period of time) के लिए 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition of Time (For/Since)

---

[Q919] Hardly had he stepped out of the house _______ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) before
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction for 'Hardly' or 'Scarcely' is always 'when'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q920] Despite working hard, he could not succeed because he lacked _______ guidance.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) sensible
(b) sensitive
(c) proper
(d) literate
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In the context of lacking direction or advice, 'proper' guidance is the most appropriate collocation.
उदा. सही दिशा या सलाह न मिलने के संदर्भ में 'proper guidance' (उचित मार्गदर्शन) सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Collocation

---

[Q921] Neither the manager nor his subordinates _______ present at the meeting yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject nearest to it. Here, 'subordinates' is plural, so 'were' is used.
उदा. जब दो कर्ता 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो क्रिया (verb) अपने सबसे पास वाले कर्ता के अनुसार आती है। यहाँ 'subordinates' बहुवचन है, अतः 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q922] The police are looking _______ the matter to find the culprit.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) into
(b) at
(c) after
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'look into' means to investigate a matter.
उदा. फ्रेजल वर्ब 'look into' का अर्थ किसी मामले की जांच करना (investigate) होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q923] Had I known about the traffic jam, I _______ another route.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) will take
(b) would take
(c) would have taken
(d) had taken
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In third conditional sentences expressing past hypothetical situations, the structure is 'Had + subject + V3, subject + would have + V3'.
उदा. भूतकाल की अवास्तविक स्थिति (Third Conditional) में 'Had + V3' के साथ मुख्य खंड में 'would have + V3' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q924] The rapid advancement of technology has made our lives much more _______.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) convenient
(b) convenience
(c) conveniently
(d) convene
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An adjective is required after the verb 'made' and its object 'our lives' to act as an object complement. 'Convenient' is an adjective.
उदा. वर्ब 'made' और ऑब्जेक्ट 'our lives' के बाद विशेषता बताने के लिए एडजेक्टिव (Adjective) की आवश्यकता होती है।
[RULE] Parts of Speech (Adjective Form)

---

[Q925] A grand reception will be _______ in honor of the winning cricket team.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) held
(b) organized
(c) kept
(d) made
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use the verb 'hold' (held in past/passive) to indicate hosting an event, meeting, or reception.
उदा. किसी समारोह, सभा या रिसेप्शन के आयोजन के लिए 'hold' वर्ब का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Collocations

---

[Q926] Only one of the students _______ selected for the national scholarship program.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) are
(c) was
(d) have been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The structure 'One of + plural noun' takes a singular verb because the subject is 'One'.
उदा. 'One of + plural noun' के बाद हमेशा एकवचन (singular) वर्ब का प्रयोग होता है क्योंकि मुख्य कर्ता 'One' होता है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q927] His speech was so _______ that the audience remained spellbound through out.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) tedious
(b) captivating
(c) offensive
(d) monotonous
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Since the audience remained 'spellbound' (मंत्रमुग्ध), the speech must have been captivating (fascinating/attractive).
उदा. चूँकि श्रोता 'spellbound' (मंत्रमुग्ध) थे, इसलिए भाषण निश्चित रूप से 'captivating' (आकर्षक) रहा होगा।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Contextual Usage

---

[Q928] I have been looking forward _____ see you next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: The phrase is "look forward to" + noun/verb-ing. Example: *I have been looking forward to seeing you.*
उदाहरण: *मैं अगले हफ़्ते तुम्हें देखने के लिए उत्सुक हूँ.*
[RULE] "look forward to"

---

[Q929] He was known _____ his punctuality.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The adjective *known* is followed by *for* when stating a reputation. Example: *He was known for his punctuality.*
उदाहरण: *उसे अपनी समयबद्धता के लिए जाना जाता है.*
[RULE] Known + for

---

[Q930] The company is planning _____ expand its operations into Asia.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) of
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The verb *plan* is followed by *to* + base form of verb. Example: *They plan to expand.*
उदाहरण: *कंपनी अपनी संचालन को एशिया में विस्तारित करने की योजना बना रही है.*
[RULE] Plan + to + verb

---

[Q931] The meeting was ___ due to the unforeseen circumstances.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) cancelled
(b) cancel
(c) canceling
(d) cancelling
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past participle *cancelled* is used after a past tense verb. Example: *The meeting was cancelled.*
उदाहरण: *अनपेक्षित परिस्थितियों के कारण बैठक रद्द कर दी गई.*
[RULE] Past participle after *was*

---

[Q932] The house was built ___ 1920.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The preposition *in* is used with years. Example: *built in 1920.*
उदाहरण: *घर 1920 में बनाया गया था.*
[RULE] Preposition + year

---

[Q933] She was praised _____ her bravery during the rescue.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The verb *praise* is followed by *for* when indicating a reason. Example: *praised for her bravery.*
उदाहरण: *उसे बचाव के दौरान उसकी बहादुरी के लिए प्रशंसा की गई.*
[RULE] Praise + for

---

[Q934] By the time the train arrived, the passengers ____.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had boarded
(b) have boarded
(c) were boarding
(d) boarded
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: Past perfect simple (had + past participle) describes an action completed before another past event. Example: *by the time the train arrived, they had boarded.*
उदाहरण: *ट्रेन आने तक, यात्री पहले ही चढ़ चुके थे.*
[RULE] Past perfect tense

---

[Q935] The project was delayed _____ lack of resources.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) due to
(b) because of
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: *due to* is followed by a noun phrase or gerund to express reason. Example: *delayed due to lack of resources.*
उदाहरण: *संसाधनों की कमी के कारण परियोजना विलंबित हुई.*
[RULE] "due to" + noun/gerund

---

[Q936] She is ___ for her artistic talent.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) known
(b) knowned
(c) know
(d) knowing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The adjective *known* is used to indicate reputation. Example: *She is known for her talent.*
उदाहरण: *वह अपनी कलात्मक प्रतिभा के लिए जानी जाती है.*
[RULE] Known + for

---

[Q937] We will start the project _____ the first quarter of 2024.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule: The preposition *in* is used with a quarter or month. Example: *in the first quarter.*
उदाहरण: *हम परियोजना को प्रथम तिमाही 2024 में शुरू करेंगे.*
[RULE] Preposition + quarter/month

---

[Q938] The committee will discuss the proposal _____ the next meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Rule: The preposition *in* is used with meetings or events in time. Example: *in the next meeting.*
उदाहरण: *समिति अगली बैठक में प्रस्ताव पर चर्चा करेगी.*
[RULE] Preposition + meeting

---

[Q939] She was accused ________ stealing her colleague's wristwatch.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) with
(d) at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The fixed preposition used with the adjective/participle 'accused' is 'of'.
उदा. 'Accused' शब्द के साथ हमेशा निश्चित प्रीपोजिशन 'of' का प्रयोग किया जाता है (Accused of = आरोप लगाना)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q940] Despite working hard, he could not ________ the examination.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) clear
(b) pass through
(c) beat
(d) qualify with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We say 'clear an exam' or 'pass an exam'. 'Pass through' means to go past something, and 'beat' does not fit here.
उदा. परीक्षा पास करने के लिए 'clear an exam' या 'pass an exam' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q941] His speech was so ________ that the audience was spellbound.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) boring
(b) captivating
(c) tedious
(d) monotonous
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The context implies a positive reaction ('spellbound' / मंत्रमुग्ध), so the speech must be attractive or fascinating ('captivating').
उदा. वाक्य का संदर्भ बताता है कि दर्शक मंत्रमुग्ध थे, इसलिए भाषण 'captivating' (आकर्षक) होना चाहिए।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q942] Neither the manager nor his subordinates ________ present at the meeting yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of Subject-Verb Agreement, when subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the closer subject ('subordinates' is plural).
उदा. जब दो विषय 'neither... nor' से जुड़ते हैं, तो वर्ब अपने सबसे करीब वाले सब्जेक्ट के अनुसार आती है ('subordinates' बहुवचन है, अतः 'were' आएगा)।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q943] The manager advised his team to refrain ________ making hasty decisions.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) against
(c) off
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'refrain' is always followed by the preposition 'from' + gerund.
उदा. 'Refrain' (दूर रहना) के साथ हमेशा प्रीपोजिशन 'from' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q944] If I ________ the king of this country, I would eradicate poverty completely.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) am
(b) were
(c) was
(d) have been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
For hypothetical or unreal conditional sentences in the present, 'were' is used with all subjects in the 'if' clause.
उदा. काल्पनिक (Hypothetical) वाक्यों में 'if' क्लॉज़ के साथ सभी सब्जेक्ट्स के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 2)

---

[Q945] She has a great ________ for classical music and often attends concerts.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) passion
(b) taste
(c) habit
(d) routine
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The collocation 'taste for' means a liking or preference for something, especially art or music.
उदा. 'Taste for' एक सही कॉलोकेशन है जिसका अर्थ किसी चीज़ (जैसे संगीत या कला) के प्रति रुचि होना है।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q946] The old man could not walk fast ________ his old age and frail health.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because
(b) due to
(c) although
(d) despite of
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Due to' is used to state a reason before a noun phrase. ('Despite of' is grammatically incorrect; it should be 'Despite' or 'In spite of').
उदा. कारण बताने के लिए नाउन फ्रेज़ से पहले 'due to' का प्रयोग किया जाता है। ('Despite of' गलत होता है)।
[RULE] Prepositions of Cause

---

[Q947] The teacher asked the students to ________ their notebooks and listen carefully.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) put off
(b) put away
(c) put down
(d) put on
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Put down' means to write something down or make a note of it. 
उदा. 'Put down' एक phrasal verb है जिसका अर्थ लिखना या नोट करना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs (Put down)

---

[Q948] Prevention is ________ than cure.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) good
(b) better
(c) best
(d) well
[ANS] b
[SOL]
This is a standard proverb requiring the comparative degree ('better') followed by 'than'.
उदा. यह एक प्रसिद्ध लोकोक्ति (proverb) है: 'Prevention is better than cure' (इलाज से परहेज बेहतर है)।
[RULE] Degrees of Comparison (Proverb)

---

[Q949] Despite working hard, he could not succeed ________ his goal.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) in
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'succeed' is followed by the preposition 'in' (succeed in doing something).
उदा. 'Succeed' के साथ फिक्स्ड प्रिपोजिशन 'in' का प्रयोग होता है (जैसे- succeed in the exam)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q950] If I ________ the king of the country, I would eradicate poverty.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In conditional sentences expressing an imaginary or unreal past/present wish, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक इच्छा (Hypothetical condition) को व्यक्त करने के लिए सभी सब्जेक्ट्स के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, भले ही सब्जेक्ट सिंगुलर क्यों न हो।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Subjunctive Mood)

---

[Q951] The government has taken several measures to ________ the economy.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) boost
(b) boast
(c) burst
(d) beast
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Boost' means to help or encourage something to increase or improve.
उदा. 'Boost' का अर्थ किसी चीज़ को बढ़ावा देना या सुधारना होता है। 'Boast' का अर्थ डींग हाँकना होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Confusing Words

---

[Q952] The stray dog barked ________ the stranger aggressively.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) upon
(d) against
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Bark at' is the correct phrasal collocation when directing anger or noise towards someone.
उदा. किसी पर भौंकने के लिए 'bark at' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with Verbs

---

[Q953] A grand celebration is being ________ in honor of the retiring principal.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) held
(b) hold
(c) holding
(d) holded
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence is in the present continuous passive voice (is/am/are + being + V3). The V3 form of 'hold' is 'held'.
उदा. वाक्य प्रेजेंट कंटीन्यूअस पैसिव वॉइस में है (is/am/are + being + V3)। 'hold' की थर्ड फॉर्म 'held' होती है।
[RULE] Passive Voice Verb Forms

---

[Q954] The manager's decision to cut costs was considered _____ by the employees.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) inappropriate
(b) inappropriately
(c) inappropriateness
(d) inappropriatenessly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct word is an adjective: inappropriate. It modifies the decision. In Hindi: "कर्मचारियों द्वारा यह निर्णय अनुचित माना गया।"
[RULE] Adjective usage after noun.

---

[Q955] She has been working here _____ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'since' to refer to a point in time. Hindi: "वह 2010 से यहाँ काम कर रही है।"
[RULE] Time expression with since.

---

[Q956] The team will review the proposal "____" the next meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Preposition 'on' indicates the time of the meeting. Hindi: "टीम अगली बैठक पर प्रस्ताव की समीक्षा करेगी।"
[RULE] Preposition for scheduled time.

---

[Q957] The scientist was praised for his _____ in the field of genetics.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) contribution
(b) contribute
(c) contributing
(d) contributes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun form 'contribution' fits after 'in'. Hindi: "विज्ञानी को आनुवंशिकी के क्षेत्र में उनके योगदान के लिए प्रशंसा मिली।"
[RULE] Noun usage after preposition.

---

[Q958] He couldn't attend the seminar, _____ he was ill.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because
(b) since
(c) though
(d) and
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'because' to give reason. Hindi: "वह सेमिनार में उपस्थित नहीं हो सका क्योंकि वह बीमार था।"
[RULE] Conjunction for reason.

---

[Q959] The new policy will be implemented "____" the end of the month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) by
(c) in
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'By' indicates the deadline. Hindi: "नई नीति महीने के अंत तक लागू की जाएगी।"
[RULE] Preposition for deadlines.

---

[Q960] She has been learning French "____" five years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'For' is used for duration. Hindi: "वह पाँच वर्षों से फ्रेंच सीख रही है।"
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'for'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Duration with for.

---

[Q961] The report was submitted "____" the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) before
(b) after
(c) during
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Before' indicates earlier than the deadline. Hindi: "रिपोर्ट समय सीमा से पहले प्रस्तुत की गई।"
[RULE] Preposition for time comparison.

---

[Q962] He was applauded for his courage, _____ he risked every thing.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which
(b) who
(c) that
(d) where
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'That' introduces a restrictive clause about the risk. Hindi: "उसने सब कुछ जोखिम में डालते हुए बहादुरी के लिए प्रशंसा पाई।"
[RULE] Relative pronoun for restrictive clause.

---

[Q963] The committee will review the application "____" the next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'In' is used for a general period. Hindi: "समिति अगले सप्ताह आवेदन की समीक्षा करेगी।"
[RULE] Preposition for a period of time.

---

[Q964] The company has been expanding its operations "____" the market.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'In' indicates within a market. Hindi: "कंपनी बाजार में अपनी गतिविधियाँ बढ़ा रही है।"
[RULE] Preposition for inclusion.

---

[Q965] The government has taken steps ___ ensuring the safety of the citizens.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) of
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive form of the verb follows the noun 'steps'.
उदाहरण: The steps to ensure safety were effective.
[RULE] Infinitive after a noun

---

[Q966] She was accused ___ cheating in the exam.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) for
(c) with
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'accuse' is followed by the preposition 'of' + gerund.
उदाहरण: He was accused of stealing the money.
[RULE] Accusation + of + gerund

---

[Q967] The committee met ___ discuss the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) with
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the verb 'meet', an infinitive expresses purpose.
उदाहरण: They met to decide the budget.
[RULE] Infinitive after 'meet'

---

[Q968] He is responsible ___ maintaining the records.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) with
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'responsible' is always followed by 'for' + noun.
उदाहरण: She is responsible for the team's success.
[RULE] Preposition after 'responsible'

---

[Q969] They were praised ___ their dedication.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'praise' is followed by 'for' + noun.
उदाहरण: He was praised for his hard work.
[RULE] Preposition after 'praise'

---

[Q970] I am looking forward ___ meeting you next week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) at
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'looking forward to' takes a gerund.
उदाहरण: I am looking forward to seeing you.
[RULE] Preposition 'to' after 'looking forward'

---

[Q971] The students were eager ___ starting the project.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) with
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'eager' is followed by the infinitive form of the verb.
उदाहरण: She was eager to learn.
[RULE] Adjective 'eager' + infinitive

---

[Q972] The book is ___ of the best novels of the year.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) among
(b) with
(c) for
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'among' is used when comparing within a group.
उदाहरण: She is among the top performers.
[RULE] Preposition 'among' for comparison

---

[Q973] She couldn't attend the meeting ___ a sudden illness.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) because of
(c) due to
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase 'because of' correctly introduces a reason after 'couldn’t attend'.
उदाहरण: He missed the game because of an injury.
[RULE] Reason phrase after inability verb

---

[Q974] He has been working ___ this project since March.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) in
(c) on
(d) at
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The preposition 'on' follows 'working' when referring to a project.
उदाहरण: She is working on her thesis.
[RULE] Preposition after 'work' when referring to a task

---

[Q975] The manager was noted ___ punctuality and dedication.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'note' is followed by 'for' + noun phrase to highlight a quality.
उदाहरण: He was noted for his leadership.
[RULE] Verb 'note' + for + noun phrase

---

[Q976] She couldn't _____ the truth in his eyes.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) see
(b) saw
(c) seeing
(d) sees
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In negative sentences with modal verbs like could, the base form of the verb follows. 
उदा. "I could not go" (मैं नहीं जा सकता).
[RULE] Modal verbs + base form

---

[Q977] He was accused _____ leaking the confidential report.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) for
(c) by
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'accused of' is the standard collocation. 
उदा. "She was accused of theft."
[RULE] Preposition after 'accused'

---

[Q978] The _____ of the story was so compelling that every one stayed until the end.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) plot
(b) plotting
(c) plotted
(d) plots
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Plot is a noun meaning the sequence of events in a story. 
उदा. "The plot is interesting."
[RULE] Noun usage

---

[Q979] They had been working on the project _____ when the manager arrived.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) when
(d) while
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Since indicates the starting point of an action that continues to the present. 
उदा. "I have lived here since 2010."
[RULE] Since vs. for

---

[Q980] The meeting was postponed _____ the unexpected cancellation of the keynote speaker.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) due to
(b) because of
(c) for
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'due to' is used to indicate the reason for postponement. 
उदा. "The flight was delayed due to weather."
[RULE] Preposition 'due to'

---

[Q981] She was filled with _____ after hearing the news.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) elation
(b) elationed
(c) elated
(d) elationing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Elation is a noun meaning extreme happiness. The adjective would be 'elated'. 
उदा. "He felt elated."
[RULE] Noun vs. adjective

---

[Q982] If he _____ earlier, he would have caught the bus.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) has left
(c) left
(d) will leave
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Third conditional uses past perfect in the if‑clause. 
उदा. "If she had studied, she would have passed."
[RULE] Third conditional

---

[Q983] The teacher praised the student _____ his hard work.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) with
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The expression 'praise for' is used to indicate the reason for praise. 
उदा. "They praised him for his courage."
[RULE] Preposition after 'praise'

---

[Q984] The scientist's discovery was hailed as a _____ breakthrough.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) major
(b) majorly
(c) majority
(d) majorous
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Major is an adjective meaning important or significant. 
उदा. "It was a major achievement."
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q985] By the time the concert ended, the audience had already _____.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) left
(b) lefting
(c) leaves
(d) leaving
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle 'left' is used after 'had already' in past perfect. 
उदा. "She had already left."
[RULE] Past perfect with 'had already'

---

[Q986] The committee will announce the results _____ next week.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition 'in' is used for a general time period. 
उदा. "We will finish in two weeks."
[RULE] Preposition of time

---

[Q987] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure the promotion as his rivals were _____ him.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ahead of
(b) ahead off
(c) along with
(d) away from
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition phrase 'ahead of' means in front of or superior to someone in a race, competition, or position. 'Ahead off' is incorrect grammar.
उदा. 'Ahead of' का अर्थ आगे या किसी से बेहतर होना है। प्रतियोगिता में प्रतिद्वंदियों से आगे होने के लिए 'ahead of' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions / Phrasal Expressions

---

[Q988] The manager asked the employees to adhere _____ the company rules strictly.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) with
(b) to
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'adhere' is always followed by the fixed preposition 'to' when meaning to obey or abide by a rule, policy, or agreement.
उदा. 'Adhere' के साथ हमेशा निश्चित प्रीपोज़िशन 'to' का प्रयोग होता है जिसका अर्थ नियमों या नीतियों का पालन करना है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q989] The government has launched a new scheme to _____ the living standards of rural citizens.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) elevate
(b) ameliorate
(c) aggravate
(d) accelerate
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Ameliorate' means to make something bad or unsatisfactory better. It fits best with 'living standards'. 'Aggravate' means to worsen.
उदा. 'Ameliorate' का अर्थ स्थिति या जीवन स्तर को सुधारना है। 'Aggravate' का अर्थ बिगाड़ना होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q990] Scarcely had she stepped outside _____ it started pouring heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) than
(b) then
(c) when
(d) that
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Scarcely' or 'Hardly' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before'), whereas 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Scarcely' या 'Hardly' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions and Correlatives

---

[Q991] She is proficient _____ mathematics and can solve complex problems within seconds.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' (skilled/competent) is appropriately followed by the preposition 'in' when referring to a subject or skill.
उदा. विशेषण 'proficient' (निपुण) के बाद किसी विषय या कौशल में दक्षता दिखाने के लिए प्रीपोज़िशन 'in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition with Adjectives

---

[Q992] The committee members could not reach a consensus because they were divided in _____ opinions.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) its
(b) their
(c) his
(d) ones
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Since the members are divided in their opinions (acting as individuals rather than a single unit), the plural possessive pronoun 'their' is required.
उदा. चूँकि समिति के सदस्य अपनी राय में विभाजित हैं (अलग-अलग व्यक्तियों के रूप में कार्य कर रहे हैं), इसलिए बहुवचन सर्वनाम 'their' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Pronoun Agreement (Nouns of Multitude)

---

[Q993] Regular exercise and a balanced diet are _____ to maintaining good health.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) indifferent
(b) imperative
(c) impertinent
(d) impaired
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Imperative' means of vital importance; crucial. It fits the context that exercise and diet are crucial for health.
उदा. 'Imperative' का अर्थ अत्यंत महत्वपूर्ण या अनिवार्य है। स्वास्थ्य के लिए व्यायाम और आहार जरूरी हैं, अतः यह सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Fit

---

[Q994] Not only the principal but also the teachers _____ present at the meeting yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'not only... but also', the verb agrees with the subject closer to it ('teachers', which is plural and in past tense).
उदा. जब दो कर्ता 'not only... but also' से जुड़ते हैं, तो क्रिया अपने सबसे पास वाले कर्ता ('teachers', जो कि बहुवचन है) के अनुसार आती है। काल भूतकाल (yesterday) है, अतः 'were' सही है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q995] Despite the heavy downpour, the players carried _____ with their practice.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) out
(b) off
(c) on
(d) over
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'carry on' means to continue an activity or task despite obstacles.
उदा. फ्रेज़ल वर्ब 'carry on' का अर्थ बाधाओं के बावजूद किसी कार्य को जारी रखना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q996] The government has __________ a new scheme for the welfare of farmers.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) launched
(b) started
(c) opened
(d) originated
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use 'launched' when talking about officially introducing a new scheme, project, or product. 
उदा. सरकारी योजना (scheme) या प्रोजेक्ट की शुरुआत के लिए 'launched' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Collocation / Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q997] Hardly had I stepped out of the house __________ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) while
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction for 'Hardly' or 'Scarcely' is 'When'. 
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Conjunction Pairing

---

[Q998] She has been suffering from fever __________ last Monday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) for
(b) from
(c) since
(d) by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Since' is used for a point of time in the past (like last Monday) in present/past perfect continuous tense. 
उदा. निश्चित समय (point of time) जैसे 'last Monday' के लिए 'since' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि अवधि (period of time) के लिए 'for' आता है।
[RULE] Preposition of Time

---

[Q999] Neither the teacher nor the students __________ present in the class yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the nearest subject ('students', which is plural). 
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े वाक्यों में वर्ब हमेशा पास वाले सब्जेक्ट के अनुसार आती है, यहाँ 'students' बहुवचन है इसलिए 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1000] Despite working hard, he could not __________ good marks in the examination.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) secure
(b) fetch
(c) catch
(d) bring
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In standard English collocation, students 'secure' or 'obtain' marks or ranks in an examination. 
उदा. परीक्षा में अच्छे अंक प्राप्त करने के लिए 'secure marks' या 'obtain marks' का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q1001] A committee was __________ to investigate the matter thoroughly.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) constituted
(b) formed
(c) invented
(d) discovered
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Constituted' or 'appointed' is the right formal term for setting up a committee or official body. 
उदा. किसी समिति (committee) या आयोग के गठन के लिए औपचारिक रूप से 'constituted' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q1002] He is proficient __________ mathematics and physics.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is always followed by the preposition 'in' to indicate skill in a subject. 
उदा. 'Proficient' (निपुण) शब्द के बाद हमेशा फिक्स प्रीपोजिशन 'in' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1003] Had you informed me earlier, I __________ attend the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) would have attended
(b) will attend
(c) would attend
(d) had attended
[ANS] a
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure: 'Had + subject + V3' takes 'would have + V3' in the main clause. 
उदा. यह कंडीशनल सेंटेंस का नियम है जहाँ 'Had + V3' होने पर मुख्य खंड (main clause) में 'would have + V3' आता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q1004] The manager asked me what my __________ qualifications were.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) educational
(b) education
(c) educate
(d) educating
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We need an adjective ('educational') to modify the noun 'qualifications'. 
उदा. संज्ञा (qualifications) की विशेषता बताने के लिए एडजेक्टिव 'educational' का प्रयोग किया जाएगा।
[RULE] Parts of Speech

---

[Q1005] Preventive measures must be taken to __________ the spread of the contagious disease.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) curb
(b) provoke
(c) encourage
(d) accelerate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Curb' means to restrain or keep in check, which fits the context of stopping a disease's spread. 
उदा. बीमारी के फैलने को रोकने या नियंत्रित करने के लिए 'curb' (अंकुश लगाना) सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1006] She has been working ___ the company for five years.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) for
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Use of preposition 'for' indicates duration of time.
उदाहरण: वह पाँच वर्षों से कंपनी में काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Preposition of time

---

[Q1007] If he ___ earlier, he would have avoided the mistake.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) left
(c) will leave
(d) has left
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Third conditional requires past perfect in the if‑clause.
उदाहरण: अगर वह पहले चला गया होता, तो उसने गलती से बचा लिया होता।
[RULE] Third conditional (past perfect)

---

[Q1008] The committee's decision was ___________.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ambiguous
(b) unequivocal
(c) tentative
(d) vague
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'unequivocal' means absolutely certain.
उदाहरण: समिति का निर्णय स्पष्ट और संदेह रहित था।
[RULE] Adjective meaning absolute certainty

---

[Q1009] Please submit the report ___ 10th August.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) by
(c) before
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition 'by' specifies a deadline.
उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट 10 अगस्त से पहले जमा करें।
[RULE] Preposition of deadline

---

[Q1010] She is ___ to the meeting when I called her.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrive
(b) has arrived
(c) arriving
(d) arrived
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Present continuous is used for scheduled future events.
उदाहरण: वह चल रही है बैठक में जब मैंने उसे फोन किया।
[RULE] Present continuous for future arrangements

---

[Q1011] The new policy has been ____________ to most employees.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) adopted
(b) abandoned
(c) advertised
(d) alienated
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'adopted' means accepted or taken up.
उदाहरण: नई नीति अधिकांश कर्मचारियों द्वारा अपनाई गई है।
[RULE] Verb meaning acceptance

---

[Q1012] He was known ___ his generosity.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'for' introduces the reason or attribute.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी उदारता के लिए जाना जाता था।
[RULE] Preposition of attribute

---

[Q1013] The team ___ to win the championship last year.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was hoping
(b) hope
(c) hoped
(d) hopes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past continuous ('was hoping') expresses an intention in the past.
उदाहरण: टीम ने पिछले वर्ष चैंपियनशिप जीतने की आशा की थी।
[RULE] Past continuous for past intention

---

[Q1014] She gave a ___________ explanation, leaving every one confused.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) clear
(b) ambiguous
(c) simple
(d) concise
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'ambiguous' means unclear or open to multiple interpretations.
उदाहरण: उसने अस्पष्ट स्पष्टीकरण दिया, जिससे सब भ्रमित रह गए।
[RULE] Adjective meaning unclear

---

[Q1015] The book lies ___ the table.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) under
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'On' is used for objects resting on a surface.
उदाहरण: किताब टेबल पर रखी है।
[RULE] Preposition of location (surface)

---

[Q1016] She was disappointed ___ the sudden cancellation of the event.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) to
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *by* is used to indicate the cause of disappointment.
उदाहरण: वह अचानक रद्द होने से निराश थी।
[RULE] Preposition

---

[Q1017] They ___ finished their assignments before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) have
(b) had
(c) has
(d) having
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past perfect tense (*had finished*) is used to show an action completed before a specific time.
उदाहरण: उन्होंने समय सीमा से पहले अपना असाइनमेंट पूरा कर लिया था।
[RULE] Past Perfect

---

[Q1018] I would have helped ___ you were there.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) if
(b) when
(c) so that
(d) unless
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conditional clause requires *if* to express a hypothetical situation.
उदाहरण: अगर तुम वहाँ होते तो मैं मदद करता।
[RULE] Conditionals

---

[Q1019] The book was highly ___ by critics.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) praise
(b) praised
(c) praising
(d) praises
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past participle *praised* is used in passive voice.
उदाहरण: पुस्तक की आलोचकों द्वारा अत्यधिक प्रशंसा की गई।
[RULE] Passive Voice

---

[Q1020] His performance was ___ for the award.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eligible
(b) eligibility
(c) eligiblely
(d) eligibled
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective *eligible* describes suitability for something.
उदाहरण: उसकी प्रदर्शन पुरस्कार के लिए योग्य था।
[RULE] Adjective

---

[Q1021] They were astonished ___ the unexpected turn of events.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) to
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Preposition *at* is used after *astonished* to indicate the cause.
उदाहरण: वे अप्रत्याशित घटनाक्रम से चकित थे।
[RULE] Preposition

---

[Q1022] The river runs ___ the mountains.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) above
(b) below
(c) through
(d) across
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Preposition *through* shows passage within the mountains.
उदाहरण: नदी पर्वतों के बीच बहती है।
[RULE] Preposition

---

[Q1023] He will ___ the report by tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submits
(c) submitted
(d) submitting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive *submit* follows *will* for future simple tense.
उदाहरण: वह कल तक रिपोर्ट जमा करेगा।
[RULE] Future Tense

---

[Q1024] The new policy will ___ the existing regulations.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) alter
(b) alters
(c) altered
(d) altering
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive *alter* is used after *will* to express an action.
उदाहरण: नई नीति मौजूदा नियमों को बदल देगी।
[RULE] Infinitive

[Q1025] She was ___ a great photographer.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) known
(b) knows
(c) knew
(d) know
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle *known* used as adjective in passive construction.
उदाहरण: वह एक महान फोटोग्राफर के रूप में जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Passive Voice

[Q1026] The book is ___ the shelf.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) under
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When indicating position on a surface, we use the preposition *on*.
उदाहरण: पुस्तक शेल्फ पर रखी है।
[RULE] Preposition for position on a surface

---

[Q1027] He has a strong desire ___ learning new languages.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) of
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun *desire* is followed by the infinitive marker *to* when expressing purpose.
उदाहरण: उसे नई भाषा सीखने की तीव्र इच्छा है।
[RULE] Infinitive after desire noun

[Q1028] The students were asked ___ the questions.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) answer
(b) to answer
(c) answering
(d) answered
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After the verb *asked*, we use the infinitive *to answer* to indicate the action requested.
उदाहरण: छात्रों से प्रश्नों का उत्तर देने के लिए कहा गया।
[RULE] Infinitive after verb asked

---

[Q1029] She is ___ the party to night
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) going to attend
(b) attending
(c) go to attend
(d) going
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The present continuous tense *is attending* expresses an arrangement for the future.
उदाहरण: वह आज रात पार्टी में जा रही है।
[RULE] Present continuous for future arrangements

---

[Q1030] I have never seen a bird so ___.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) beautiful
(b) beautifully
(c) beauty
(d) beautifulness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective *beautiful* modifies the noun *bird*; *beautifully* would be an adverb and does not fit here.
उदाहरण: मैंने कभी इतना सुंदर पक्षी नहीं देखा।
[RULE] Adjective to modify noun

---

[Q1031] He was praised ___ his outstanding performance.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used after *praised* to indicate the reason.
उदाहरण: उसकी उत्कृष्ट प्रदर्शन के लिए उसकी तारीफ की गई।
[RULE] Preposition after praised

---

[Q1032] The project will be completed ___ December.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The preposition *by* indicates a deadline.
उदाहरण: परियोजना दिसंबर तक पूर्ण हो जाएगी।
[RULE] Preposition for deadlines

---

[Q1033] The child was very ___ after the accident.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) hurt
(b) hurtful
(c) hurther
(d) hurtly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*hurt* is an adjective describing the state of the child; *hurtful* means causing hurt, which does not fit.
उदाहरण: दुर्घटना के बाद बच्चा बहुत घायल था।
