---

[Q1034] She failed ___ the exam.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) in
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the verb *failed*, we use the infinitive marker *to* to express the action that was not achieved.
उदाहरण: वह परीक्षा में असफल रही।
[RULE] Infinitive after fail/failed

---

[Q1035] She has been working on this research project ________ three months.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'for' with a period of time (three months). Use 'since' for a point of time.
उदा. निश्चित समय (point of time) के लिए 'since' और अवधि (period of time) के लिए 'for' का प्रयोग करते हैं। 'three months' एक अवधि है, अतः 'for' सही है।
[RULE] Prepositions of Time (Since vs For)

---

[Q1036] The manager insisted ________ checking all the financial reports before the audit.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) with
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'insist' is followed by the preposition 'on'.
उदा. 'Insist' शब्द के बाद हमेशा फिक्स प्रीपोजिशन 'on' का प्रयोग किया जाता है (Insist on doing something)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1037] The committee reached a consensus ________ the new recruitment policy.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) on
(b) over
(c) about
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Consensus is typically followed by the preposition 'on' when referring to a specific topic.
उदा. आम सहमति (consensus) किसी मुद्दे पर होने के लिए 'consensus on' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with Nouns

---

[Q1038] Neither the teacher nor the students ________ present in the auditorium today.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the nearer subject (students, which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े होने पर वर्ब पास वाले सब्जेक्ट (students) के अनुसार plural ('were') होगी।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1039] Despite working hard, he could not succeed because he lacked ________.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) perseverance
(b) persecution
(c) perspiration
(d) perpertuity
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Perseverance' means persistent effort, which fits the context of failing despite hard work.
उदा. 'Perseverance' (लगन/निरंतर प्रयास) सही शब्द है क्योंकि सफलता के लिए कड़ी मेहनत के साथ निरंतरता की भी आवश्यकता होती है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1040] The government has taken several steps to ________ the economy during the down turn.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) bolster
(b) hinder
(c) obstruct
(d) impede
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Bolster' means to support or strengthen, which fits a positive economic intervention.
उदा. 'Bolster' का अर्थ मजबूत करना या सहारा देना होता है। सरकार अर्थव्यवस्था को मजबूत (bolster) करने के कदम उठाती है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Meaning

---

[Q1041] She is proficient ________ French and can translate technical documents easily.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is followed by the preposition 'in' (proficient in a subject/language).
उदा. किसी भाषा या विषय में दक्ष होने के लिए 'proficient in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition with Adjective

---

[Q1042] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure a promotion _________ the stiff competition.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because of
(b) despite of
(c) in spite
(d) due to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Because of' or 'due to' can be used to show cause. 'Despite of' is grammatically incorrect because 'despite' never takes 'of'. 'In spite' must be followed by 'of'.
उदा. 'Despite' के साथ कभी 'of' नहीं आता। 'In spite of' सही होता है, जबकि 'Because of' यहाँ कारण बताने के लिए सबसे उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Prepositions / Conjunctions of Cause

---

[Q1043] The manager asked the employees to _________ the proposal before the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) go through
(b) go on
(c) go off
(d) go down
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Go through' means to read or examine something carefully. 
उदा. 'Go through' एक Phrasal Verb है जिसका अर्थ किसी दस्तावेज़ को ध्यान से पढ़ना या जांचना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1044] Hardly had I stepped out of the house _________ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before'. 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के जोड़े के रूप में हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q1045] She is proficient _________ French and can translate the document easily.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is always followed by the preposition 'in' when referring to a skill or language.
उदा. 'Proficient' (दक्ष या निपुण) शब्द के बाद हमेशा निश्चित प्रीपोजिशन 'in' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1046] If the government _________ strict actions against corruption, the situation would have been different.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) had taken
(b) took
(c) has taken
(d) was taking
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In type 3 conditional sentences (unreal past), the 'if' clause takes the past perfect tense ('had + V3') and the main clause takes 'would/could/might + have + V3'.
उदा. यह Type 3 Conditional वाक्य है, जिसमें कल्पना की जाती है। यदि मुख्य clause में 'would have + V3' हो, तो 'if' वाले भाग में 'had + V3' आता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q1047] The criminal was found guilty and sentenced _________ death.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'sentence' is followed by the preposition 'to' (e.g., sentenced to death/imprisonment).
उदा. 'Sentence' (सजा सुनाना) के बाद हमेशा प्रीपोजिशन 'to' का प्रयोग होता है, जैसे 'sentenced to death'।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1048] The teacher advised the students to refrain _________ making noise in the library.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) against
(c) in
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Verbs like refrain, abstain, prevent, prohibit are followed by the preposition 'from' + gerund (V1-ing).
उदा. 'Refrain' (बचना या दूर रहना) के बाद हमेशा 'from' प्रीपोजिशन और उसके बाद Gerund (V1-ing) आता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with Verbs

---

[Q1049] Neither the captain nor the players _________ present at the ceremony yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it. 'Players' is plural, so the plural verb 'were' is used (and past tense is required due to 'yesterday').
उदा. जब दो कर्ता 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो क्रिया (verb) अपने सबसे पास वाले कर्ता के अनुसार आती है। यहाँ 'players' बहुवचन है और 'yesterday' से भूतकाल का पता चलता है, अतः 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1050] His speech was so _________ that the audience was spellbound for hours.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) captivating
(b) tedious
(c) monotonous
(d) dreary
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Captivating' means attracting and holding interest; charming. Other options (tedious, monotonous, dreary) mean boring or dull, which contradict 'spellbound'.
उदा. 'Captivating' का अर्थ मोहक या आकर्षित करने वाला होता है। चूँकि श्रोता मंत्रमुग्ध थे, इसलिए भाषण आकर्षक (captivating) ही रहा होगा।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1051] She is senior _________ me in service, yet she consults me on important decisions.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) to
(b) than
(c) from
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Latin comparative adjectives like senior, junior, superior, inferior, prior, posterior are followed by 'to' instead of 'than'.
उदा. 'Senior', 'junior', 'superior' जैसे शब्दों के बाद तुलना के लिए 'than' नहीं बल्कि 'to' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Adjectives (Comparisons)

---

[Q1052] She has been working ____ this job for 5 years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'in' to indicate a place of work. 
उदा. वह इस नौकरी में पाँच साल से काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Preposition of place (in)

---

[Q1053] The students were asked to write a letter ____ the principal.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) of
(d) about
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'to' when addressing a letter. 
उदा. छात्र प्रधानाचार्य को पत्र लिखने को कहा गया।
[RULE] Preposition for addressing (to)

---

[Q1054] He has always been fond ____ playing chess.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) to
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The adjective 'fond' is followed by 'of'. 
उदा. वह शतरंज खेलना पसंद करता है।
[RULE] Adjective + preposition (fond of)

---

[Q1055] The manager will start the meeting at 10 o’clock ____ Thursday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'on' before days of the week. 
उदा. प्रबंधक गुरुवार को 10 बजे बैठक शुरू करेगा।
[RULE] Preposition for days (on)

---

[Q1056] The team performed ____ a great job during the tournament.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the indefinite article 'a' before a consonant sound. 
उदा. टीम ने टूर्नामेंट के दौरान एक बेहतरीन काम किया।
[RULE] Indefinite article (a)

---

[Q1057] After ____ the meeting, she was very upset.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) having
(b) to have
(c) had
(d) having to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the gerund form after 'after'. 
उदा. बैठक के बाद वह बहुत परेशान थी।
[RULE] Gerund after preposition (having)

---

[Q1058] It is difficult ____ understand the new regulations.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) of
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use the infinitive form after 'difficult'. 
उदा. नई नियमों को समझना कठिन है।
[RULE] Adjective + infinitive (difficult to)

---

[Q1059] The chef ______ the dish with a pinch of salt.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) seasoned
(b) season
(c) seasoning
(d) seasoned with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Past tense verb 'seasoned' describes the action. 
उदा. शेफ ने डिश को नमक के चुटकी से सजीव किया।
[RULE] Past simple verb (seasoned)

---

[Q1060] The company ______ a new product last month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) launched
(b) launch
(c) launching
(d) launches
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use simple past tense for completed action. 
उदा. कंपनी ने पिछले महीने एक नया उत्पाद लॉन्च किया।
[RULE] Simple past tense (launched)

[Q1061] She was born____ 1990.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'in' with years. 
उदा. वह 1990 में पैदा हुई।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'in'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition for years (in)

[Q1062] The committee will convene ____ the next session.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2018]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use 'on' before days or dates. 
उदा. समिति अगली सत्र में सोमवार को मिलेगी।
[RULE] Preposition for dates (on)

[Q1063] She has been working ___ the company for 8 years.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'in' to indicate association with an organization.
संगठन के साथ संबद्धता दिखाने के लिए 'in' का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition – in the organization

[Q1064] The meeting was ___ at 10 a.m.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) held
(b) held on
(c) held by
(d) held to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past participle 'held' is used as an adjective describing the meeting.
भूतकालिक विशेषण 'held' का उपयोग बैठक का वर्णन करने के लिये किया जाता है।
[RULE] Past participle as adjective

[Q1065] He was ___ by the news.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) surprised
(b) surprise
(c) surprised by
(d) surprise by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'surprised' is a past participle adjective used after 'was'.
'Surprised' भूतकालिक विशेषण है जो 'was' के बाद आता है।
[RULE] Past participle adjective

[Q1066] She has ___ her home work.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) finished
(b) finish
(c) finishing
(d) finishes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple 'finished' shows a completed action.
भूतकाल सरल क्रिया 'finished' पूर्ण कार्य को दर्शाती है।
[RULE] Past simple tense

[Q1067] They were looking forward ___ to the picnic.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) for to
(c) to
(d) to to
[ANS] c
[SOL]
After 'look forward', the preposition 'to' is followed by an infinitive.
'Look forward' के बाद 'to' + infinitive आता है।
[RULE] Look forward + to + infinitive

[Q1068] The book was ___ by the librarian.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) cared
(b) cared for
(c) care
(d) caring
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'cared for' is a phrasal verb meaning looked after.
'cared for' का अर्थ है देखभाल करना।
[RULE] Phrasal verb – care for

[Q1069] We should ___ the proposal before submitting.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) review
(b) to review
(c) reviewing
(d) reviewed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After modal 'should', use bare infinitive.
'should' के बाद बिना 'to' के infinitive आता है।
[RULE] Modal + bare infinitive

[Q1070] He is ___ a new job.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) looking for
(b) looking to
(c) looking at
(d) looking
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'looking for' indicates searching or seeking.
'looking for' का अर्थ खोज रहा है।

[Q1071] The new manager is known ___ the department.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) of
(c) for
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'in' to refer to a place or organization.
स्थान या संगठन को दर्शाने के लिए 'in' प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition – in

[Q1072] She has been teaching ___ the school.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) for
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'at' is used with institutions such as schools.
'At' संस्था के साथ प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition – at the institution

---

[Q1073] The government has launched a massive campaign to _________ awareness about road safety.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) spread
(b) spreaded
(c) spred
(d) spreaing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
To infinitive ('to') takes the base form (V1) of the verb. 'Spread' remains the same in V1, V2, and V3.
उदा. Infinitives ('to' + verb) के बाद हमेशा Verb की Base Form (V1) का प्रयोग होता है। 'Spread' की तीनों forms समान होती हैं।
[RULE] Non-finite Verbs (Infinitives)

---

[Q1074] She has been working in this organization _________ five years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) since
(c) for
(d) during
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Use 'for' with a period of time (five years) and 'since' with a point of time.
उदा. अवधि (period of time) जैसे 'five years' के लिए 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, जबकि निश्चित समय (point of time) के लिए 'since' आता है।
[RULE] Prepositions of Time

---

[Q1075] Had you informed me earlier, I _________ attended the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will have
(b) would have
(c) had
(d) would
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Third conditional structure: If clause has 'had + V3', the main clause takes 'would have + V3'.
उदा. Conditional Sentences के Type 3 में यदि subordinate clause में 'had + V3' हो, तो main clause में 'would have + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3)

---

[Q1076] The manager was highly impressed _________ her dedication and hard work.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) with
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'impressed' is generally followed by the preposition 'with' when referring to things or qualities.
उदा. Adjective 'impressed' के बाद सामान्यतः preposition 'with' का प्रयोग होता है जब किसी की योग्यताओं या गुणों की प्रशंसा की जाए।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1077] Hardly had he stepped out of the house _________ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) while
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly / Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before'.
उदा. Correlative conjunction 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है (जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है)।
[RULE] Conjunctions and Inversion

---

[Q1078] Neither the teacher nor the students _________ present in the class room.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject nearest to it ('students', which is plural).
उदा. जब दो Subject 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो verb अपने पास वाले Subject के अनुसार आती है। यहाँ 'students' plural है, अतः 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1079] Despite working hard, he could not clear the exam because his preparation was _________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) adequate
(b) inadequate
(c) efficient
(d) sufficient
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Contextual vocabulary: failing the exam despite hard work implies the preparation was insufficient or 'inadequate'.
उदा. वाक्य के संदर्भ (context) के अनुसार, कठिन परिश्रम करने के बाद भी परीक्षा पास न कर पाने का कारण तैयारी का अपर्याप्त यानी 'inadequate' होना है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1080] Choose the correct preposition: He succeeded _________ achieving his goal after years of struggle.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) with
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The verb 'succeed' is always followed by the preposition 'in' (+ gerund). 
उदा. Verb 'succeed' के बाद हमेशा fixed preposition 'in' का प्रयोग होता है और इसके बाद gerund (V1+ing) आता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition with Verbs

---

[Q1081] The thief confessed _________ the stolen goods from the locked house.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) stealing
(b) to steal
(c) to stealing
(d) stolen
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The phrase 'confess to' is followed by a gerund (-ing form), as 'to' here acts as a preposition.
उदा. Phrasal verb या expression 'confess to' के बाद preposition 'to' होने के कारण Gerund (V1+ing) यानी 'to stealing' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Gerunds after Prepositional Phrases

---

[Q1082] A committee of experts _________ appointed to investigate the financial scam.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) have
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Collective nouns like 'committee' take a singular verb when acting as a single unit.
उदा. Collective noun ('committee') जब एक इकाई (single unit) के रूप में कार्य करे, तो उसके साथ Singular Verb ('was') का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement (Collective Nouns)

---

[Q1083] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure the promotion as his rival was ________ him in experience.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) superior to
(b) superior than
(c) senior above
(d) junior than
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjectives ending in '-or' (like superior, inferior, senior, junior, prior) are followed by the preposition 'to', not 'than'.
उदा. 'Superior', 'junior', 'senior' आदि के बाद हमेशा preposition 'to' का प्रयोग होता है, 'than' का नहीं।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition with Adjectives ending in '-or'

---

[Q1084] The minister appealed to the public to maintain peace and not to ________ to rumors.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) give in
(b) give up
(c) give away
(d) give off
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Give in' means to surrender or yield. 'Give up' means to stop trying. The context demands yielding to rumors.
उदा. 'Give in' का अर्थ हार मानना या झुकना होता है। यहाँ अफवाहों के आगे न झुकने की बात हो रही है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs (Give)

---

[Q1085] Neither of the two candidates who applied for the post of branch manager ________ qualified.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) are
(b) is
(c) were
(d) have been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Neither of' takes a singular verb because it refers to none of the two individually.
उदा. 'Neither of' के बाद plural noun/pronoun आता है, लेकिन verb हमेशा singular (is/has/was) होती है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1086] She has a natural ________ for painting and has won several national awards.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) aptitude
(b) attitude
(c) altitude
(d) amplitude
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Aptitude' means a natural ability to do something. 'Attitude' means a settled way of thinking.
उदा. 'Aptitude' का अर्थ किसी कार्य में स्वाभाविक योग्यता या प्रतिभा होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Confusing Words

---

[Q1087] Had the police arrived on time, the tragedy ________ averted.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) could be
(b) could have been
(c) would be
(d) will have been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In third conditional sentences (Past hypothetical), the structure in the main clause is 'would/could/might + have + V3'.
उदा. Past conditional (Had + Subject + V3) के वाक्य के दूसरे भाग में 'would/could/might + have + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3)

---

[Q1088] The committee reached a consensus after hours of discussion and decided to ________ the new policy immediately.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) enact
(b) adapt
(c) adept
(d) adopt
[ANS] d
[SOL]
'Adopt' means to legally take up or start using a policy/idea. 'Adapt' means to adjust.
उदा. किसी नियम, नीति या प्रस्ताव को स्वीकार करने के लिए 'adopt' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Confusing Words (Adopt vs Adapt)

---

[Q1089] The government has launched a massive campaign to ________ awareness about water conservation.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) spread
(b) spoil
(c) spend
(d) sprain
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Collocation: We 'spread awareness' or 'raise awareness', making 'spread' the correct fit.
उदा. जागरूकता फैलाने के लिए 'spread awareness' सही collocation है।
[RULE] Collocations

---

[Q1090] No sooner did the train stop at the platform ________ the passengers rushed towards the exit doors.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) when
(b) then
(c) than
(d) that
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'No sooner' is always followed by the conjunction 'than'.
उदा. 'No sooner' के साथ हमेशा 'than' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'Scarcely/Hardly' के साथ 'when/before' आता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q1091] The company's profits have plummeted this quarter, forcing management to ________ drastic cost-cutting measures.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) resort to
(b) result in
(c) rely upon
(d) reflect on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Resort to' means to do something bad or extreme because you have no other choice.
उदा. 'Resort to' का अर्थ किसी मजबूरी में कोई सख्त कदम उठाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs / Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1092] It is high time the authorities ________ strict action against illegal parking in the city.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) take
(b) took
(c) have taken
(d) will take
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Phrases like 'It is time', 'It is high time', 'It is about time' are followed by a clause in the simple past tense (V2).
उदा. 'It is high time' के बाद यदि subject दिया हो, तो verb की Past Form (V2) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Tenses with Special Phrases

---

[Q1093] She has been working ________ the company for over five years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) to
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the preposition "in" to indicate the place of employment.
उदाहरण: "She has been working **in** the company for over five years."
[RULE] Preposition usage with companies

---

[Q1094] The train ________ at 10 a.m. tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) will depart
(b) is depart
(c) departed
(d) depart
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Future simple tense: subject + will + base form of verb.
उदाहरण: "The train **will depart** at 10 a.m. tomorrow."
[RULE] Future simple tense

---

[Q1095] He is ________ his project when I called.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) working on
(b) work on
(c) worked on
(d) working
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present continuous + preposition "on".
उदाहरण: "He is **working on** his project when I called."
[RULE] Present continuous + preposition

---

[Q1096] Please refrain ________ the kitchen while cooking.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) in
(c) of
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Refrain from" is a fixed phrase.
उदाहरण: "Please refrain **from** the kitchen while cooking."
[RULE] Fixed prepositional phrase "refrain from"

---

[Q1097] She has been ________ the news for the last hour.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) following
(b) followed
(c) follow
(d) followed by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Following" is the present participle used after "has been".
उदाहरण: "She has been **following** the news for the last hour."
[RULE] Present perfect continuous tense

---

[Q1098] The new policy will ________ the employees' morale.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) boost
(b) boosts
(c) boosted
(d) boosting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Future simple tense: subject + will + base verb.
उदाहरण: "The new policy will **boost** the employees' morale."
[RULE] Future simple tense

---

[Q1099] If he ________ earlier, he would have caught the bus.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) left
(c) has left
(d) leaves
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Had left" is the past perfect form used in third conditional.
उदाहरण: "If he **had left** earlier, he would have caught the bus."
[RULE] Third conditional (past perfect)

---

[Q1100] The manager is known ________ his strict discipline.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) to
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Known for" is the correct preposition to express reputation.
उदाहरण: "The manager is known **for** his strict discipline."
[RULE] Preposition after "known"

---

[Q1101] They will start the meeting ________ the chairman's arrival.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) upon
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Upon" indicates that the meeting will begin immediately after.
उदाहरण: "They will start the meeting **upon** the chairman's arrival."
[RULE] Preposition meaning "immediately after"

---

[Q1102] I couldn't find my keys; they must have ________ some where.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) gone
(b) went
(c) went out
(d) gone out
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Must have gone" is the correct modal perfect construction.
उदाहरण: "I couldn't find my keys; they must have **gone** somewhere."
[RULE] Modal perfect construction

---

[Q1103] He had no ________ to explain the sudden change in his behavior.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) cause
(b) excuse
(c) reason
(d) solution
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The word *reason* is a noun meaning a cause or explanation.
उदा. "उसे अपनी अचानक परिवर्तन के लिए कोई कारण नहीं मिला।"
[RULE] Noun: reason

---

[Q1104] The committee will meet ________ Friday morning.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use *on* with days of the week.
उदा. "समिति शुक्रवार सुबह मिलेगी।"
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'on'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition: on (days)

---

[Q1105] She was ________ to the new job, but she accepted it.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reluctant
(b) regret
(c) indecisive
(d) hesitant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*Reluctant* is an adjective meaning unwilling or disinclined.
उदा. "वह नयी नौकरी के लिए अनिच्छुक थी, पर उसने स्वीकार कर ली।"
[RULE] Adjective: reluctant

---

[Q1106] The results were inconclusive, ________ we decided to conduct further tests.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) hence
(b) however
(c) therefore
(d) meanwhile
[ANS] c
[SOL]
*Therefore* is a conjunctive adverb meaning 'as a result'.
उदा. "परिणाम अनिश्चित थे, इसलिए हमने आगे परीक्षण किए।"
[RULE] Conjunction/adverb: therefore

---

[Q1107] They have been married for ________ years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) four
(b) forever
(c) a
(d) many
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use a cardinal number to indicate exact years.
उदा. "वे चार साल से विवाहित हैं।"
[RULE] Cardinal number: four

---

[Q1108] The manager asked the employees to submit the reports ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) by
(d) before
[ANS] c
[SOL]
*By* indicates the deadline for submission.
उदा. "कर्मचारियों से रिपोर्ट जमा करने को कहा गया है।"
[RULE] Preposition: by (deadline)

---

[Q1109] He has been studying English ________ 6 years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) until
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*For* is used with a duration of time.
उदा. "वह छह वर्षों से अंग्रेजी पढ़ रहा है।"
[RULE] Preposition: for (duration)

---

[Q1110] The children were so tired that they fell asleep ________ the play ground.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) under
(b) on
(c) inside
(d) in
[ANS] d
[SOL]
*In* is used to indicate being inside an area.
उदा. "बच्चे खेल के मैदान में सो गए।"
[RULE] Preposition: in (location)

---

[Q1111] Please turn off the lights before you leave, otherwise the house will stay ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) dark
(b) darkened
(c) darkening
(d) darkness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*Dark* is an adjective describing the state of not being lit.
उदा. "बत्तियाँ बंद करो, वरना घर अँधेरा रहेगा।"
[RULE] Adjective: dark

---

[Q1112] We should not ignore the warnings; otherwise, we could face ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) many consequences
(b) serious consequences
(c) seriousness
(d) many seriousness
[ANS] b
[SOL]
*Serious consequences* refers to significant negative outcomes.
उदा. "चेतावनी को अनदेखा न करें, अन्यथा गंभीर परिणाम हो सकते हैं।"
[RULE] Phrase: serious consequences

---

[Q1113] Despite his best efforts, he could not secure the promotion because he lacked the necessary __________ for the senior managerial role.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) credentials
(b) acquisitions
(c) apparel
(d) appliances
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Credentials refer to qualifications, achievements, or aspects of a person's background that indicate they are suitable for a task. 'Credentials' is the correct vocabulary choice.
उदा. 'Credentials' का अर्थ योग्यता या प्रमाण पत्र होता है, जो किसी वरिष्ठ पद के लिए आवश्यक होते हैं।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1114] The minister emphasized that the government is fully committed __________ the welfare of marginalized communities.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) with
(d) towards
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective 'committed' is always followed by the fixed preposition 'to' (committed to something).
उदा. 'Committed' के बाद हमेशा निश्चित प्रीपोजिशन 'to' का प्रयोग होता है (committed to)।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1115] Had the authorities taken timely action, the disaster __________ averted.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) could be
(b) can be
(c) could have been
(d) will have been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure expressing an unfulfilled past condition: 'If + past perfect (had taken), subject + would/could/might + have + V3'. Thus, 'could have been' is correct.
उदा. यह पास्ट कंडीशनल (Third Conditional) का वाक्य है जिसमें 'Had + V3' के साथ दूसरे क्लॉज़ में 'would/could/might have + V3' आता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q1116] She is well known for her __________ nature; she always shares her belongings and helps those in need without he sit at i on.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) malevolent
(b) parsimonious
(c) altruistic
(d) pusillanimous
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Altruistic means showing a disinterested and selfless concern for the well-being of others, which fits the context of sharing and helping.
उदा. 'Altruistic' का अर्थ परोपकारी या दूसरों की भलाई करने वाला होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1117] Hardly had the train stepped onto the platform __________ the passengers rushed towards the coaches.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly' or 'Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before', whereas 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q1118] The manager asked the employee to __________ the report for any grammatical errors before final submission.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) look down
(b) look into
(c) look over
(d) look after
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Look over' means to examine something quickly or inspect it for errors. 'Look into' means to investigate. Here, checking a report for errors requires 'look over'.
उदा. 'Look over' का अर्थ किसी चीज़ की सरसरी तौर पर या गलती ढूंढने के लिए जांच करना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1119] Not only the principal but also the teachers __________ present at the annual function.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are connected by 'not only... but also', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it. Here, 'teachers' is plural, so the plural verb 'were' is used.
उदा. जब दो सब्जेक्ट्स 'not only... but also' से जुड़े हों, तो वर्ब अपने सबसे नज़दीकी सब्जेक्ट के अनुसार आती है ('teachers' बहुवचन है, अतः 'were' आएगा)।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1120] The committee members could not reach a consensus because their opinions on the new policy were completely __________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) homogeneous
(b) divergent
(c) consistent
(d) congruent
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Divergent means tending to be different or develop in different directions, which explains why they could not reach a consensus.
उदा. 'Divergent' का अर्थ भिन्न या अलग-अलग दिशाओं में जाने वाला होता है, जिससे सहमति (consensus) नहीं बन पाई।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1121] Rohan takes pride __________ his academic achievements and often boasts about them.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The idiom/phrase is 'to take pride in' something (or the verb 'pride oneself on'). Therefore, 'in' is the correct preposition.
उदा. सही फ्रेज़ 'take pride in' होता है, जिसका अर्थ किसी बात पर गर्व करना है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1122] By the time the rescue team arrived at the remote village, the storm __________ heavy damage to the infrastructure.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) caused
(b) has caused
(c) had caused
(d) was causing
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In sentences with 'By the time + past simple', the action that occurred earlier in the past must be in the past perfect tense ('had + V3').
उदा. 'By the time + V2' वाले वाक्यों में जो कार्य पहले समाप्त हुआ हो, उसके लिए Past Perfect Tense ('had + V3') का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Tenses

---

[Q1123] She was reprimanded for ________ the dead line of the project.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Quaint
(b) missed
(c) misses
(d) missing
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The correct form is "missing" because the verb ‘to miss’ is used in participle form after ‘reprimanded for’.

सही रूप "missing" है क्योंकि "reprimanded for" के बाद क्रिया का पार्टिसिपल रूप प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Verb Participle

---

[Q1124] The committee has decided to ________ the meeting until next month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) postpone
(b) postponed
(c) postponing
(d) Zealous
[ANS] c
[SOL]
After the verb ‘decided’ we use the infinitive form ‘to postpone’. Thus the correct option is "postponing" as it is the present participle used after the preposition ‘until’.

"decided to postpone" के बाद ‘postpone’ का इंफिनिटिव रूप आता है, इसलिए ‘postponing’ सही है।
[RULE] Infinitive and Present Participle

---

[Q1125] He had to ________ his decision after receiving new evidence.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reconsider
(b) reconsidered
(c) reconsidering
(d) Lucid
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The sentence is in simple past tense, so the base verb ‘reconsider’ is used after ‘had to’.

यह वाक्य साधारण भूतकाल में है, इसलिए ‘had to’ के बाद मूल क्रिया ‘reconsider’ प्रयोग होती है।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1126] The solution to the problem lies ________ a clear understanding of the data.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The idiomatic expression is "lies in".

यह वाक्य ‘lies in’ वाक्यांश का ही प्रयोग करता है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q1127] The scientist ________ a groundbreaking theory during the conference.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) presented
(b) presenting
(c) present
(d) Munificent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct past tense form is ‘presented’.

यहाँ भूतकाल का प्रयोग है, इसलिए ‘presented’ सही है।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1128] I am looking forward to ________ you at the event next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) see
(b) seeing
(c) to see
(d) to seeing
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After the phrase ‘looking forward to’ we use the gerund form ‘seeing’.

‘looking forward to’ के बाद ‘seeing’ जेरंड रूप ही आता है।
[RULE] Gerund after Preposition

---

[Q1129] The company’s profits have been steadily rising for the past five years, ________ it a sign of healthy growth.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which
(b) that
(c) who
(d) whichever
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative pronoun ‘which’ is used to give additional information about the preceding clause.

यहाँ ‘which’ का प्रयोग उपवाक्य को जोड़ने के लिए किया गया है।
[RULE] Relative Pronouns

---

[Q1130] He has always been ________ his younger brother, offering help whenever needed.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a mentor
(b) a mentoring
(c) mentor
(d) mentoring
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The adjective ‘mentoring’ (participle form) correctly describes his role.

यहाँ ‘mentoring’ विशेषण के रूप में प्रयोग हुआ है।
[RULE] Adjective from Verb

---

[Q1131] The new policy will come into effect ________ the end of this month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The idiomatic phrase is ‘come into effect in’ a month.

यहाँ ‘in’ का प्रयोग ही सही है।
[RULE] Prepositions

---

[Q1132] After the meeting, the direct or ________ that the project would be delayed by two weeks.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) notice
(b) noted
(c) noticing
(d) Pensive
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The past tense form ‘noted’ correctly conveys that the director made a remark.

भूतकाल में ‘noted’ प्रयोग किया गया है।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1133] The committee was ___ to the project proposal, but they expressed some concerns.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) pleased
(b) pleasing
(c) pleasement
(d) pleasure
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *to be* with a past participle (pleased) is used to express a state of satisfaction.
उदा. समिति प्रस्ताव से संतुष्ट थी।
[RULE] Verb + Past Participle (State)

---

[Q1134] She has been working in the company ___ 2015.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *since* to indicate a point in time from which something continues to the present.
उदा. वह 2015 से कंपनी में काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Preposition *since* for starting point

---

[Q1135] The new policy will ___ the existing regulations to make them more effective.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) amend
(b) amending
(c) amendment
(d) amended
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *amend* is used to indicate a change or improvement.
उदा. नई नीति मौजूदा विनियमों में सुधार करेगी।
[RULE] Verb (amend) – Change / Modify

---

[Q1136] He is looking forward ___ a chance to visit the Him a lay as.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrasal verb *look forward to* is followed by a noun or gerund.
उदा. वह हिमालय की यात्रा का इंतजार कर रहा है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verb *look forward to* + Gerund

---

[Q1137] The scientist was ___ for his discovery of a new method of purification.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) awarded
(b) award
(c) awarding
(d) awards
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *to award* is used in its past tense to indicate that a prize was given.
उदा. वैज्ञानिक को नई शुद्धिकरण विधि के लिए सम्मानित किया गया।
[RULE] Past Tense Verb *award*

---

[Q1138] The report highlights the need ___ stringent measures to control pollution.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) of
(d) at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The noun *need* is followed by *to* + infinitive to express necessity.
उदा. रिपोर्ट प्रदूषण नियंत्रण के लिए कड़ी कार्रवाई की आवश्यकता पर प्रकाश डालती है।
[RULE] Noun *need* + to + Infinitive

---

[Q1139] The conference will be held ___ the National Convention Centre on 12th March.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"At" is used for a specific place or venue.
उदा. सम्मेलन 12 मार्च को राष्ट्रीय सम्मेलन केंद्र में आयोजित किया जाएगा।
[RULE] Preposition *at* for specific venue

---

[Q1140] After ___ the final report, the committee decided to postpone the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reviewing
(b) review
(c) reviewed
(d) reviewer
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Gerund *reviewing* acts as the object of the preposition *after*.
उदा. अंतिम रिपोर्ट की समीक्षा के बाद समिति ने बैठक को स्थगित करने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Gerund after *after*

---

[Q1141] The students were ___ to take part in the science fair.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eligible
(b) eligibility
(c) eligiblely
(d) eligibleing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Eligible" is the adjective required to describe the students' suitability.
उदा. छात्र विज्ञान मेले में भाग लेने के योग्य थे।
[RULE] Adjective *eligible*

---

[Q1142] The new law aims ___ the rights of consumers against unfair trade practices.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) protect
(b) protecting
(c) protected
(d) protects
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The infinitive *to protect* is required after *aims*.
उदा. नया कानून उपभोक्ताओं के अधिकारों की रक्षा के लिए है।
[RULE] Verb *aims* + to + Infinitive

---

[Q1143] He _________ the new policy, but he didn't understand its implications.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) adopted
(b) adopt
(c) adopts
(d) adopting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past tense of the verb 'adopt' is 'adopted', which indicates a completed action.
यहाँ 'adopted' का प्रयोग भूतकाल के लिए किया गया है, जो क्रिया के पूर्ण होने को दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Simple Past Tense of regular verb

---

[Q1144] The committee will convene ________ next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'in' is used with months, seasons, and years.
'इन' का प्रयोग महीनों, वर्षों के साथ किया जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition for time expressions

---

[Q1145] She is known _______ her generosity.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'for' is used after 'known' to indicate the reason.
'known' के बाद 'for' का प्रयोग कारण बताने के लिए होता है।
[RULE] Preposition after known

---

[Q1146] The machine was repaired _________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) quickly
(b) quick
(c) quicker
(d) quickest
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'quickly' is the adverb form of the adjective 'quick', used to describe how the action was performed.
'quickly' एक क्रिया विशेषण है जो दर्शाता है कि कार्य कैसे किया गया।
[RULE] Adverb formation from adjective

---

[Q1147] He has been working here for _____ years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ten
(b) ten years
(c) tenly
(d) tenure
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When expressing duration, we use a numeral alone.
अवधि बताने के लिए केवल संख्या का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Numeral for duration

---

[Q1148] The book was published ________ 2005.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'in' is used with years.
साल के साथ 'in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'in'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition for year

---

[Q1149] We should not ignore ________ of the issue.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the root cause
(b) the root causes
(c) the root cause's
(d) the root cause of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'root cause' is a noun phrase; the correct form is singular.
सही शब्द 'root cause' है, जिसे एकवचन में प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Noun phrase usage

---

[Q1150] The study was conducted _______ a controlled environment.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'in' is used to indicate location within an environment.
'इन' का प्रयोग किसी स्थान के भीतर होने के लिए किया जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition for location

---

[Q1151] She will be __________ the project after the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) presenting
(b) present
(c) presented
(d) presentation
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The gerund form 'presenting' follows 'will be' to indicate future continuous action.
'will be' के बाद 'presenting' जैसा gerund प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Gerund after modal verb

---

[Q1152] He was praised ________ his hard work.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2023 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'for' is used after 'praised' to indicate reason.
'praised' के बाद 'for' का प्रयोग कारण बताने के लिए होता है।
[RULE] Preposition after praised

---

[Q1153] The manager advised his team to ________ the opportunity without any he sit at i on.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) grab
(b) grasp
(c) seize
(d) hold
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Grab' is commonly used with 'opportunity' in modern business contexts to mean taking something quickly and eagerly.
उदा. जब कोई मौका मिले, तो उसे बिना किसी झिझक के 'grab' (पकड़ना) करना चाहिए।
[RULE] Collocation

---

[Q1154] Despite her heavy workload, she managed to ________ time for her hobbies.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) carve out
(b) bring out
(c) cut out
(d) take out
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Phrasal verb 'carve out' means to create or obtain something, especially by careful effort.
उदा. व्यस्त कार्यसूची के बावजूद अपने शौक के लिए समय निकालना 'carve out' कहलाता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1155] He is very particular ________ his dress and appearance.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) about
(b) for
(c) with
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'particular' is followed by the preposition 'about' when referring to being fastidious or meticulous about something.
उदा. 'Particular about' का अर्थ किसी चीज़ के प्रति बहुत अधिक सावधान या नकचढ़ा होना होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1156] The police interrogated the suspect to ________ the truth behind the mysterious theft.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) elicit
(b) illicit
(c) evoke
(d) emit
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Elicit' means to evoke or draw out a reaction, answer, or fact from someone. 'Illicit' is an adjective meaning unlawful.
उदा. सच को बाहर निकलवाने (elicit) के लिए पुलिस ने संदिग्ध से पूछताछ की।
[RULE] Confusing Words / Vocabulary

---

[Q1157] Select the correct form of the verb to fill in the blank. Hardly had I stepped out of the house ________ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' or 'before', whereas 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Hardly' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions / Correlatives

---

[Q1158] The government has launched a massive awareness campaign to ________ the spread of malaria.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) curb
(b) promote
(c) accelerate
(d) foster
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Curb' means to restrain or keep in check. We curb the spread of a disease.
उदा. बीमारी के फैलने को रोकने (curb) के लिए सरकार ने अभियान शुरू किया है।
[RULE] Vocabulary in Context

---

[Q1159] Select the most appropriate preposition to fill in the blank. She was endowed ________ rare musical talents from her childhood.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) with
(b) by
(c) in
(d) from
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'endow' is strictly followed by the preposition 'with' when talking about possessing a quality or talent.
उदा. किसी विशेष गुण से संपन्न होने के लिए 'endowed with' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1160] Select the most appropriate synonym/vocabulary word to fill in the blank. The witness gave a ________ account of the accident, leaving no details out.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) lucid
(b) vague
(c) turbid
(d) obscure
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Lucid' means expressed clearly; easy to understand. Since no details were left out, the account was clear.
उदा. स्पष्ट और आसानी से समझ आने वाले विवरण को 'lucid' कहते हैं।
[RULE] Vocabulary

---

[Q1161] If I ________ the king of this country, I would eradicate poverty completely.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) am
(d) be
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In conditional sentences expressing an unreal or hypothetical past/present wish, 'were' is used for all subjects with the verb 'to be'.
उदा. काल्पनिक इच्छा व्यक्त करने के लिए सभी कर्ता (subjects) के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood / Conditionals

---

[Q1162] His speech was so ________ that the audience was moved to tears.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) poignant
(b) trivial
(c) tedious
(d) harsh
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Poignant' means evoking a keen sense of sadness or regret, which fits the context of moving the audience to tears.
उदा. 'Poignant' का अर्थ मार्मिक या दिल को छू लेने वाला होता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary

---

[Q1163] She is proficient _____ several languages, including French and German.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is always followed by the preposition 'in' to indicate skill or expertise in a subject or activity.
उदा. Adjective 'proficient' के बाद हमेशा preposition 'in' का प्रयोग होता है, जिसका अर्थ किसी चीज़ में कुशल होना होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1164] The government has taken strict measures to _____ inflation in the country.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) curb
(b) boost
(c) elevate
(d) foster
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The word 'curb' means to restrain or keep in check, which is the appropriate vocabulary choice when talking about controlling inflation.
उदा. महंगाई (inflation) को नियंत्रित करने के लिए 'curb' (रोक लगाना/नियंत्रित करना) सबसे उचित शब्द है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1165] If I _____ the king of the country, I would eradicate poverty.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In conditional sentences expressing an imaginary, unreal present situation, 'were' is used with all subjects even if they are singular.
उदा. अवास्तविक या काल्पनिक शर्त (Imaginative condition) वाले वाक्य में सभी subjects के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Subjunctive Mood)

---

[Q1166] He is addicted _____ smoking, which is injurious to health.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) with
(b) to
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase 'addicted to' takes the preposition 'to', followed by a gerund or noun.
उदा. 'Addicted' के बाद हमेशा preposition 'to' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1167] The committee _____ divided in its opinion regarding the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) has
(d) have
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a collective noun like 'committee' is divided in its opinion, it takes a plural verb and plural pronoun.
उदा. जब collective noun (जैसे committee) में मतभेद हो, तो वह plural verb लेता है ('are')।
[RULE] Collective Noun and Verb Agreement

---

[Q1168] Despite working hard, he could not achieve the desired _____.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) success
(b) successful
(c) succeed
(d) successfully
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the determiner 'the' and adjective 'desired', a noun is required. 'Success' is the noun form.
उदा. Article 'the' और adjective के बाद noun की आवश्यकता होती है। 'success' एक noun है।
[RULE] Parts of Speech

---

[Q1169] She prefers coffee _____ tea in the morning.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) than
(b) to
(c) over
(d) from
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'prefer' is followed by the preposition 'to' when comparing two things, not 'than'.
उदा. 'Prefer' के साथ तुलना करने के लिए 'to' का प्रयोग होता है, 'than' का नहीं।
[RULE] Preposition with Adjectives/Verbs

---

[Q1170] The principal congratulated the students _____ their brilliant success in the board exams.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) on
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'congratulate' is correctly followed by the preposition 'on' an achievement or occasion.
उदा. किसी उपलब्धि पर बधाई देने के लिए 'congratulate' के बाद preposition 'on' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1171] He has been living in this city ___ five years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'for' to indicate duration when the starting time is not specified. उदा. पाँच वर्षों से यहाँ रह रहा है।
[RULE] Use of 'for' with duration

---

[Q1172] Please be careful ___ handling the fragile items.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) to
(c) with
(d) in
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Use 'with' to specify the manner of carefulness. उदा. नाज़ुक वस्तुओं को संभालते समय सावधान रहें।
[RULE] Preposition 'with' after 'be careful'

---

[Q1173] The company is known ___ providing excellent customer service.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) at
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Known for' indicates the reason for fame. उदा. कंपनी ग्राहक सेवा के लिए जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Collocation 'known for'

---

[Q1174] She has a talent ___ playing the violin.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) of
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Talents for' is the usual pattern. उदा. वह वायलिन बजाने में कुशल है।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'for'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Use 'talent for' to indicate aptitude

---

[Q1175] They decided ___ postponing the meeting until next week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) in
(d) without
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive after 'decided'. उदा. वे बैठक को अगले हफ़्ते तक स्थगित करने का निर्णय लेते हैं।
[RULE] Infinitive 'to' after verb 'decide'

---

[Q1176] The new policy will affect ___ the employees' morale.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the
(b) a
(c) an
(d) no
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Definite article used for a known group. उदा. नई नीति कर्मचारियों के मनोबल को प्रभावित करेगी।
[RULE] Definite article with specific noun phrase

---

[Q1177] He was praised ___ his honesty.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Praised for' indicates the reason for praise. उदा. उसके ईमानदारी के लिए प्रशंसा की गई।
[RULE] Collocation 'praised for'

---

[Q1178] She has been working here ___ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Since' indicates time from past to present. उदा. 2010 से यहाँ काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Use 'since' with starting point time

---

[Q1179] Please refrain ___ speaking loudly in the library.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) of
(c) to
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Refrain from' means to avoid. उदा. पुस्तकालय में ज़ोर से बात करने से परहेज़ करें।
[RULE] Collocation 'refrain from'

---

[Q1180] She has been _____ a professional dancer for 15 years.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) practiced
(b) practise
(c) practising
(d) practice
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Present participle is used after 'has been' to indicate continuous action. सही रूप: 'practising'.
उदा. वह 15 वर्षों से एक पेशेवर नर्तकी है।
[RULE] Present participle after 'has been'

---

[Q1181] The committee met _____ the suggest i on was rejected.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) after
(b) by
(c) although
(d) without
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use of preposition 'after' to indicate a sequence of events.
उदा. समिति ने सुझाव के अस्वीकृत होने के बाद बैठक की।
[RULE] Preposition 'after' for sequence

---

[Q1182] He has been living in Paris for three years, but he ______ had lived in London before.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) also
(b) only
(c) still
(d) yet
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adverb 'also' is used to add information.
उदा. उसने पेरिस में तीन साल रहे हैं, लेकिन वह लंदन में भी रहा है।
[RULE] Adverb 'also' for addition

---

[Q1183] The new policy will be implemented _____ the next fiscal year.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) by
(c) on
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'in' is used with a period or time duration.
उदा. नई नीति अगले वित्तीय वर्ष में लागू होगी।
[RULE] Preposition 'in' with time

---

[Q1184] I would have gone to the party, _____ I was not invited.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) but
(b) unless
(c) if
(d) though
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conjunction 'but' introduces a contrasting idea.
उदा. मैं पार्टी में जाऊंगा, लेकिन मुझे आमंत्रित नहीं किया गया।
[RULE] Conjunction 'but' for contrast

---

[Q1185] The child stared ____ the magician's trick.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) to
(c) for
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'at' is used with 'stare'.
उदा. बच्चा जादूगर के करतब पर नज़र डाल रहा था।
[RULE] Preposition 'at' with stare

---

[Q1186] The new software is ______ efficient than the previous version.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) more
(b) most
(c) much
(d) less
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Comparative adjective 'more' before adjective.
उदा. नया सॉफ्टवेयर पिछले संस्करण से अधिक कुशल है।
[RULE] Comparative 'more' + adjective

---

[Q1187] He was known _____ his generosity.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) to
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'for' is used after 'known' to indicate reason.
उदा. वह अपनी उदारता के लिए जाना जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition 'for' after 'known'

---

[Q1188] The book was written _____ a famous author.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) to
(c) from
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'by' indicates the author.
उदा. पुस्तक एक प्रसिद्ध लेखक द्वारा लिखी गई थी।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' for authorship

---

[Q1189] She has never been _____ the taste of fish.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) of
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition 'to' after 'been' in negative sense of experience.
उदा. उसने कभी मछली के स्वाद को नहीं चखा।
[RULE] Preposition 'to' after 'been' in negative experience

---

[Q1190] She has been ___ her work for the last three hours.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) doing
(b) done
(c) does
(d) do
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "has been" requires a present continuous form. Hence, the correct form is "doing".

इंग्लिश में "has been" के बाद वर्तमान निरंतर काल (present continuous) का प्रयोग होता है। इसलिए सही शब्द "doing" है।
[RULE] Present Continuous with "be + –ing"

---

[Q1191] The committee will convene ___ the after no on.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Specific times are expressed using the preposition "at". Therefore, "at the afternoon" is the correct choice.

विशिष्ट समय पर "at" का प्रयोग किया जाता है, अतः "at the afternoon" सही है।
[RULE] Preposition for time

---

[Q1192] He was appointed as the new manager, ___ of the company.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) to
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "in" is used to indicate a position within an organization. Thus, "in the company" is appropriate.

संगठन के भीतर स्थिति दर्शाने के लिए "in" का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition indicating position within an organization

---

[Q1193] The children were excited ___ the trip.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) about
(b) to
(c) for
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Excited" is followed by the preposition "about" when expressing feelings towards something. Hence, "about the trip" is correct.

"excited" के बाद भाव दिखाने के लिये "about" का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition after adjectives indicating feelings

---

[Q1194] She ___ her keys yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) lost
(b) lose
(c) losing
(d) loses
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past simple tense of the irregular verb "lose" is "lost". Therefore, the correct form is "lost".

अनियमित क्रिया "lose" का भूतकाल "lost" है।
[RULE] Simple Past of irregular verbs

---

[Q1195] The test will be held ___ 10 pm.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"At" is the correct preposition to denote a specific time. Thus, "at 10 pm" is the proper choice.

विशिष्ट समय के लिये "at" का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Preposition for specific time

---

[Q1196] The book was written ___ a young author.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) to
(c) for
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "by" indicates the agent who performed the action. Therefore, "by a young author" is correct.

"by" का प्रयोग कार्य करने वाले व्यक्ति को दर्शाने के लिये किया जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition indicating the agent

---

[Q1197] They had to ___ the door after the storm.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) close
(b) closed
(c) closing
(d) closes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb following the modal "had to" takes the base form. Hence, "close" is correct.

मॉडल "had to" के बाद क्रिया का मूल रूप प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Modal + base verb (imperative form)

---

[Q1198] The policy was drafted ___ a committee of experts.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) of
(c) for
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "by" is used to show the agent who performed an action. Thus, "by a committee of experts" is the correct choice.

"by" क्रिया करने वाले को दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Preposition indicating the agent of a passive construction

---

[Q1199] His performance was praised ___ his dedication.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) to
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "praised for" is the idiomatic expression used to indicate the reason for praise. Therefore, "for" is correct.

"praised for" ही सही वाक्यांश है।
[RULE] Preposition after the verb "praised" to indicate reason

---

[Q1200] Despite his best efforts, he could not _______ the target assigned to him.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) achieve
(b) attain
(c) accomplish
(d) access
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We 'achieve' a target, goal, or objective. 'Achieve' means to successfully bring about or reach a desired objective by effort.
उदा. लक्ष्य (target) या उद्देश्य को प्राप्त करने के लिए 'achieve' verb का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Collocation / Vocabulary

---

[Q1201] The manager asked the employees to _______ the project report before leaving the office.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) look up
(b) look over
(c) look into
(d) look after
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Look over' means to examine something quickly. The manager wants them to review or examine the report.
उदा. 'Look over' का अर्थ किसी दस्तावेज़ आदि का सरसरी तौर पर या सावधानी से निरीक्षण (examine) करना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1202] The museum displays a rare _______ of ancient coins from the Gupta period.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) group
(b) assembly
(c) collection
(d) crowd
[ANS] c
[SOL]
A set of objects of the same type collected over a period of time is called a 'collection' (especially coins, stamps, etc.).
उदा. पुरानी वस्तुओं, टिकटों या सिक्कों के समूह को 'collection' कहा जाता है।
[RULE] Noun Collocation / Vocabulary

---

[Q1203] Hardly had I stepped out of the house _______ it started raining heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before'). 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions

---

[Q1204] She is proficient _______ mathematics and solves complex equations with ease.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' is fixedly followed by the preposition 'in' to indicate skill in a particular subject or activity.
उदा. 'Proficient' (कुशल) शब्द के बाद हमेशा preposition 'in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1205] The government has taken strict measures to _______ the rising inflation in the country.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) curb
(b) boost
(c) spread
(d) expand
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Curb' means to restrain or control something undesirable like inflation or pollution.
उदा. महंगाई (inflation) या किसी बुरी चीज़ को नियंत्रित करने या रोकने के लिए 'curb' verb का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Usage

---

[Q1206] Had the driver been alert, the fatal accident _______ avoided.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) can be
(b) could be
(c) could have been
(d) will have been
[ANS] c
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure: 'If + past perfect (had been), subject + could/would/might + have + V3'.
उदा. यह Third Conditional वाक्य है, जिसमें past की असंभव घटना के परिणाम के लिए 'could have + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q1207] The committee members could not reach a consensus because their opinions were _______.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) similar
(b) identical
(c) divergent
(d) consistent
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Divergent' means tending to be different or develop in different directions. If opinions differ, a consensus cannot be reached.
उदा. 'Divergent' का अर्थ भिन्न या अलग-अलग होना है। अलग-अलग विचार होने के कारण सहमति (consensus) नहीं बन सकी।
[RULE] Vocabulary / Contextual Meaning

---

[Q1208] Neither the teacher nor the students _______ present in the class room yesterday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it. Here, 'students' is plural, so 'were' is used.
उदा. 'Neither... nor' से जुड़े वाक्यों में verb अपने सबसे पास वाले subject के अनुसार आती है। 'students' plural है, अतः 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1209] The teacher advised the students to listen _______ to what the guest speaker had to say.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) attentive
(b) attention
(c) attentively
(d) attentiveness
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The verb 'listen' needs to be modified by an adverb ('attentively'), not an adjective.
उदा. Verb 'listen' की विशेषता बताने के लिए adverb ('attentively') का प्रयोग किया जाएगा, adjective का नहीं।
[RULE] Parts of Speech (Adverb vs Adjective)

---

[Q1210] The government has taken strict measures to curb the rising ________ of inflation.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) rate
(b) pace
(c) speed
(d) tempo
[ANS] a
[SOL]
With inflation, we use the word 'rate' to denote the speed or frequency at which prices rise.
उदा. महंगाई (inflation) बढ़ने की गति या दर को दर्शाने के लिए 'rate' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Collocation with Inflation

---

[Q1211] She was confident ________ her success in the competitive examination.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) about
(c) in
(d) of
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The adjective 'confident' is followed by the preposition 'of' when expressing certainty about something.
उदा. 'Confident' विशेषण के बाद निश्चितता व्यक्त करने के लिए पूर्वसर्ग (preposition) 'of' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Confident of)

---

[Q1212] The manager asked the employees to ________ their differences and work together.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) put aside
(b) put off
(c) put down
(d) put across
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Put aside' means to ignore or forget something (usually a difference or disagreement) for a particular purpose.
उदा. 'Put aside' एक phrasal verb है जिसका अर्थ किसी मतभेद को नजरअंदाज करना या भुलाना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verb (Put aside)

---

[Q1213] Neither the teacher nor the students ________ present in the class today.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) is
(b) are
(c) has
(d) was
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of Subject-Verb Agreement, when subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it. Here, 'students' is plural, so 'are' is used.
उदा. जब दो सब्जेक्ट्स 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो क्रिया (verb) अपने सबसे पास वाले सब्जेक्ट के अनुसार आती है। यहाँ 'students' बहुवचन है, इसलिए 'are' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1214] He is addicted ________ playing online games all night.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) with
(b) to
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective/participle 'addicted' always takes the preposition 'to' after it.
उदा. 'Addicted' शब्द के साथ हमेशा निश्चित पूर्वसर्ग 'to' का प्रयोग होता है (जैसे- addicted to alcohol/games)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Addicted to)

---

[Q1215] The arduous journey through the mountains left the travellers completely ________.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) energetic
(b) exhausted
(c) enthusiastic
(d) elated
[ANS] b
[SOL]
An arduous (very difficult) journey would logically leave travellers feeling completely tired or 'exhausted'.
उदा. एक कठिन (arduous) यात्रा यात्रियों को पूरी तरह से थका देगी, इसलिए यहाँ 'exhausted' (थका हुआ) सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Contextual Vocabulary

---

[Q1216] Had the captain played well, the team ________ the match.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) will win
(b) would win
(c) would have won
(d) had won
[ANS] c
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence structure: 'Had + subject + V3' takes 'would have + V3' in the main clause.
उदा. यह Third Conditional वाक्य है। यदि शर्त वाले क्लॉज़ में 'Had + V3' हो, तो परिणाम वाले क्लॉज़ में 'would have + V3' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q1217] The committee will ________ its report by the end of next week.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) submit
(b) submitting
(c) submitted
(d) submission
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Modal verbs like 'will' are always followed by the base form of the verb (V1).
उदा. मॉडल वर्ब 'will' के बाद हमेशा वर्ब की पहली फॉर्म (V1) का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Modals + V1

---

[Q1218] It is high time you ________ your career seriously.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) start
(b) started
(c) have started
(d) will start
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Phrases like 'It is time', 'It is high time', etc., when followed by a subject, always take the Simple Past tense (V2).
उदा. यदि 'It is high time' के बाद कोई सब्जेक्ट दिया गया हो, तो वाक्य के अगले भाग में Past Indefinite (V2) का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] It is high time + Simple Past

---

[Q1219] She is proficient ________ painting and classical music.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'proficient' (skilled) is followed by the preposition 'in'.
उदा. किसी कला या कार्य में कुशल होने के लिए प्रयुक्त शब्द 'proficient' के साथ पूर्वसर्ग 'in' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Proficient in)

---

[Q1220] The manager was very “_____” about the project dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) concerned
(b) concern
(c) concernedly
(d) concernment
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective "concerned" correctly describes a person's feeling about something.
उदा. वह परियोजना की समय सीमा को लेकर चिंतित थी।
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1221] She has been working here for “_____” years.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) five
(b) five years
(c) five year
(d) five years'
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When indicating duration, the noun "years" can be omitted after the number.
उदा. वह यहाँ पाँच साल से काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Number + noun omission for duration

---

[Q1222] He was “_____” by the sudden noise.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) startled
(b) startle
(c) startledness
(d) startling
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Startled" is the past participle used as an adjective.
उदा. अचानक शोर से वह चौंक गया।
[RULE] Past participle as adjective

---

[Q1223] The company is “_____” to improve employee welfare.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) committed
(b) commit
(c) commitment
(d) Tenacious
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Committed" is the adjective form meaning dedicated.
उदा. कंपनी कर्मचारी कल्याण को बेहतर बनाने के लिए प्रतिबद्ध है।
[RULE] Adjective from verb

---

[Q1224] The committee will meet “_____” Thursday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"On" is the correct preposition for days of the week.
उदा. समिति गुरुवार को मिलेगी।
[RULE] Preposition for days

---

[Q1225] She was “_____” to the news of the promotion.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) elated
(b) elate
(c) elation
(d) elatedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Elated" is the adjective meaning very happy.
उदा. उसे पदोन्नति की खबर से बहुत खुशी हुई।
[RULE] Adjective from verb

---

[Q1226] The new policy will be implemented “_____” the end of the month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By" indicates the latest time or deadline.
उदा. नई नीति महीने के अंत तक लागू की जाएगी।
[RULE] Preposition for deadlines

---

[Q1227] They have been studying French for “_____” months.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ten
(b) ten months
(c) ten month
(d) ten months'
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The number alone can indicate duration; the noun is optional.
उदा. उन्होंने दस महीने से फ्रेंच का अध्ययन किया है।
[RULE] Number alone for duration

---

[Q1228] The report was “_____” by the auditors.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) examined
(b) examining
(c) examination
(d) examine
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Examined" is the past participle used as an adjective.
उदा. रिपोर्ट का ऑडिटरों द्वारा निरीक्षण किया गया।
[RULE] Past participle as adjective

---

[Q1229] He was praised “_____” his efforts.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"For" is the correct preposition to indicate the reason for praise.
उदा. उसके प्रयासों के लिए उसे प्रशंसा मिली।
[RULE] Preposition for reason

---

[Q1230] They ____ the train to arrive on time.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) hoped that
(b) wanted that
(c) expected that
(d) planned that
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Use the past tense of *expect* to express a prediction that the train would reach on time.
उदा. उन्होंने उम्मीद की कि ट्रेन समय पर पहुंचेगी।
[RULE] Past tense of expect

---

[Q1231] She has been studying English ____ 2012.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *since* with a specific point in time. 2012 is a point, so *since 2012*.
उदा. वह 2012 से अंग्रेज़ी पढ़ रही है।
[RULE] Since vs. for

---

[Q1232] He was known for his ____ generosity.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) great
(b) generous
(c) generously
(d) generouslyness
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After *for* we use an adjective, not an adverb. So *generous*.
उदा. वह अपनी उदारता के लिए जाना जाता है।
[RULE] Adj after for

---

[Q1233] The project will be completed ____ the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) within
(b) in
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*Within* the deadline means before it ends.
उदा. परियोजना समय सीमा के भीतर पूरा हो जाएगी।
[RULE] Preposition with deadline

---

[Q1234] If you ____ the instructions carefully, you will not make mistakes.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) followed
(c) following
(d) to follow
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a conditional clause we use the simple present: *follow*. 
उदा. यदि आप निर्देशों का सावधानी से पालन करते हैं, तो आप गलतियाँ नहीं करेंगे।
[RULE] Simple present in if‑clauses

---

[Q1235] The new policy aims ____ the gap between the rich and the poor.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to close
(b) closing
(c) closed
(d) to closing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *to* + infinitive after *aims*.
उदा. नई नीति अमीर और गरीब के बीच की खाई को बंद करने का लक्ष्य रखती है।
[RULE] Infinitive after aims

---

[Q1236] I am looking forward to ____ you again.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) see
(b) seeing
(c) to see
(d) seen
[ANS] b
[SOL]
*Looking forward to* is followed by a gerund.
उदा. मैं फिर से आपसे मिलने का इंतज़ार कर रहा हूँ।
[RULE] Gerund after looking forward to

---

[Q1237] She has a talent ____ playing the violin.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) by
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
*Talent for* indicates a skill.
उदा. उसे वायलिन बजाने में कौशल है।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'for'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Talent for

---

[Q1238] The company was established ____ 1995.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*In* a year. 1995 is a year.
उदा. कंपनी की स्थापना 1995 में हुई थी।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'in'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] In a year

---

[Q1239] His speech was ____ and filled with emotion.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) moving
(b) moved
(c) movedly
(d) movedness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the adjective *moving* to describe the speech.
उदा. उसका भाषण भावनाओं से भरा हुआ था।
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1240] She has been working on this project ________ three months.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'For' is used for a period of time (duration like three months). 'Since' is used for a point of time.
उदा. अवधि (duration) जैसे 'three months' के साथ 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, जबकि निश्चित समय (point of time) के लिए 'since' आता है।
[RULE] Preposition of Time (For vs Since)

---

[Q1241] Had you informed me earlier, I ________ attended the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) would have
(b) will have
(c) would
(d) had
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Third conditional structure: If + past perfect (Had + subject + V3), subject + would have + V3.
उदा. यह Third Conditional वाक्य है। यदि शर्त वाले भाग में 'Had + V3' हो, तो परिणाम वाले क्लॉज में 'would have + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 3)

---

[Q1242] Hardly had I stepped out of the house ________ it started raining.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) when
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before'), whereas 'No sooner' is followed by 'than'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है, जबकि 'No sooner' के साथ 'than' आता है।
[RULE] Correlative Conjunctions

---

[Q1243] He is proficient ________ English and can translate texts easily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Fixed preposition: 'Proficient in' means skilled or competent in a particular subject or activity.
उदा. 'Proficient' शब्द के बाद हमेशा निश्चित प्रीपोजिशन 'in' का प्रयोग होता है जिसका अर्थ किसी कार्य में निपुण होना है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1244] The manager decided to ________ the meeting due to unavoidable circumstances.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) call off
(b) call out
(c) call on
(d) call up
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Phrasal verb 'call off' means to cancel something.
