उदा. 'Call off' का अर्थ किसी कार्यक्रम या मीटिंग को रद्द (cancel) करना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1245] Rohan is junior ________ me in the corporate hierarchy.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) than
(b) to
(c) from
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Latin comparative adjectives ending in '-or' (junior, senior, superior, inferior, prior) take 'to' instead of 'than'.
उदा. 'junior', 'senior', 'superior' आदि शब्दों के बाद तुलना के लिए 'than' के स्थान पर 'to' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Adjectives with Prepositions

---

[Q1246] The government has taken strict measures to ________ inflation in the country.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) curb
(b) boost
(c) elevate
(d) promote
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Vocabulary in context: 'Curb' means to restrain or keep in check (control inflation).
उदा. वाक्य के संदर्भ के अनुसार, महंगाई (inflation) को नियंत्रित करने के लिए 'curb' (नियंत्रित करना/रोकना) सबसे उपयुक्त शब्द है।
[RULE] Vocabulary and Contextual Usage

---

[Q1247] One of the major problems facing our cities ________ air pollution.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) are
(b) is
(c) were
(d) have been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Subject-Verb Agreement: The structure 'One of + plural noun' takes a singular verb because the main subject is 'One'.
उदा. 'One of + plural noun' के बाद आने वाली वर्ब हमेशा singular होती है क्योंकि मुख्य सब्जेक्ट 'One' (एकवचन) होता है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1248] She has been working on this complex project ________ the last three years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'for' to denote a period of time (duration) like 'three years'. 'Since' is used for a point of time.
उदा. निश्चित समय (point of time) के लिए 'since' और समय की अवधि (period of time) के लिए 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है। यहाँ 'three years' अवधि है, इसलिए 'for' आएगा।
[RULE] Preposition of Time (For vs Since)

---

[Q1249] The committee reached a consensus ________ the new tax policy after hours of debate.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) with
(d) over
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun 'consensus' is typically followed by the preposition 'on' regarding a topic or policy.
उदा. 'consensus' (सहमति) शब्द के बाद आमतौर पर किसी विषय या नीति पर 'on' प्रीपोजिशन का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1250] If the government ________ strict measures, corruption could be curbed effectively.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) takes
(b) took
(c) had taken
(d) is taking
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In unreal conditional sentences of the type 'If + Past Subjunctive (V2), would/could/might + V1', the past tense form 'took' is correct.
उदा. काल्पनिक शर्त वाले वाक्यों में 'If + V2' के साथ 'could/would + V1' आता है, इसलिए यहाँ 'took' का प्रयोग होगा।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Type 2)

---

[Q1251] His novel was so ________ that readers could not put it down until the very last page.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) tedious
(b) riveting
(c) monotonous
(d) prosaic
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Riveting' means completely engrossing or compelling, which fits the context that readers couldn't put the book down.
उदा. 'Riveting' का अर्थ है अत्यंत आकर्षक या मनमोहक, जिससे पाठक किताब को अंत तक छोड़ नहीं पाए। अन्य विकल्प नकारात्मक अर्थ वाले हैं।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Contextual Usage

---

[Q1252] The manager asked me ________ I intended to submit the final report.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) that
(b) when
(c) if
(d) what
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In indirect speech inquiring about time, the question word 'when' acts as a conjunction without changing its interrogative sense of time.
उदा. समय के संदर्भ में अप्रत्यक्ष प्रश्न (Indirect speech) पूछते समय कंजंक्शन के रूप में 'when' का प्रयोग उपयुक्त है।
[RULE] Indirect Speech & Conjunctions

---

[Q1253] The athlete was disqualified because he failed to abide ________ the anti-doping rules.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) with
(b) by
(c) to
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrasal verb 'abide by' means to accept or act in accordance with a rule, decision, or recommendation.
उदा. 'Abide by' एक निश्चित फ्रेजल वर्ब है जिसका अर्थ नियमों या आदेशों का पालन करना होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs & Prepositions

---

[Q1254] Neither the teacher nor the students ________ present in the class room when the inspection happened.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
According to the rule of proximity in subject-verb agreement, when subjects are joined by 'neither...nor', the verb agrees with the closer subject ('students', which is plural).
उदा. 'Neither...nor' से जुड़े वाक्यों में क्रिया अपने सबसे पास वाले कर्ता (subject) के अनुसार आती है। यहाँ 'students' बहुवचन है, अतः 'were' आएगा।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1255] Despite his immense wealth, he leads a very ________ lifestyle, avoiding all luxuries.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) lavish
(b) austere
(c) pompous
(d) extravagant
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Austere' means severe or strict in manner, attitude, or appearance, specifically living a simple life without comforts/luxuries. This contrasts with 'immense wealth'.
उदा. 'Austere' का अर्थ सादा और संयमी जीवन जीने वाला होता है, जो अपार धन होने के बावजूद विलासिता से दूर रहने के विरोधाभास को दर्शाता है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Contrast Context

---

[Q1256] The startup aims to ________ new talent from premier engineering institutes this campus season.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) absorb
(b) recruit
(c) adopt
(d) Eloquent
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Recruit' is the standard formal verb used for hiring or enlisting new employees or personnel into an organization.
उदा. किसी संस्थान या कंपनी द्वारा नए कर्मचारियों या प्रतिभाओं को काम पर रखने के लिए 'recruit' शब्द का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Collocation & Vocabulary

---

[Q1257] The manager insisted ______ the completion of the report by evening.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) upon
(c) for
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'insist' is followed by the fixed preposition 'on'.
उदा. 'Insist' शब्द के बाद हमेशा फिक्स प्रीपोजिशन 'on' का प्रयोग किया जाता है (insist on doing something)।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1258] If I ______ the king of the country, I would eradicate poverty.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) am
(d) be
[ANS] b
[SOL]
In unreal conditional sentences expressing a wish or hypothetical situation, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक वाक्य (Hypothetical sentence) जिसमें इच्छा प्रकट की जाए, वहाँ सभी विषयों के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग होता है, भले ही subject singular हो।
[RULE] Subjunctive Mood

---

[Q1259] His novel is notable ______ its vivid description of rural life.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) by
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Notable for' means worthy of attention because of a specified characteristic.
उदा. किसी विशिष्ट गुण के लिए प्रसिद्ध या उल्लेखनीय होने के लिए 'notable for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition (Notable for)

---

[Q1260] Neither the teacher nor the students ______ present in the class today.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) has
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When two subjects are joined by 'neither... nor', the verb agrees with the subject closest to it ('students', which is plural).
उदा. जब दो subject 'neither... nor' से जुड़े हों, तो verb अपने सबसे करीब वाले subject (यहाँ 'students' जो कि plural है) के अनुसार आती है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1261] Despite working hard, he could not ______ the examination.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) clear
(b) pass through
(c) succeed
(d) qualify into
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use 'clear an exam' or 'pass an exam'. 'Pass through' has a different meaning.
उदा. परीक्षा उत्तीर्ण करने के लिए 'clear' या 'pass' का प्रयोग होता है, 'pass through' का अर्थ किसी स्थान से गुजरना होता है।
[RULE] Collocations / Phrasal Usage

---

[Q1262] The committee members were divided in ______ opinions regarding the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) its
(b) their
(c) his
(d) her
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a collective noun like 'committee' is divided in opinion, it takes plural pronouns and verbs.
उदा. जब collective noun (जैसे committee) में मतभेद हो और सदस्यों की अलग-अलग राय हो, तो plural pronoun 'their' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Pronoun Agreement with Collective Nouns

---

[Q1263] She is superior ______ me in experience and technical skills.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) than
(b) to
(c) from
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Adjectives ending in '-or' (like superior, junior, senior, inferior) take the preposition 'to' instead of 'than'.
उदा. जिन विशेषق (Adjectives) के अंत में '-or' आता है (जैसे superior, junior, senior), उनके बाद 'than' की बजाय 'to' प्रीपोजिशन का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Adjectives taking 'To' instead of 'Than'

---

[Q1264] She has been working on this project ___ three months.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'for' with a period of time (three months) in present perfect continuous tense.
उदा. अवधि (duration) के लिए 'for' का प्रयोग किया जाता है, जैसे 'three months'।
[RULE] Preposition of Time (For vs Since)

---

[Q1265] Hardly had he reached the station ___ the train left.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) before
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction for 'Hardly' or 'Scarcely' is always 'when'.
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा 'when' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conjunction Pairs

---

[Q1266] Neither of the two candidates ___ qualified for the post.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) have
(b) has
(c) are
(d) were
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Neither of' takes a singular verb because it refers to individuals separately.
उदा. 'Neither of' के बाद हमेशा एकवचन (singular verb) यानी 'has' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Subject-Verb Agreement

---

[Q1267] The teacher advised the students to refrain ___ smoking.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) from
(b) at
(c) in
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb 'refrain' is always followed by the preposition 'from'.
उदा. 'Refrain' शब्द के बाद हमेशा 'from' प्रीपोजिशन का उपयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Fixed Preposition

---

[Q1268] If I ___ a bird, I would fly across the country.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) am
(b) was
(c) were
(d) be
[ANS] c
[SOL]
For unreal conditional sentences expressing a wish, 'were' is used with all subjects.
उदा. काल्पनिक इच्छा (hypothetical condition) को व्यक्त करने के लिए सभी विषयों के साथ 'were' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences (Subjunctive Mood)

---

[Q1269] The manager asked him to explain the reason ___ he was late.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) why
(b) because
(c) that
(d) how
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'The reason' is followed by 'why', not by 'because'.
उदा. 'The reason' के बाद कारण बताने के लिए 'why' का प्रयोग होता है, न कि 'because' का।
[RULE] Relative Adverbs / Superfluous Expressions

---

[Q1270] Despite working hard, he could not achieve ___ marks to pass the exam.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) few
(b) little
(c) enough
(d) many
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Enough' is used to indicate the required quantity or degree before nouns.
उदा. पर्याप्त मात्रा या आवश्यकता दर्शाने के लिए 'enough' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Determiners and Quantifiers

---

[Q1271] His novel was so captivating that it kept me ___ till midnight.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) awake
(b) awaken
(c) woke
(d) waking
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Keep + object + adjective' structure is used here. 'Awake' acts as a predicative adjective.
उदा. यहाँ 'awake' एक विशेषण (adjective) के रूप में प्रयुक्त हुआ है जिसका अर्थ 'जागते हुए' है।
[RULE] Adjectives and Causative Verbs

---

[Q1272] The police are looking ___ the matter to find the culprit.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) into
(b) after
(c) at
(d) up
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Phrasal verb 'look into' means to investigate.
उदा. 'Look into' एक phrasal verb है जिसका अर्थ 'जांच करना' (investigate) होता है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1273] He is senior ___ me by two years in the department.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) than
(b) to
(c) from
(d) then
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Adjectives ending in '-ior' (senior, junior, superior, inferior) take 'to' instead of 'than'.
उदा. 'Senior', 'junior', आदि शब्दों के बाद तुलना के लिए 'than' की बजाय 'to' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Adjectives with Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1274] She is known ________ her punctuality.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **for** is used after *known* to indicate the reason or attribute. 
उदाहरण: वह अपनी समयबद्धता के लिए जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Preposition after 'known'

---

[Q1275] He has been working ________ the company for five years.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When referring to being employed in an organization, use **in**. 
उदाहरण: वह पाँच साल से कंपनी में काम कर रहा है।
[RULE] Preposition 'in' with employment

---

[Q1276] The book was written ________ a famous author.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) from
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use **by** after *written* to indicate the author. 
उदाहरण: पुस्तक एक प्रसिद्ध लेखक द्वारा लिखी गई है।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' after 'written'

---

[Q1277] She is ________ doctor in the city hospital.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no article
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Article a is used before singular countable noun starting with consonant sound.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'a'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Indefinite Article (A vs An)

---

[Q1278] I will meet you ________ 5 pm.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
For specific times, the preposition **at** is used. 
उदाहरण: मैं तुम्हें पाँच बजे मिलूँगा।
[RULE] Preposition 'at' for time

---

[Q1279] They have been living ________ this city since 2010.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) for
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Living in a place uses **in**. 
उदाहरण: वे इस शहर में 2010 से रह रहे हैं।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'in'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition 'in' with residence

---

[Q1280] The train will arrive ________ the platform.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) by
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The preposition **on** is used to indicate a location on a surface. 
उदाहरण: ट्रेन प्लेटफार्म पर पहुंचेगी।
[RULE] Preposition 'on' for surface

---

[Q1281] He was praised ________ his courage.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After *praised*, the preposition **for** expresses the reason. 
उदाहरण: उसे उसकी बहादुरी के लिए प्रशंसा मिली।
[RULE] Preposition 'for' after 'praised'

---

[Q1282] She is ________ the best student in the class.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the
(b) a
(c) an
(d) this
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The definite article **the** is used for a specific noun. 
उदाहरण: वह कक्षा की सबसे अच्छी छात्रा है।
[RULE] Definite article 'the'

---

[Q1283] The film was produced ________ the renowned studio.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **by** indicates the producer. 
उदाहरण: फिल्म एक प्रसिद्ध स्टूडियो द्वारा बनाई गई थी।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' with production

---

[Q1284] If he ________ early, he could have caught the bus.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) arrives
(b) arrived
(c) arriving
(d) arrive
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The past tense **arrived** is required in the past‑perfect conditional. 
उदाहरण: यदि वह जल्दी पहुँचता, तो वह बस पकड़ सकता था।
[RULE] Past tense in past‑perfect conditional

---

[Q1285] He has been working here __ 2005.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "since" after a starting point in time.
उदाहरण: "He has been working here since 2005."

Rule: "since" indicates the starting point of an action that continues to the present.
[RULE] Preposition for starting point

---

[Q1286] We cannot accept this proposal __ the fact that it is flawed.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) despite
(b) because of
(c) with
(d) in spite of
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Because of" introduces a reason or cause.
उदाहरण: "We cannot accept this proposal because of its flaws."

Rule: Use "because of" to give a reason or cause for a statement.
[RULE] Preposition for cause

---

[Q1287] The manager asked me to submit the report __ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) on
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By" is used to indicate a deadline.
उदाहरण: "Submit the report by Friday."

Rule: Use "by" to specify the latest time by which something must be done.
[RULE] Preposition for deadline

---

[Q1288] She is known __ her excellent cooking skills.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) in
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"For" expresses the reason or quality for which someone is known.
उदाहरण: "She is known for her excellent cooking skills."

Rule: Use "for" after "known" to specify the attribute.
[RULE] Preposition after known

---

[Q1289] The company decided to reduce its expenses __ the economic down turn.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) during
(b) against
(c) to
(d) in
[ANS] d
[SOL]
"In" is used to denote a period of time such as a downturn.
उदाहरण: "…expenses in the economic downturn."

Rule: Use "in" to refer to a particular time period.
[RULE] Preposition for time period

---

[Q1290] The student was praised __ his dedication to the project.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"For" indicates the reason for praise.
उदाहरण: "Praised for his dedication."

Rule: Use "for" after praise to specify the quality being appreciated.
[RULE] Preposition after praise

---

[Q1291] Please submit the application __ the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) on
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By" sets a deadline.
उदाहरण: "Submit the application by the deadline."

Rule: Use "by" to indicate the latest time for an action.
[RULE] Preposition for deadline

---

[Q1292] The judge ruled the evidence __ admissible.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) not
(b) to
(c) is
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Not" negates the statement.
उदाहरण: "The evidence is not admissible."

Rule: Use "not" to negate an adjective or verb phrase.
[RULE] Negation with not

---

[Q1293] The new policy will be effective __ 1st January.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) on
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"On" is used with specific dates.
उदाहरण: "Effective on 1st January."

Rule: Use "on" when referring to a specific day.
[RULE] Preposition for specific date

---

[Q1294] They arrived __ the train station just in time.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"At" indicates a specific location.
उदाहरण: "They arrived at the train station."

Rule: Use "at" for points or specific locations.
[RULE] Preposition for location

---

[Q1295] She has been studying Italian __ three years.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) by
(d) from
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"For" is used to denote duration.
उदाहरण: "She has been studying Italian for three years."

Rule: Use "for" for the length of time an activity continues.
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q1296] The manager insisted ___ checking all the financial documents before signing the deal.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) with
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb 'insist' is always followed by the fixed preposition 'on'.
उदा. 'Insist' verb के साथ हमेशा fixed preposition 'on' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositions

---

[Q1297] Had the police arrived on time, the jeweler ___ saved from the robbery.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) would be
(b) would have been
(c) will be
(d) had been
[ANS] b
[SOL]
This is a third conditional sentence in passive voice: 'If + had + V3, would + have + been + V3'.
उदा. यह Third Conditional का passive structure है जिसमें 'would have been + V3' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Conditional Sentences

---

[Q1298] The minister appealed to the public to maintain peace and not to ___ to rumors.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) give in
(b) give up
(c) give away
(d) give off
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Give in' means to surrender or yield to something. Here, it means not surrendering to rumors.
उदा. 'Give in' का अर्थ हार मानना या झुकना होता है। यहाँ अफवाहों के आगे न झुकने की बात की गई है।
[RULE] Phrasal Verbs

---

[Q1299] Hardly had I stepped out of the house ___ it started pouring heavily.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) when
(b) than
(c) then
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correlative conjunction 'Hardly/Scarcely' is always followed by 'when' (or 'before').
उदा. 'Hardly' या 'Scarcely' के साथ हमेशा conjunction 'when' का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Conjunctions and Correlatives

---

[Q1300] The government has taken several steps to ___ the economic growth of the country.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Hard]
(a) impede
(b) bolster
(c) hinder
(d) diminish
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Bolster' means to support or strengthen. The context requires a positive word for economic growth.
उदा. 'Bolster' का अर्थ मजबूत करना या समर्थन देना है। आर्थिक वृद्धि के संदर्भ में यह सही शब्द है।
[RULE] Vocabulary & Contextual Usage

---

[Q1301] She speaks English with such fluency that every one mistakes her ___ a native speaker.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) as
(b) for
(c) like
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase is 'to mistake someone for someone/something', meaning to wrongly identify.
उदा. 'Mistake someone for' एक fixed phrase है जिसका अर्थ किसी को दूसरा समझ लेना होता है।
[RULE] Fixed Prepositional Phrases

---

[Q1302] Rajesh is accustomed ___ working for long hours without taking a break.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2021 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) to
(b) with
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective phrase 'accustomed' is followed by 'to' and then a gerund (V1+ing).
उदा. 'Accustomed' के बाद preposition 'to' और उसके बाद gerund का प्रयोग किया जाता है।
[RULE] Verbs/Adjectives followed by Gerunds

[Q1303] She has been studying ___ 9 AM for the exam.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) from
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'since' to indicate the starting point of an action that continues up to now.
उदा. वह 9 बजे से पढ़ रही है।
[RULE] Since for starting point

[Q1304] He was unaware ___ what the consequences would be.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) for
(c) to
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Unaware of' means lacking knowledge or awareness about something.
उदा. उसे उसके परिणामों के बारे में पता नहीं था।
[RULE] Unaware + of

(a) when
(b) if

'After' is used to indicate that one action follows another.

(a) in

[ANS] a

'In' is used to denote a period of time.

[Q1307] She offered to help ___ he needed assistance.

(a) if
(b) when
(c) whether
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'If' introduces a conditional clause.

[Q1353] The book is ______ the topic.

(a) relevant to
(b) relevant on
(c) relevant for
(d) relevant with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The adjective 'relevant' is followed by the preposition 'to' when indicating a subject.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) left
(b) leaving
(c) leaves

[ANS] a

[SOL]
Use the present participle form of the verb after the continuous verb "has been".
उदाहरण: She has been working on the project.
[RULE] Present participle after continuous tense

---

[Q1406] The train was expected to arrive ___________ 10 a.m.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the preposition "at" to indicate a specific time.
उदाहरण: The train will arrive at 10 a.m.
[RULE] Preposition for time (at)

---

[Q1407] I can’t understand his argument because he always speaks ___________.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) slowly
(b) slower
(c) slow
(d) the slowest
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adverbial form of the adjective "slow" is "slowly".
उदाहरण: He speaks slowly to emphasize his points.
[RULE] Adverbial form of adjectives

---

[Q1408] The company has to ___________ its employees with the new policies.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) inform
(b) informs
(c) informed
(d) informing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the base form of the verb after the modal "has to".
उदाहरण: The company must inform all staff about changes.
[RULE] Base verb after modal + to

---

[Q1409] He was found guilty of ___________ his sister's diary.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) stealing
(b) theft
(c) stole
(d) stolen
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Stealing" is the gerund form used after the verb "of".
उदाहरण: She was arrested for stealing the diary.
[RULE] Gerund after preposition

---

[Q1410] We need to finish the report ___________ Monday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) on
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "by" to indicate a deadline.
उदाहरण: Submit the report by Monday.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'by'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition for deadline (by)

---

[Q1411] The new policy will ___________ all employees from the end of this month.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) apply
(b) applies
(c) applied
(d) applying
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Apply" is the base form after the modal "will".
उदाहरण: The new rule will apply to everyone.
[RULE] Base verb after modal + to

---

[Q1412] She has been ___________ the book since last week.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reading
(b) read
(c) readed
(d) reads
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the present participle after "has been" for continuous action.
उदाहरण: She has been reading for hours.
[RULE] Present participle after continuous tense

---

[Q1413] The children were playing ___________ the garden.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "in" for indoor or enclosed space.
उदाहरण: They played in the garden all afternoon.
[RULE] Preposition for location (in)

---

[Q1414] He will be promoted once he ___________ the required qualifications.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) obtains
(b) obtained
(c) obtaining
(d) obtain
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Obtains" is the base form after the conditional "once".
उदाहरण: He will succeed once he obtains the certificate.
[RULE] Base verb after conditional

---

[Q1415] The meeting was postponed __ the unexpected resignation of the direct or.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) due to
(b) in spite of
(c) on account of
(d) at the expense of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'due to' to express cause.
उदाहरण: बैठक अप्रत्याशित इस्तीफे के कारण स्थगित कर दी गई।
[RULE] Preposition: due to

---

[Q1416] He was __ a decision that would alter the course of his career.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) making
(b) to make
(c) made
(d) making of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The present participle 'making' follows the verb 'was' in the past continuous tense.
उदाहरण: उसने अपने करियर का मार्ग बदलने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Past continuous with present participle

---

[Q1417] She has always been __ an inspiration to young artists.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a source of
(b) a source for
(c) a source in
(d) a source at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use 'source of' to indicate origin.
उदाहरण: वह युवा कलाकारों के लिए प्रेरणा का स्रोत रही हैं।
[RULE] Preposition: source of

---

[Q1418] The committee decided to postpone the event __ the lack of funding.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) as a result of
(b) because of
(c) on account of
(d) due to
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Both 'on account of' and 'due to' express cause, but the sentence prefers 'on account of'.
उदाहरण: समिति ने धन की कमी के कारण कार्यक्रम स्थगित करने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Preposition: on account of

---

[Q1419] The new policy aims __ transparency in all government transactions.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to promote
(b) promoting
(c) promoted
(d) promotes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive 'to promote' follows the noun 'aims'.
उदाहरण: नई नीति सभी सरकारी लेनदेन में पारदर्शिता को बढ़ावा देने का लक्ष्य रखती है।
[RULE] Infinitive after noun 'aims'

---

[Q1420] He was known for his __ sense of humor.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rich
(b) richer
(c) richest
(d) richly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Rich' is the correct adjective; 'richly' is an adverb and cannot modify 'sense'.
उदाहरण: वह अपने समृद्ध हास्य के लिए जाना जाता था।
[RULE] Adjective vs. adverb

---

[Q1421] The scientists __ their findings at the annual conference.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) presented
(b) present
(c) presenting
(d) to present
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past simple tense 'presented' matches the past event.
उदाहरण: वैज्ञानिकों ने अपनी खोजों को वार्षिक सम्मेलन में प्रस्तुत किया।
[RULE] Past simple tense

---

[Q1422] Despite __ a long journey, she arrived on time.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) having taken
(b) to have taken
(c) to take
(d) taken
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The perfect participle 'having taken' indicates a completed action before another action.
उदाहरण: लंबी यात्रा करने के बावजूद, वह समय पर पहुंची।
[RULE] Perfect participle 'having + past participle'

---

[Q1423] The report highlights several __ areas that require immediate attention.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) critical
(b) critically
(c) criticism
(d) critic
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Critical' is the adjective needed here; 'critically' is an adverb.
उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट ने कई महत्वपूर्ण क्षेत्रों को उजागर किया जिन्हें तुरंत ध्यान देने की आवश्यकता है।
[RULE] Adjective vs. adverb

---

[Q1424] She has been working as an engineer __ five years now.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'since' to indicate the starting point of an action that continues to the present.
उदाहरण: वह पांच साल से इंजीनियर के रूप में काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Since vs. for

---

[Q1425] The company was praised for its __ commitment to sustainability.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) unwavering
(b) unwaveringly
(c) unwaveringness
(d) Uncanny
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Unwavering' is the adjective describing the commitment.
उदाहरण: कंपनी को स्थिरता के प्रति उसकी अडिग प्रतिबद्धता के लिए प्रशंसा मिली।
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1426] She has been working ___ this project for over a year.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When referring to a project or task, the preposition "on" is used. 
उदाहरण: "She has been working on this project for over a year."
[RULE] Preposition of Work/Task

---

[Q1427] The committee will convene ___ Wednesday evening to discuss the budget.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) in
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
We use "at" for specific times. 
उदाहरण: "The committee will convene at Wednesday evening."
[RULE] Preposition of Time

---

[Q1428] He was praised ___ his dedication during the crisis.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) to
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "for" is used to indicate the reason or cause of praise. 
उदाहरण: "He was praised for his dedication."
[RULE] Preposition of Reason

---

[Q1429] The new policy aims ___ reduce the carbon footprint of the company.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) in
(c) for
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In infinitive constructions after a verb meaning 'aim', we use "to". 
उदाहरण: "aim to reduce".
[RULE] Infinitive after Verbs of Intent

---

[Q1430] Please submit your report ___ the end of the month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By" is used to indicate a deadline. 
उदाहरण: "submit by the end of the month."
[RULE] Preposition of Deadline

---

[Q1431] The manager was unaware ___ the sudden change in schedule.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) to
(c) in
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Unaware of" is the correct idiom meaning not aware of something. 
उदाहरण: "was unaware of the change."
[RULE] Idiomatic Preposition

---

[Q1432] She has a tendency ___ procrastinate when faced with deadlines.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"A tendency to" is the correct construction. 
उदाहरण: "a tendency to procrastinate."
[RULE] Infinitive after Noun

---

[Q1433] He managed ___ finish the report before the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) a
(c) on
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Managed to" is the correct phrase indicating success. 
उदाहरण: "managed to finish."
[RULE] Infinitive after Verbs of Ability

---

[Q1434] The project will be completed ___ March, pending final approvals.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"By March" sets a deadline. 
उदाहरण: "completed by March."
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'by'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition of Deadline

---

[Q1435] He was known ___ his meticulous attention to detail.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) to
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Known for" indicates what someone is famous for. 
उदाहरण: "known for his meticulous attention."
[RULE] Preposition of Reputation

---

[Q1436] She was chosen ___ a very difficult assignment, which made her feel very proud.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used after *chosen* to indicate the purpose or object of the choice.
उदाहरण: उसे एक कठिन असाइनमेंट के लिए चुना गया।
[RULE] Preposition after chosen

---

[Q1437] The committee decided to postpone the meeting ___ until next Wednesday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) so
(b) therefore
(c) so that
(d) in order to
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The phrase *in order to* is used to express purpose, here meaning the meeting would be delayed until next Wednesday.
उदाहरण: बैठक को अगले बुधवार तक विलंबित करने के लिए समिति ने निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Purpose clause

---

[Q1438] He has been working for a multinational ___ for the past six years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) company
(b) corporation
(c) firm
(d) enterprise
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The noun *company* fits best with the adjective *multinational*.
उदाहरण: वह पिछले छह वर्षों से एक बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनी के लिए काम कर रहा है।
[RULE] Noun usage

---

[Q1439] I have never seen ___ such a beautiful sunset in my life.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) any
(d) the
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When referring to a single instance of something that begins with a vowel sound, use *an*.
उदाहरण: मैंने अपनी ज़िंदगी में कभी ऐसी सुंदर सूर्यास्त नहीं देखी है।
[RULE] Indefinite article before vowel sound

---

[Q1440] The new policy will be implemented ___ September 2026.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *in* is used with months and years to indicate a point in time.
उदाहरण: नई नीति सितंबर 2026 में लागू की जाएगी।
[RULE] Preposition with month/year

---

[Q1441] She was ___ to the conference by her supervisor, who praised her work.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) chosen
(b) invited
(c) appointed
(d) selected
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb *invited* correctly expresses that she was asked to attend.
उदाहरण: उसे अपनी पर्यवेक्षक द्वारा सम्मेलन में आमंत्रित किया गया।
[RULE] Verb choice for attending

---

[Q1442] ___ was the reason for the delay in the project completion.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) This
(b) It
(c) That
(d) These
[ANS] c
[SOL]
*That* is used to refer to a specific reason mentioned earlier.
उदाहरण: वह परियोजना पूर्णता में देरी का कारण था।
[RULE] Pronoun reference

---

[Q1443] The report will be ready ___ Friday at the latest.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) on
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*By* is used to indicate a deadline.
उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट शुक्रवार तक तैयार होगी।
[RULE] Deadline preposition

---

[Q1444] The city’s population has been growing steadily ___ the past decade.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) through
[ANS] b
[SOL]
*Since* introduces a time period that began in the past and continues to the present.
उदाहरण: शहर की जनसंख्या पिछले दशक से लगातार बढ़ रही है।
[RULE] Time expression with since

---

[Q1445] She is known ___ her excellent problem‑solving skills.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*For* is the preposition used after *known* to indicate the reason.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी उत्कृष्ट समस्या‑समाधान क्षमताओं के लिए जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Preposition after known

---

[Q1446] The committee members were very ___ about the changes proposed in the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) critical
(b) criticism
(c) critically
(d) criticised
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The correct word is *critical* (adjective) meaning "having a high standard". 
उदा. समिति के सदस्यों को नई नीति में प्रस्तावित परिवर्तनों के बारे में बहुत आलोचनात्मक (critical) विचार थे।
[RULE] Adjective usage – critical

---

[Q1447] She decided ___ a new strategy to improve the team’s performance.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to adopt
(b) adopt
(c) adopting
(d) adopted
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After *decided* we use the base form of the verb without *to*: *decided to adopt* is wrong. 
उदा. उसने टीम के प्रदर्शन को बेहतर बनाने के लिए नई रणनीति अपनाने का निर्णय लिया।
[RULE] Verb + base form after decided

---

[Q1448] The museum is located ___ the old city walls.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) within
(b) inside
(c) between
(d) among
[ANS] b
[SOL]
*Inside* is the appropriate preposition meaning "within the confines of". 
उदा. संग्रहालय पुरानी शहर की दीवारों के अंदर स्थित है।
[RULE] Preposition – inside

---

[Q1449] He is the only person ___ ever travelled to the remote island.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who
(b) whom
(c) whose
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When referring to a person in a relative clause, use *who*. 
उदा. वह एकमात्र व्यक्ति है जिसने कभी उस दूरस्थ द्वीप का दौरा किया है।
[RULE] Relative pronoun – who

---

[Q1450] The project will be completed ___ the end of next month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*By* indicates a deadline. 
उदा. परियोजना अगले महीने के अंत तक पूरी हो जाएगी।
[RULE] Preposition for deadline – by

---

[Q1451] The author’s latest novel is ___ to receive critical acclaim.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) likely
(b) likelihood
(c) likelyly
(d) likened
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*Likely* is the correct adjective meaning "probable". 
उदा. लेखक का नवीनतम उपन्यास आलोचनात्मक प्रशंसा प्राप्त करने की संभावना रखता है।
[RULE] Adjective – likely

---

[Q1452] The data ___ was presented in the meeting was in complete.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) which
(b) whose
(c) who
(d) that
[ANS] d
[SOL]
*That* is correct for a restrictive relative clause referring to the data. 
उदा. वह डेटा जो बैठक में प्रस्तुत किया गया था, अपूर्ण था।
[RULE] Relative pronoun – that (restrictive clause)

---

[Q1453] They had to ___ the proposal because it was unrealistic.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reject
(b) rejecting
(c) rejected
(d) rejection
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After *had to* we use the base form of the verb: *reject*. 
उदा. उन्हें प्रस्ताव को अस्वीकार करना पड़ा क्योंकि यह अवास्तविक था।
[RULE] Modal + base form after had to

---

[Q1454] The company’s profits have ___ since the new management took over.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) increased
(b) increasing
(c) increase
(d) increasingly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*Increased* (past tense) correctly matches the present perfect construction *have increased*. 
उदा. नई प्रबंधन के आने के बाद कंपनी के मुनाफे में वृद्धि हुई है।
[RULE] Past tense in present perfect

---

[Q1455] The mayor announced that the city will ___ a new park by the river.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) build
(b) building
(c) built
(d) to build
[ANS] d
[SOL]
*To build* is the infinitive form after *will*. 
उदा. महापौर ने घोषणा की कि शहर नदी के किनारे एक नया पार्क बनाएगा।
[RULE] Future tense + infinitive

---

[Q1456] The scientist's theory was accepted by the scientific community, _____ he had presented a compelling set of data.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) although
(b) despite
(c) because
(d) however
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The word 'because' introduces a reason that supports the preceding statement.
हिंदी: "क्योंकि" का प्रयोग कारण बताने के लिए किया जाता है।
[RULE] Reason clauses

---

[Q1457] She has been working as a nurse _____ the hospital for over ten years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'at' is used with institutions like hospitals.
हिंदी: अस्पताल जैसी संस्था के साथ 'at' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with institutions

---

[Q1458] The committee will ___ the proposals at the upcoming meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) discuss
(b) discussed
(c) discussing
(d) discussed to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive form 'discuss' follows 'will'.
हिंदी: 'will' के बाद क्रिया का मूल रूप उपयोग होता है।
[RULE] Future tense with infinitive

---

[Q1459] He bought a new laptop, ___ he could work on the go.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) so that
(b) in order that
(c) for
(d) because
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Both 'so that' and 'in order that' express purpose. The standard phrase is 'so that'.
हिंदी: उद्देश्य व्यक्त करने के लिये 'so that' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Purpose clauses

---

[Q1460] The artist's work has been admired _____ critics and the general public alike.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) with
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'by' is used to indicate the agents of admiration.
हिंदी: 'by' का प्रयोग उन लोगों के लिए होता है जो प्रशंसा करते हैं।
[RULE] Prepositions with passive voice

---

[Q1461] If you ___ the meeting, we will have to postpone the announcement.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) miss
(b) missed
(c) missing
(d) misses
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The past simple 'missed' is used in a second conditional clause.
हिंदी: शर्तीय वाक्य में भूतकाल का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Second conditional

---

[Q1462] The new policy will improve the quality of life for ______ citizens.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) all
(b) every
(c) each
(d) any
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase 'every citizen' means all citizens.
हिंदी: 'every' का मतलब सभी से है।
[RULE] Quantifiers

---

[Q1463] The mayor, _____ had been in office for a decade, announced a new development plan.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who
(b) whom
(c) whose
(d) which
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative pronoun 'who' is used for people.
हिंदी: लोगों के लिए 'who' का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Relative pronouns

---

[Q1464] The committee's decision was made after ___ all the evidence thoroughly.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reviewing
(b) to review
(c) review
(d) a review
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The gerund 'reviewing' follows the preposition 'after'.
हिंदी: 'after' के बाद gerund का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Prepositions with gerund

---

[Q1465] The company announced a new policy to ___ the environmental impact of its operations.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reduce
(b) reducing
(c) reduced
(d) to reduce
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive 'reduce' follows the verb 'to' in the purpose clause.
हिंदी: उद्देश्य बताने के लिये infinitive का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Infinitive of purpose

---

[Q1466] He was the first person to ___ the new highway system.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) design
(b) designing
(c) designed
(d) to design
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The past simple 'designed' matches the past perfect sense of 'first person to'.
हिंदी: 'first person to' के बाद भूतकाल का प्रयोग होता है।
[RULE] Past tense after 'first person to'

[Q1467] She has been living _______ this city for five years.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In English, we use the preposition **in** with cities and countries. 
उदा. वह इस शहर में पाँच साल से रह रही है।
[RULE] Preposition – in (used with cities)

---

[Q1468] The manager is responsible _______ ensuring the project is completed on time.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) to
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb **be responsible** is followed by the preposition **for**. 
उदा. प्रबंधक परियोजना को समय पर पूरा करने के लिए जिम्मेदार है।
[RULE] Preposition – for (after be responsible)

[Q1469] If he _______ earlier, he would have caught the train.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had arrived
(b) has arrived
(c) arrived
(d) arrives
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Conditional sentences of the third type use the **past perfect** tense. 
उदाहरण: अगर वह पहले पहुँच गया होता, तो ट्रेन पकड़ पाता।
[RULE] Verb Form – Past Perfect (had + past participle)

[Q1470] Her explanation was so _______ that every one understood the concept immediately.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) clear
(b) cleared
(c) clarify
(d) clarity
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective **clear** is used to describe something that can be easily understood. 
उदाहरण: उसकी व्याख्या इतनी स्पष्ट थी कि सभी ने तुरंत समझ लिया।
[RULE] Vocabulary – clear (adjective)

---

[Q1471] The book is placed _______ the shelf.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
We use the preposition **on** when something is physically resting on a surface. 
उदाहरण: किताब शेल्फ पर रखी है।
[RULE] Preposition – on (placement on a surface)

---

[Q1472] They have been working on the project _______ until now.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase **since** is used with a starting point in time and continues up to the present. 
उदाहरण: वे परियोजना पर अब तक काम कर रहे हैं।
[RULE] Preposition – since (time reference)

---

[Q1473] The committee will conduct the meeting _______ Tuesday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
For days of the week, we use **on**. 
उदाहरण: समिति मंगलवार को बैठक करेगी।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'on'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition – on (days of the week)

[Q1474] The painting was hung _______ the wall at a height of two meters.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The painting is attached to the wall, so we use **on**. 
उदाहरण: पेंटिंग दीवार पर 2 मीटर ऊँचाई पर टंगी हुई है।

[Q1475] I wish I _______ you sooner.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) met
(b) meet
(c) am meeting
(d) meeting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After **wish** + **I**, we use the simple past for unreal past wishes. 
उदाहरण: मैं चाहता था कि मैं तुम्हें पहले मिल पाता।
[RULE] Verb Form – Simple Past after wish

[Q1476] The athlete's performance was _______ than expected.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) better
(b) best
(c) betterly
(d) bestly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The comparative form of **good** is **better**.

[Q1477] She has been working __________ the company for 3 years.

(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) with

Prepositions used with organizations.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition for future time frames.

हिंदी में: "अगले महीने में"।

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) some
(b) a
(c) much
(d) any
[ANS] a

Quantifier with uncountable nouns like money.
In English: use "some" to indicate an unspecified amount.
हिंदी में: "कुछ पैसे"।

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) short

(d) shortly
[ANS] a

Adjective used as a noun modifier.

(a) by

(c) at

Idiomatic preposition after "amazed".

Preposition to express reason.

(a) for

Preposition for duration of time.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) with
(c) of

[ANS] a

Fixed phrase "responsible for".

Comparative form of the adjective "good".

(a) in

(c) on

Preposition for specific time and place.

(a) since

(d) by

Use *since* to indicate a point of time from the past to the present.

(a) submit

The base form of the verb is required after 'to' in the infinitive.

[ANS] a

The base form of the verb follows *will*.

(a) protect

(d) protection
[ANS] a

Base form of the verb after *will*.
उदाहरण: The new law will *protect* the rights of citizens.

(a) for

The correct preposition after *praised* is *for*.

(a) increase

[ANS] a

Base form of the verb follows *to*.

[Q1495] The direct or was ___ to a new position.

(a) promoted

(c) promotion

Past participle *promoted* is used after *was* in passive construction.

The phrase *known for* is followed by *for*.

[Q1497] During the conference, the speaker emphasized the need to ___ innovative solutions.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) develop
(b) developing
(c) developed
(d) development
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Base form of the verb after *to* in infinitive.
उदाहरण: The speaker emphasized the need to *develop* innovative solutions.

[Q1498] She has been working ___ the company for 5 years.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) for
(d) on

We use the preposition **for** to indicate a period of time.

[RULE] Preposition usage – duration

---

[Q1499] They arrived ___ the station just in time.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) to
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **at** is used with a point or a specific location.

[Q1500] I have never ___ such a beautiful painting.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) see
(b) seen
(c) seeing
(d) to see
[ANS] b

After a perfect‑tense verb (have/has/had), we use the past participle form. **Seen** is the past participle of see.

[RULE] Perfect tense – past participle

[Q1501] It was a ___ decision to cancel the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) sensible
(b) sensiblely
(c) sensibility
(d) sensational
[ANS] a

The adjective **sensible** describes a good, practical choice.

[Q1502] He apologized ___ his mistake.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) of
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use the preposition **for** after apologise to indicate the reason.

[RULE] Preposition usage – apology

---

[Q1503] By the time the train arrived, the passengers ___.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) had left
(b) left
(c) leaving
(d) will leave
[ANS] a

[RULE] Past perfect tense

[Q1504] The scientist's discovery was hailed as a ___ breakthrough.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) ground-breaking
(b) groundbreaking
(c) ground breaking
(d) ground-breakingly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The hyphenated adjective **ground-breaking** means revolutionary. 
उदाहरण: "वैज्ञानिक की खोज को एक क्रांतिकारी सफलता कहा गया।"
[RULE] Hyphenated adjectives – ground‑breaking

[Q1505] The book is ___ the table.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) under
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **on** indicates something placed on top of a surface.

[Q1506] She was ___ to the party because she had a fever.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) invited
(b) to invite
(c) invite
(d) inviting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The past participle **invited** is used after the simple past verb *was*. 
उदाहरण: "वह पार्टी के लिए आमंत्रित थी क्योंकि उसे बुखार था।"
[RULE] Passive voice – past participle

---

[Q1507] He was known for his ___ attitude towards learning.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) enthusiastic
(b) enthusiasm
(c) enthusiastically
(d) enthusiast
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective **enthusiastic** describes having enthusiasm. 
उदाहरण: "वह सीखने के प्रति उत्साही रवैया रखता था।"
[RULE] Adjective usage – enthusiastic

---

[Q1508] She was awarded the prize ___ her outstanding performance in the competition.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) with
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used after the verb *awarded* to indicate the reason or cause.
उदा. "He was awarded the award **for** his bravery."
[RULE] Preposition after *awarded*

---

[Q1509] The scientist was praised ___ his discovery of the new particle.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *praised* is followed by *for* to specify the reason.
उदा. "He was praised **for** his dedication."
[RULE] Preposition after *praised*

---

[Q1510] He has been working ___ this company since 2010.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) for
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition *at* is used with *company* to denote the place of employment.
उदा. "I work **at** a bank."
[RULE] Preposition with *company*

---

[Q1511] The committee will meet ___ Friday morning to discuss the proposal.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *on* is used with days and dates.
उदा. "We will meet **on** Monday."
[RULE] Preposition after a day

---

[Q1512] I can't believe he forgot ___ the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) about
(c) for
(d) with
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After *forgot*, the preposition *about* is used to refer to an event.
उदा. "She forgot **about** her appointment."
[RULE] Preposition after *forgot*

---

[Q1513] The policy was enacted ___ the early 1990s.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) during
(b) at
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The preposition *in* is used with a decade or period.
उदा. "It began **in** the 1980s."
[RULE] Preposition with a decade

---

[Q1514] They are planning to launch the product ___ the next quarter.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) during
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *in* is used with a time period such as a quarter or year.
उदा. "We will finish **in** June."
[RULE] Preposition with a time period

---

[Q1515] The book, ___ author is renowned world wide, was published last year.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) whose
(b) which
(c) that
(d) where
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The relative pronoun *whose* indicates possession of the author.
उदा. "The novel, **whose** protagonist is famous, won awards."
[RULE] Relative pronoun for possession

---

[Q1516] He succeeded ___ the project manager's resignation.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) after
(b) during
(c) before
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *after* is used to indicate a sequence of events.
उदा. "He succeeded **after** the CEO left."
[RULE] Preposition after an event

---

[Q1517] The company has seen a sharp decline ___ revenue in the last quarter.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) of
(c) by
(d) into
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase *decline of revenue* uses *of* to link the noun *decline* with the thing that declined.
उदा. "A decline **of** sales was noted."
[RULE] Preposition linking noun phrases

---

[Q1518] She has been working ___ the company for over a decade.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) with
(b) at
(c) in
(d) on
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The preposition *in* is used with *company* when referring to being employed inside the organization.
Example in Hindi: वह कंपनी में दस साल से काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Preposition after the verb *have been working*

---

[Q1519] The manager was ___ the meeting when the fire alarm rang.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) by
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition *at* is used to indicate the location of a meeting. *In* is used for a room, not a meeting itself.
Hindi: जब फायर अलार्म बजा, तो मैनेजर बैठक में था।
[RULE] Preposition indicating location of an event

---

[Q1520] After the present at i on, the committee asked the speaker to ___ a brief summary.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) write
(b) wrote
(c) writes
(d) written
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive form *write* follows the verb *asked* in the past tense. *Wrote* is past tense but not suitable after *asked* in this construction.
Hindi: प्रस्तुति के बाद, समिति ने वक्ता से संक्षिप्त सारांश लिखने के लिए कहा।
[RULE] Infinitive after the verb *ask* in past tense

---

[Q1521] The policy will be effective ___ the next fiscal year.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) during
(b) by
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The preposition *in* is used to refer to a period of time, such as a fiscal year. *By* indicates a deadline, not a time frame.
Hindi: नीति अगले वित्तीय वर्ष में प्रभावी होगी।
[RULE] Preposition indicating time period

---

[Q1522] The students were asked to submit their reports ___ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *on* is used with days of the week. *By* would indicate a deadline, but *on* is correct here.
Hindi: छात्रों से रिपोर्ट शुक्रवार को जमा करने के लिए कहा गया।
[RULE] Preposition with days of the week

---

[Q1523] He is known ___ his punctuality and attention to detail.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) at
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used after *known* to indicate the reason or quality.
Hindi: वह अपनी समयबद्धता और विस्तृत ध्यान के लिए जाना जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition after *known*

---

[Q1524] The new software will ___ the process of data entry.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) simplify
(b) simplified
(c) simplifies
(d) simplifying
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive *simplify* follows the auxiliary verb *will*. The other forms are incorrect in this structure.
Hindi: नया सॉफ्टवेयर डेटा एंट्री की प्रक्रिया को सरल बनाएगा।
[RULE] Infinitive after modal verb *will*

---

[Q1525] The river ___ the city into two parts.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) divides
(b) divide
(c) divided
(d) dividing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The simple present verb *divides* matches the subject *river* and describes a habitual action.
Hindi: नदी शहर को दो हिस्सों में विभाजित करती है।
[RULE] Subject-verb agreement in simple present

---

[Q1526] The committee will convene ___ the end of the month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) in
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition *at* is used with a specific point in time, such as the end of a month. *In* refers to a period.
Hindi: समिति महीने के अंत में मिलेंगी।
[RULE] Preposition indicating precise time point

---

[Q1527] She is eager ___ learning more about data science.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) at
(c) for
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive *to* follows the adjective *eager* when expressing desire. Other prepositions are incorrect.
Hindi: वह डेटा विज्ञान के बारे में अधिक सीखने के लिए उत्सुक है।
[RULE] Infinitive after adjective *eager*

---

[Q1528] He has ____ to finish the project before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a chance
(b) a chance of
(c) a chance to
(d) a chance at
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Use of 'have a chance to + verb' meaning opportunity.
उदा. वह जर्मनी जाने का एक मौका रखता है।
[RULE] have a chance to + verb

---

[Q1529] The manager was ______ by the employees for his impartiality.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) appreciated
(b) appreciating
(c) appreciation
(d) appreciative
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Passive construction with 'be + past participle'.
उदा. कर्मचारी उसकी निष्पक्षता के लिए उसकी प्रशंसा करते हैं।
[RULE] be + past participle (passive voice)

---

[Q1530] She has been studying medicine ______ her 12th grade.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Since' is used to indicate the starting point of an action that continues to the present.
उदा. वह 12वीं कक्षा से ही चिकित्सा पढ़ रही है।
[RULE] since + time reference

---

[Q1531] I am looking forward to ______ you at the conference.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) see
(b) seeing
(c) to see
(d) saw
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After 'look forward to' we use a gerund.
उदा. मैं सम्मेलन में आपको देखने के लिए उत्सुक हूँ।
[RULE] look forward to + gerund

---

[Q1532] The company announced a plan ______ reduce costs.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) of
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A plan to + verb expresses intention.
उदा. कंपनी ने लागत घटाने की योजना की घोषणा की।
[RULE] plan to + infinitive

---

[Q1533] He has a habit of ______ his keys.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) losing
(b) lose
(c) lost
(d) loses
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Noun + of + gerund indicates habitual action.
उदा. उसका अपनी चाबियाँ खोने की आदत है।
[RULE] noun + of + gerund

---

[Q1534] The teacher asked the students to ______ their assignments by Monday.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submitting
(c) submitted
(d) submits
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'ask + someone to', use infinitive.
उदा. शिक्षक ने छात्रों से सोमवार तक अपने असाइनमेंट जमा करने को कहा।
[RULE] ask + someone to + infinitive

---

[Q1535] She felt ____ after hearing the news.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) happened
(b) happening
(c) happy
(d) happily
[ANS] c
[SOL]
After 'feel' we use adjective to describe emotion.
उदा. समाचार सुनकर वह खुश महसूस करती है।
[RULE] feel + adjective

---

[Q1536] The building was built ______ 1850.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Built in' is used with a year or period.
उदा. भवन 1850 में बनाया गया था।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'in'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] built in + year

---

[Q1537] He is known ______ his rigorous training.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'known for' + noun or phrase.
उदा. वह अपनी कठोर प्रशिक्षण के लिए जाना जाता है।
[RULE] known for + noun/phrase

---

[Q1538] The committee will review the proposal ______ the new policy is implemented.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) before
(b) after
(c) during
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'After' is used to indicate a subsequent event.
उदा. नई नीति लागू होने के बाद समिति प्रस्ताव की समीक्षा करेगी।
[RULE] verb + after + clause

---

[Q1539] The committee has decided to __________ the meeting due to unforeseen circumstances.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) postpone the meeting
(b) postpone to the meeting
(c) cancel the meeting
(d) defer the meeting
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *postpone* means to delay something. It is followed by the object directly: *postpone the meeting*. In Hindi: "बैठक को स्थगित किया गया।"
उदा. The meeting will be postponed tomorrow.
[RULE] Correct verb usage with object

---

[Q1540] She has been working here __________ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) in
(d) from
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *since* to indicate a point in time from which something has been continuous. In Hindi: "2010 से वह यहाँ काम कर रही है।"
उदा. I have lived here since 2015.
[RULE] Preposition for time starting point

---

[Q1541] The new policy will be implemented __________ the next fiscal year.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) during
(b) by
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Use *in* to refer to a period of time: *in the next fiscal year*. In Hindi: "अगले वित्तीय वर्ष में नीति लागू की जाएगी।"
उदा. The project will finish in June.
[RULE] Preposition for time period

---

[Q1542] The students were asked to __________ their assignments before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submit to
(c) submit for
(d) submit on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *submit* is used with the object directly: *submit their assignments*. In Hindi: "वे अपनी असाइनमेंट जमा करें।"
उदा. Please submit the report by Friday.
[RULE] Correct verb form with object

---

[Q1543] Despite the rain, the match continued __________.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) without interruption
(b) without an interruption
(c) without interruptions
(d) Gregarious
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the adjective phrase *without interruption* to mean uninterrupted. In Hindi: "बारिश के बावजूद मैच बिना किसी रुकावट के चला।"
उदा. The play continued without interruption.
[RULE] Adjective phrase for continuity

---

[Q1544] He was __________ to the committee for his outstanding contribution.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) nominated
(b) nominated as
(c) nominated by
(d) nominated for
[ANS] d
[SOL]
When someone is selected for a role, use *nominated for*. In Hindi: "उसे उत्कृष्ट योगदान के लिए समिति में नामित किया गया।"
उदा. She was nominated for the award.
[RULE] Correct preposition with nominated

---

[Q1545] The conference will be held __________ the auditorium on Tuesday.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *in* before a building or place: *in the auditorium*. In Hindi: "सम्मेलन मंगलवार को ऑडिटोरियम के अंदर आयोजित किया जाएगा।"
उदा. The event will be held in the hall.
[RULE] Preposition for location inside a place

---

[Q1546] The manager was __________ by the employees for his fairness.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) respected
(b) respected by
(c) respected to
(d) respected for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When someone receives respect from others, use *respected by*. In Hindi: "प्रबंधक को कर्मचारियों द्वारा उनकी निष्पक्षता के कारण सम्मानित किया गया।"
उदा. She was respected by her peers.
[RULE] Preposition for source of respect

---

[Q1547] The project is expected to be completed __________ the end of the year.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) in
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *by* to indicate a deadline: *by the end of the year*. In Hindi: "परियोजना वर्ष के अंत तक पूरी होने की उम्मीद है।"
उदा. Finish the task by tonight.
[RULE] Preposition for deadline

---

[Q1548] The book was written __________ a renowned author.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) to
(c) for
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *by* to indicate authorship: *written by a renowned author*. In Hindi: "किताब एक प्रसिद्ध लेखक द्वारा लिखी गई है।"
उदा. The novel was written by the famous writer.
[RULE] Preposition for authorship

---

[Q1549] He ___ to the bank to withdraw money.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) go
(b) went
(c) goes
(d) going
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past simple tense is used for a completed action. 
उदाहरण: उसने पैसे निकालने के लिए बैंक गया।
[RULE] Past Simple Tense

---

[Q1550] The painting was ___ by the famous artist.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) paint
(b) painted
(c) painting
(d) painter
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past participle is used in passive voice. 
उदाहरण: पेंटिंग प्रसिद्ध कलाकार द्वारा बनाई गई थी।
[RULE] Passive Voice

---

[Q1551] I have never ___ sushi before.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) eated
(b) ate
(c) eating
(d) eat
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past simple after 'never' expresses an action that has not happened. 
उदाहरण: मैंने कभी सुषी नहीं खाई।
[RULE] Past Simple after Never

---

[Q1552] The students were ___ for their exam results.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) waiting
(b) waited
(c) wait
(d) waits
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Present participle used as an adjective after 'were'. 
उदाहरण: छात्र अपने परीक्षा परिणाम के लिए इंतजार कर रहे थे।
[RULE] Present Participle as Adjective

---

[Q1553] She is ___ student in the class.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the best
(b) best
(c) best of
(d) best's
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Superlative adjective does not take an article. 
उदाहरण: वह कक्षा में सबसे अच्छी छात्रा है।
[RULE] Superlative Adjective

---

[Q1554] They will ___ the project by next month.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) complete
(b) completing
(c) completed
(d) completes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive is used after modal verb 'will'. 
उदाहरण: वे अगले महीने तक परियोजना पूरा करेंगे।
[RULE] Infinitive after Modal Verb

---

[Q1555] The teacher asked us to ___ our home work before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) finish
(b) finished
(c) finishing
(d) finishes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive follows 'asked to'. 
उदाहरण: शिक्षक ने हमसे कहा कि हम अपनी होमवर्क समय सीमा से पहले पूरा करें।
[RULE] Infinitive after 'ask'

---

[Q1556] We should ___ the best possible solution.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) find
(b) finding
(c) found
(d) finds
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Infinitive follows modal verb 'should'. 
उदाहरण: हमें सबसे अच्छा संभव समाधान ढूंढना चाहिए।
[RULE] Infinitive after Modal Verb

---

[Q1557] He has been ___ in this field for over a decade.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) work
(b) working
(c) worked
(d) works
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Present participle after 'been' forms present continuous. 
उदाहरण: वह इस क्षेत्र में एक दशक से अधिक समय से काम कर रहा है।
[RULE] Present Continuous with 'been'

---

[Q1558] The manager was _______ the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) aware of
(b) aware in
(c) aware at
(d) aware on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: The phrase ‘aware of’ is used to indicate knowledge about something.
उदा. वह नई नीति से अवगत था।
[RULE] Preposition: of (awareness)

---

[Q1559] She has been working here _______ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) at
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use ‘since’ to refer to a specific point in time.
उदा. वह 2010 से यहाँ काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Time preposition: since

---

[Q1560] The book is _______ the box.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) under
(b) on
(c) over
(d) into
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: ‘under’ indicates a position below something.
उदा. किताब बॉक्स के नीचे है।
[RULE] Preposition of position: under

---

[Q1561] He _______ the news with great enthusiasm.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reacted
(b) replied
(c) responded
(d) accepted
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use ‘reacted’ when expressing an emotional response.
उदा. उसने खबर पर बहुत उत्साह के साथ प्रतिक्रिया दी।
[RULE] Verb choice: react vs reply

---

[Q1562] The committee will meet _______ Wednesday.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) in
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: ‘on’ is used with days of the week.
उदा. समिति बुधवार को मिलेगी।
[RULE] Preposition with days: on

---

[Q1563] They have been studying English _______ five years.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) over
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: ‘for’ indicates a duration of time.
उदा. वे पाँच वर्षों से अंग्रेज़ी पढ़ रहे हैं।
[RULE] Preposition of duration: for

---

[Q1564] The children were playing _______ the park.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: ‘in’ is used for a place that is enclosed or bounded.
उदा. बच्चे पार्क में खेल रहे थे।
[RULE] Preposition of location: in

---

[Q1565] The company will announce the results _______ tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) on
(c) by
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: ‘on’ is used with specific dates or days.
उदा. कंपनी कल परिणाम घोषित करेगी।
[RULE] Preposition with dates: on

---

[Q1566] He is _______ doctor.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) some
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use ‘an’ before a word that starts with a vowel sound.
उदा. वह एक डॉक्टर है।
[RULE] Indefinite article before vowel sound: an

---

[Q1567] The painting was hung _______ the wall.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: ‘on’ is used for surfaces.
उदा. पेंटिंग दीवार पर लटका हुआ था।
[RULE] Preposition of surface: on

---

[Q1568] He was ___________ the news, he decided to resign.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) shocked by
(b) shocked at
(c) shocked with
(d) shocked to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The idiomatic expression is "shocked at" meaning surprised by the news. In Hindi: "उसे ख़बर से चौंक गया, उसने इस्तीफा देने का निर्णय लिया।"
[RULE] Preposition usage with adjectives like "shocked"

---

[Q1569] The train _______ at the platform for 10 minutes.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) stops
(b) stops at
(c) stop at
(d) stop
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb "stop" in the present simple third person singular requires an 's': "stops at". In Hindi: "ट्रेन 10 मिनट तक प्लेटफ़ॉर्म पर रुकी।"
[RULE] Present simple tense agreement with singular subject

---

[Q1570] It is essential _______ admit that we made a mistake.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) to
(d) for
[ANS] c
[SOL]
"Essential to" is a fixed complement of the adjective "essential". In Hindi: "यह ज़रूरी है कि हम अपनी गलती स्वीकार करें।"
[RULE] Complement of adjectives with infinitive

---

[Q1571] The company has been in business for _______ years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) twenty
(b) twenty year
(c) twenty years
(d) twenty years old
[ANS] c
[SOL]
"For twenty years" is the correct form; the noun phrase must agree in number. In Hindi: "कंपनी पचास वर्षों से व्यवसाय में है।"
[RULE] Phrase structure with preposition 'for' + noun phrase

---

[Q1572] He will _______ his decision after the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reconsider
(b) reconsidered
(c) reconsidering
(d) Gregarious
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The future simple requires the base form of the verb: "will reconsider". In Hindi: "वह बैठक के बाद अपना निर्णय पुनः विचार करेगा।"
[RULE] Future simple construction with modal 'will'

---

[Q1573] The painting was _______ a masterpiece.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) considered
(b) consider
(c) considering
(d) considerate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Considered" is the past participle used in passive construction. In Hindi: "पेंटिंग को एक उत्कृष्ट कृति माना गया।"
[RULE] Passive voice with past participle

---

[Q1574] I am looking forward _______ your reply.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) at
(d) of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"Looking forward to" is the fixed collocation. In Hindi: "मैं आपकी प्रतिक्रिया के इंतज़ार में हूँ।"
[RULE] Prepositional phrase after idiom "looking forward"

---

[Q1575] They have been studying _______ the new policy.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) about
(c) in
(d) under
[ANS] b
[SOL]
"Studying about" is not idiomatic; the correct preposition is "about". In Hindi: "वे नई नीति के बारे में पढ़ रहे हैं।"
[RULE] Preposition usage with the verb "study"

---

[Q1576] The doctor advised him to take the medicine _______ 8:00 am.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2023 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) by
(c) before
(d) after
[ANS] a
[SOL]
"At 8:00 am" indicates the specific time. In Hindi: "डॉक्टर ने सलाह दी कि वह 8 बजे दवा ले।"
[RULE] Preposition 'at' for specific time

---

[Q1577] She has an ___ interest in environmental issues.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use of article 'an' before a word starting with a vowel sound.
उदा. "She has an interest in ..."
[RULE] Article Usage – 'an' before vowel sound

---

[Q1578] She could not get a job ___ he had studied abroad.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because
(b) since
(c) although
(d) in spite of
[ANS] d
[SOL]
'In spite of' is a prepositional phrase used to express contrast.
उदा. "She could not get a job in spite of his studies abroad."
[RULE] Preposition Phrase – 'in spite of'

[Q1579] ___ Mary was appointed as the manager.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) While
(b) When
(c) Although
(d) Since
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'When' is used to indicate the time at which an action occurs.
उदा. "When Mary was appointed..."
[RULE] Temporal Conjunction – 'When'

---

[Q1580] He has always been ___ to the news.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) aware
(b) aware of
(c) aware for
(d) aware to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Aware of' is the correct collocation meaning 'conscious of'.
उदा. "He is aware of the situation."
[RULE] Collocation – 'aware of'

---

[Q1581] She is ___ a very talented singer.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) being
(b) to
(c) as
(d) in
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'As' is used to describe a role or function.
उदा. "She is as a singer."
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'as'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition Use – 'as'

---

[Q1582] So many people ___ the museum on Sunday.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) were
(b) was
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past tense plural verb 'were' agrees with plural subject 'people'.
उदा. "So many people were at the museum."
[RULE] Subject–Verb Agreement (Past Tense)

---

[Q1583] He was ___ in the garden.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) standing
(b) stands
(c) stand
(d) standed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Standing' is the correct verb form for present continuous tense.
उदा. "He was standing in the garden."
[RULE] Verb Form – Present Continuous

[Q1584] Please make ___ decision.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Indefinite article 'a' is used before a word that begins with a consonant sound.
उदा. "Please make a decision."
[RULE] Article Usage – 'a' before consonant sound

[Q1585] Despite ___ heavy traffic, they arrived on time.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the
(b) a
(c) an
(d) no
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Despite' is followed by a noun phrase; 'the heavy traffic' is correct.
उदा. "Despite the heavy traffic..."
[RULE] Preposition Usage – 'Despite'

[Q1586] He was ___ for the job.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) considered
(b) consider
(c) considering
(d) considers
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Considered' is the correct past participle after 'was'.
उदा. "He was considered for the job."
[RULE] Passive Voice – Past Participle

[Q1587] She has been studying English ________ January.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'since' for a specific point in time. 
उदाहरण: "She has been studying English since January."
[RULE] Since - Point in time

[Q1588] He could not attend the meeting ________ his health condition.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) due to
(b) because of
(c) in spite of
(d) for
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'Because of' is used for reasons. 
उदाहरण: "He could not attend because of his health condition."
[RULE] Because of - Reason

---

[Q1589] The manager asked the employees to submit the report ________ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) on
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'By' indicates deadline. 
उदाहरण: "Submit the report by Friday."
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'by'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] By - Deadline

[Q1590] The scientist was ________ a new discovery after months of research.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) proud of
(b) proud for
(c) proud in
(d) proud at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Proud of' is used for being satisfied with something. 
उदाहरण: "He was proud of the new discovery."
[RULE] Proud of - Satisfaction
