[Q1591] The new policy will be implemented ________ the next fiscal year.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'In' is used for a period of time. 
उदाहरण: "Implemented in the next fiscal year."
[RULE] In - Time Period

---

[Q1592] She has been working at the bank ________ 2015.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Since' indicates the start point. 
उदाहरण: "Working at the bank since 2015."
[RULE] Since - Starting Point

---

[Q1593] He apologized ________ his mistake.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Apologize for' is the correct phrase. 
उदाहरण: "He apologized for his mistake."
[RULE] Apologize for - Apology

[Q1594] The committee will convene ________ the 15th of May.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'On' is used for specific dates. 
उदाहरण: "On the 15th of May."
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'on'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] On - Specific Date

---

[Q1595] She is ________ the best candidate for the job.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) among
(b) between
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Among' is used when referring to more than two options. 
उदाहरण: "She is among the best candidates."
[RULE] Among - More than Two

---

[Q1596] The new software will be released ________ the end of the month.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] b
[SOL]
'In' is used for a period of time. 
उदाहरण: "Released in the end of the month."
[RULE] In - Time Period

---

[Q1597] She has been working here ____ five years.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) by
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used to indicate duration of time.
उदाहरण: मैं पाँच वर्षों से यहाँ काम कर रहा हूँ।
[RULE] Preposition of duration

---

[Q1598] They ____ the train when it started to rain.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) hopeful
(b) hoping
(c) hope
(d) hoped
[ANS] b
[SOL]
After the conjunction *when*, a gerund (verb + -ing) is required.
उदाहरण: जब बारिश शुरू हुई तो वे ट्रेन की प्रतीक्षा कर रहे थे।
[RULE] Gerund after subordinating conjunction

---

[Q1599] The book is ____ in the cupboard.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) under
(d) above
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition *in* is used for an object inside a container or space.
उदाहरण: पुस्तक अलमारी के अंदर है।
[RULE] Preposition of location

---

[Q1600] Neither of the students ____ present at the meeting.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) was
(b) were
(c) is
(d) are
[ANS] a
[SOL]
With *neither* the verb remains singular.
उदाहरण: बैठक में दोनों में से कोई भी उपस्थित नहीं था।
[RULE] Subject–verb agreement with *neither*

---

[Q1601] He has been studying Italian ____ a month.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Duration is expressed with *for*.
उदाहरण: वह एक महीने से इतालवी का अध्ययन कर रहा है।
[RULE] Preposition of duration

---

[Q1602] The road that leads ____ the river is closed.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) from
(c) into
(d) towards
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Direction towards a place uses *to*.
उदाहरण: वह सड़क नदी की ओर जाती है।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'to'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition of direction

---

[Q1603] I can't think of ___ solution to this problem.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) no
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *a* before a consonant sound; *an* before a vowel sound.
उदाहरण: मुझे इस समस्या का कोई समाधान नहीं सूझ रहा है।
[RULE] Indefinite article usage

---

[Q1604] She was the first person who ____ the news.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) hear
(b) heard
(c) hearing
(d) hears
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Past tense of *hear* is *heard*.
उदाहरण: वह समाचार सुनने वाली पहली व्यक्ति थी।
[RULE] Past tense of regular verbs

---

[Q1605] The company announced that it will launch the product ____ next month.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *in* for months and years.
उदाहरण: कंपनी ने अगले महीने उत्पाद लॉन्च करने की घोषणा की।
[RULE] Preposition of time (months)

---

[Q1606] The ___ of the conference was postponed due to bad we at her
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) schedule
(b) scheduling
(c) scheduled
(d) schedul
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the noun *schedule* to mean the plan of events.
उदाहरण: सम्मेलन का कार्यक्रम मौसम के कारण स्थगित कर दिया गया।
[RULE] Noun form of verb

---

[Q1607] Every time I visit the museum, I am amazed by its ____ collections.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) rich
(b) riches
(c) richly
(d) richness
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Adjective *rich* describes the collections; *riches* is a noun, *richly* an adverb, *richness* a noun.
उदाहरण: संग्रहालय के संग्रह समृद्ध हैं।
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1608] She has been working here ______ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
We use 'since' to indicate a starting point in time.
उदाहरण: हम 'since' का प्रयोग किसी समय बिंदु को दर्शाने के लिए करते हैं।
[RULE] Preposition usage: point in time

---

[Q1609] The committee will meet ______ Thursday.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2024]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'on' with days of the week.

[Q1661] He couldn't attend the meeting ______ he was ill.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) although
(b) because
(c) when
(d) while
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The conjunction *because* introduces a reason. Example: *I stayed home because it rained.*
उदाहरण: *वह बीमार होने के कारण बैठक में नहीं आ सका।*
[RULE] Conjunction for reason

---

[Q1662] The book, ___ author is unknown, was published last year.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) whose
(b) who
(c) that
(d) which
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The possessive pronoun *whose* is used to show ownership. Example: *The man whose car was stolen.*
उदाहरण: *पुस्तक, जिसका लेखक अज्ञात है, पिछले वर्ष प्रकाशित हुई।*
[RULE] Possessive pronoun whose

---

[Q1663] I have never seen a ___ bird.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) soaring
(b) soar
(c) soaringly
(d) Nefarious
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective *soaring* describes a bird that flies high. Example: *Soaring gulls.*
उदाहरण: *मैंने कभी एक ऊँची उड़ने वाली पक्षी नहीं देखा।*
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1664] The report was submitted ______ the dead line
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) before
(b) by
(c) after
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition *by* indicates that the action is completed on or before the deadline. Example: *Submit the assignment by Friday.*
उदाहरण: *रिपोर्ट समय सीमा के भीतर प्रस्तुत की गई।*
[RULE] Preposition by deadline

---

[Q1665] It is difficult to accept ______ such criticism.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) this
(b) such
(c) any
(d) any of
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The adjective *such* is used before a noun to emphasize the nature of the noun. Example: *such mistakes.*
उदाहरण: *ऐसी आलोचना स्वीकार करना कठिन है।*
[RULE] Use of such

---

[Q1666] The company is expected to increase its revenue ___ next quarter.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The preposition *by* is used to indicate a target time by which something will happen. Example: *Finish the project by July.*
उदाहरण: *कंपनी अपेक्षित है कि अगले तिमाही तक राजस्व बढ़ा लेगी।*
[RULE] Preposition by target time

---

[Q1667] We have to finish the project ___ the end of this month.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) in
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *by* indicates the latest time by which the project must be completed. Example: *Submit the report by Monday.*
उदाहरण: *हमें इस महीने के अंत तक परियोजना समाप्त करनी है।*
[RULE] Preposition by deadline

---

[Q1668] She has been training ___ the marathon for six months.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used with a duration of time. Example: *I have been learning piano for two years.*
उदाहरण: *वह छह महीनों से मैराथन के लिए प्रशिक्षण ले रही है।*
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q1669] The children were playing ___ the garden when it started to rain.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) by
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *in* is used to indicate location within a place. Example: *Play in the park.*
उदाहरण: *बच्चे बगीचे में खेल रहे थे जब बारिश शुरू हुई।*
[RULE] Preposition in location

---

[Q1670] The manager praised the team for their ___ effort during the crisis.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) sincere
(b) sincerity
(c) sincerely
(d) sinceries
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective *sincere* describes the quality of the effort. Example: *Sincere apologies.*
उदाहरण: *प्रबंधक ने संकट के दौरान उनकी सच्ची मेहनत की प्रशंसा की।*
[RULE] Adjective usage

---

[Q1671] The committee will decide ___ the proposed changes by next month.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) of
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use "on" after "decide" to indicate the subject of decision.
उदाहरण: "The committee will decide on the proposed changes."
[RULE] Preposition after 'decide'

---

[Q1672] He was ___ by surprise when he saw the gifts.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) taken
(b) found
(c) met
(d) given
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The idiom "taken by surprise" means to be shocked.
उदाहरण: "He was taken by surprise."
[RULE] Idiomatic expression 'taken by surprise'

---

[Q1673] They decided to postpone the meeting ___ a few days.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Use "by" to indicate a time limit or deadline.
उदाहरण: "Postpone the meeting by a few days."
[RULE] Preposition 'by' for deadlines

---

[Q1674] The book was published ___ a renowned publisher.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) with
(c) to
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "by" to indicate the publisher or author of a work.
उदाहरण: "Published by Penguin."
[RULE] Preposition 'by' with publications

---

[Q1675] She ___ to the hospital after the accident.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) went
(b) go
(c) going
(d) goes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Past tense of "go" is "went".
उदाहरण: "She went to the hospital."
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'went'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Past tense of 'go'

---

[Q1676] The students are expected to submit the report ___ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) on
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "by" for a deadline.
उदाहरण: "Submit by Friday."
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'by'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' for deadlines

---

[Q1677] I have never ___ such a beautiful view.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) seen
(b) see
(c) to see
(d) seeing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the past participle "seen" with "have never".
उदाहरण: "I have never seen such a view."
[RULE] Present perfect with 'have never'

---

[Q1678] The city is famous ___ its historic monuments.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "famous for" to indicate the reason for fame.
उदाहरण: "Famous for its monuments."
[RULE] Preposition after 'famous'

---

[Q1679] He will be responsible ___ maintaining the equipment.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) of
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use "responsible for" to denote accountability.
उदाहरण: "Responsible for maintaining equipment."
[RULE] Preposition after 'responsible'

[Q1680] She has been working _______ this project for five months.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The verb phrase requires the preposition 'on' to indicate the object of work.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'working on a project' एक सामान्य वाक्यांश है।
[RULE] Preposition usage with 'work'

[Q1681] He is eager _______ the new job.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to join
(b) join
(c) joining
(d) joined
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective 'eager' is followed by an infinitive form of the verb.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'eager to do something'।
[RULE] Infinitive after adjectives

---

[Q1682] The book was published _______ 2005.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Years are introduced with the preposition 'in' when stating publication date.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'published in 2005'।
[RULE] Preposition for years

[Q1683] I cannot attend the meeting _______ the time.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) during
(d) for
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'During' is the correct preposition to express a period of time.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'during the meeting'।
[RULE] Preposition for periods

---

[Q1684] She has been learning Spanish _______ last year.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The present perfect continuous tense uses 'since' to indicate the starting point.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'since last year'।
[RULE] Since vs. for

---

[Q1685] Please submit the report _______ Monday.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) on
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'By' is used to indicate a deadline before a particular time.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'submit by Monday'।
[RULE] Deadline prepositions

[Q1686] The company is known _______ its innovative products.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) to
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'known for' is a fixed collocation meaning 'famous for'.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'known for its excellence'।
[RULE] Known for

[Q1687] He bought a house _______ a small town.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) of
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition 'in' indicates location within a place.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'in a small town'।
[RULE] Location prepositions

[Q1688] I am looking forward _______ meeting you.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Looking forward to' is the standard expression with an infinitive.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'looking forward to meeting you'।
[RULE] Looking forward to

---

[Q1689] This is the first time I have seen a comet _______.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Nefarious
(b) in space
(c) in the sky at night
(d) in the sky
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The simplest and most common phrase is 'in the sky'.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'first time I have seen a comet in the sky'।
[RULE] Simple prepositional phrase

[Q1690] They will arrive _______ the airport at 6 p.m.
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Arrival at a specific place uses the preposition 'at'.
उदाहरण के लिए, 'arrive at the airport'।
[RULE] Arrival preposition

---

[Q1691] She has been studying Russian for the last ___ months.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) three
(b) four
(c) five
(d) six
[ANS] c
[SOL]
We use the present perfect continuous tense to talk about an action that started in the past and continues to the present. The correct preposition is "for" followed by a period of time.
उदाहरण: वह तीन महीनों से रूसी पढ़ रही है।
[RULE] Present Perfect Continuous + for + duration

[Q1692] The committee was ________ to investigate the allegations.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) called upon
(b) put forward
(c) sent out
(d) put to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The idiom "call upon" means to ask or order someone to do something. It is commonly used in formal contexts.
उदाहरण: समिति को जांच करने के लिए बुलाया गया।
[RULE] Idiomatic phrase 'call upon'

---

[Q1693] He will be promoted if he ___ the annual performance review.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) passes
(b) pass
(c) passed
(d) passing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the simple present tense after 'if' to indicate a future condition. The verb must be in its base form.
उदाहरण: यदि वह वार्षिक समीक्षा पास करता है, तो उसे पदोन्नति मिलेगी।
[RULE] If + present simple

---

[Q1694] The new policy aims to ______ the gap between urban and rural education.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bridge
(b) bridging
(c) bridged
(d) bridges
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a noun phrase, use the infinitive form of the verb 'bridge' to indicate the action of closing the gap.
उदाहरण: नई नीति शहरी और ग्रामीण शिक्षा के बीच की खाई को पाटने का लक्ष्य रखती है।
[RULE] Infinitive after noun

---

[Q1695] The doctor advised her to ______ some rest after the surgery.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) take
(b) taking
(c) took
(d) takes
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the verb 'advise', we use the base form of the infinitive without 'to'.
उदाहरण: डॉक्टर ने उसे सर्जरी के बाद कुछ आराम लेने की सलाह दी।
[RULE] Advise + base verb

---

[Q1696] They were disappointed that the concert was ______ postponed.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) not
(b) no
(c) never
(d) none
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a negative clause, we use 'not' before the verb. "No" is used before nouns, not verbs.
उदाहरण: वे निराश थे कि कॉन्सर्ट को नहीं स्थगित किया गया।
[RULE] Negative adverb 'not' + verb

---

[Q1697] He was chosen for the team ______ his excellent performance in the trials.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because of
(b) because
(c) because to
(d) Futile
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "of" is required after "because" when explaining a reason.
उदाहरण: उसकी उत्कृष्ट प्रदर्शन के कारण उसे टीम के लिए चुना गया।
[RULE] Because + of + noun/gerund

---

[Q1698] The committee will convene tomorrow, provided that all members ______ present.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) are
(b) is
(c) be
(d) been
[ANS] a
[SOL]
In a conditional clause, use the simple present tense in the main clause, and the base form in the 'provided that' clause.
उदाहरण: समिति कल बुलाई जाएगी, बशर्ते सभी सदस्य उपस्थित हों।
[RULE] Provided that + present simple

---

[Q1699] She is known ______ her warm hospitality.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) to
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "known for" is followed by a noun or gerund to indicate a characteristic.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी गर्मी की मेहमाननवाज़ी के लिए जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Known for + noun/gerund

---

[Q1700] Please keep the door ______. It is windy outside.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) open
(b) opened
(c) opens
(d) opening
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When giving a command, use the base form of the verb without 'to'.
उदाहरण: कृपया दरवाजा खुला रखें।
[RULE] Imperative + base verb

---

[Q1701] The train is expected to arrive ______ the station by 9 pm.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Use the preposition "at" to indicate the exact time of arrival.
उदाहरण: ट्रेन 9 बजे स्टेशन पर पहुंचने की उम्मीद है।
[RULE] Preposition 'at' + time

---

[Q1702] The committee was ________ the proposal after careful scrutiny.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) informed
(b) informed of
(c) informed about
(d) informed by
[ANS] c
[SOL]
In English, when someone is informed about something, we use the preposition **about**. Example: वह प्रस्ताव के बारे में सूचित किया गया।
[RULE] Preposition 'about' after 'informed'

---

[Q1703] She has been working here ________ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adverb **since** is used to indicate a starting point in time that continues up to the present. Example: वह 2010 से यहाँ काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Use of 'since' for a starting point in time

[Q1704] He will ________ the exam with a good score.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) pass
(b) passes
(c) passing
(d) to pass
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a modal verb like **will**, we use the base form of the verb (infinitive without 'to'). Example: वह परीक्षा पास करेगा।
[RULE] Infinitive after modal 'will'

---

[Q1705] The book is ________ the shelf.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **on** is used to indicate a position on a surface. Example: किताब शेल्फ पर है।
[RULE] Position preposition 'on'

[Q1706] They are going to move ________ the city.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) from
(c) into
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **to** indicates direction towards a place. Example: वे शहर में जा रहे हैं।
[RULE] Preposition 'to' for movement

---

[Q1707] He was known ________ his generosity.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When someone is known for a particular quality, we use the preposition **for**. Example: वह अपनी उदारता के लिए जाना जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition 'for' after 'known'

---

[Q1708] The train arrived ________ time.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **on** is used with a specific time to indicate punctuality. Example: ट्रेन समय पर आई।
[RULE] Preposition 'on' with time

---

[Q1709] She could not attend the meeting ________ her illness.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) due to
(b) because of
(c) because
(d) due
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase **due to** expresses the cause of something. Example: उसकी बीमारी के कारण वह बैठक में उपस्थित नहीं हो सका।
[RULE] 'due to' for cause

---

[Q1710] I am looking forward ________ meeting you again.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the idiom **look forward**, we use the preposition **to**. Example: मैं फिर से मिलने के लिए उत्सुक हूं।
[RULE] Preposition 'to' after 'look forward'

[Q1711] The company is planning ________ a new product line next year.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2025 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to launch
(b) launching
(c) launch
(d) launches
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After an expression of intention such as **planning**, we use the infinitive form **to launch**. Example: कंपनी अगले वर्ष एक नया उत्पाद लाइन लॉन्च करने की योजना बना रही है।
[RULE] Infinitive after planning

(a) at
(b) in
(c) on
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the preposition *at* to indicate the place of employment.

When a noun is countable and singular, use the indefinite article *a*.

The verb *adhere* requires the preposition *to* when following a goal.

(a) for

(d) at
[ANS] a

The preposition *for* follows *responsible* to indicate the duty.

(a) to

(a) the

[SOL]

[RULE] Infinitive after ‘have to’

(a) benefit

[ANS] a
[SOL]
‘Will’ is followed by the base form of the verb. ‘Benefit’ is correct.
उदा. नई नीति कई कर्मचारियों को लाभ पहुँचाएगी।
[RULE] Future Simple with Base Verb

---

[Q1765] She was ______ the award by the committee.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) presented
(b) to be presented
(c) presenting
(d) Redundant
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Passive voice: ‘was’ + past participle. ‘Presented’ is the correct past participle.
उदा. उसे समिति द्वारा पुरस्कार दिया गया।
[RULE] Passive Voice – Past Participle

---

[Q1766] He was accused ______ stealing the money.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) of
(b) to
(c) for
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition ‘of’ is used after ‘accused’ when followed by a gerund.
उदा. उसे पैसे चुराने के आरोप में गिरफ्तार किया गया।
[RULE] Preposition ‘of’ after ‘accused’

---

[Q1767] The book was written ______ a famous author.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2025]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition ‘by’ introduces the agent in passive constructions.
उदा. पुस्तक एक प्रसिद्ध लेखक द्वारा लिखी गई थी।
[RULE] Passive Voice – ‘by’

[Q1768] We need to schedule a meeting ______ the new quarter.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) in
(c) at
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
‘For’ is used to indicate the purpose or period of an event.

[RULE] Preposition ‘for’ with Time

[Q1769] She has been working here ____ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) from
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **since** is used to refer to a specific point in time from which an action continues. 
उदाहरण: वह 2010 से यहाँ काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Preposition of time – since

[Q1770] The team will meet ____ the manager tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) with
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The collocation **meet with** is used when referring to a meeting between people. 
उदाहरण: वे कल प्रबंधक से मिलेंगे।
[RULE] Collocation – meet with

[Q1771] He was known ____ a generous philanthropist.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) as
(b) for
(c) to
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase **known as** is used to describe a person’s reputation. 
उदाहरण: वह एक उदार परोपकारी के रूप में जाना जाता है।
[RULE] Adjective phrase – known as

---

[Q1772] The project is due ____ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) at
(c) by
(d) in
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The preposition **by** indicates the latest time by which something must be done. 
उदाहरण: परियोजना शुक्रवार तक पूरी होनी चाहिए।
[RULE] Preposition of deadline – by

---

[Q1773] I would have gone ____ if I had known.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) then
(b) there
(c) than
(d) their
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adverb **then** refers to a time in the past, contrasting with **than** which is a comparison word. 
उदाहरण: यदि मुझे पता होता तो मैं गया होता।
[RULE] Adverb – then vs than

---

[Q1774] The book was written ____ an acclaimed author.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) for
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **by** is used to indicate the author or creator of a work. 
उदाहरण: पुस्तक एक प्रसिद्ध लेखक द्वारा लिखी गई थी।
[RULE] Preposition of authorship – by

[Q1775] She is ___ of the company.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) the head
(b) head
(c) the manager
(d) manager
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When the noun *head* is used as a title, no article is required.

[Q1776] The verdict was announced ____ the court.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) at
(c) on
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **in** is used with *court* when referring to the location of a judgment. 
उदाहरण: निर्णय अदालत में घोषित किया गया।

(a) for

(c) from
(d) at
[ANS] a

The phrase **absent for** denotes a duration of absence.

[Q1778] The solution will be implemented ____ next week.

(a) before
(b) by
(c) in
(d) at

(a) to present
(b) presenting
(c) present
(d) presented
[ANS] b

Use the gerund after a verb of decision. Here, "has decided" is followed by the gerund "presenting".

[Q1780] He is looking forward ________ the new project next week.

(a) to going
(b) to go
(c) go
(d) gone
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrasal verb "look forward to" is followed by a gerund. The correct form is "to go".
उदाहरण: वह अगले हफ्ते नए प्रोजेक्ट को लेकर उत्सुक है।
[RULE] "look forward to" + gerund

[Q1781] She could not attend the meeting ________ her sudden illness.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because of
(b) due to
(c) due
(d) because
[ANS] b

"Due to" is a prepositional phrase used to explain a reason. It fits naturally after a noun phrase like "her sudden illness".

(a) about

[SOL]
"For" indicates the reason for the reprimand. 
उदाहरण: "उसकी रिपोर्ट जमा न करने के लिए उसे डांटा गया।"

(a) to submit

(c) submitting
(d) submission
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'asked', we use the infinitive form 'to submit'.

[ANS] a

(a) by

[ANS] a

With **neither…nor**, the verb is singular: **is**.

(a) were walking

Past continuous tense: **were walking**.

[SOL]
The infinitive **to phase out** is correct after *decided*. 
उदाहरण: कंपनी ने *to phase out* उत्पाद रेखा का निर्णय लिया.
[RULE] Infinitive after 'decided'

---

[Q1915] He is an expert ____ the ancient scripts.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) of
(c) for
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition **in** is used after *expert* to indicate field of expertise. 
उदाहरण: वह *in* प्राचीन पांडुलिपियों में विशेषज्ञ है.
[RULE] Preposition 'in' after 'expert'

---

[Q1916] We will ____ the meeting at 3 pm.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) start
(b) starting
(c) started
(d) to start
[ANS] d
[SOL]
The infinitive **to start** follows *will* for future action. 
उदाहरण: हम *to start* बैठक 3 बजे करेंगे.
[RULE] Infinitive after modal 'will'

---

[Q1917] The students were ____ to complete the assignment by Friday.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2026 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) expected
(b) expect
(c) expecting
(d) expects
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective **expected** correctly describes the students’ state. 
उदाहरण: छात्र *expected* थे कार्य पूरा करने के लिए.
[RULE] Adjective to describe expectation

---

[Q1918] The committee was unable to ___ a decision on the matter.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) reach
(b) reached
(c) reaching
(d) reaches
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a verb that indicates ability, the base form of the verb is used. Here, 'unable to ___' requires the infinitive form – 'reach'.
उदाहरण: मैं ___ नहीं सकता।
[RULE] Infinitive after 'unable to'

---

[Q1919] He is one of the few people ___ the country for more than a decade.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) who has lived
(b) who lives
(c) who had lived
(d) who living
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When referring to an action that started in the past and continues to the present, the present perfect tense is used: 'has lived'.
उदाहरण: वह वह व्यक्ति है जिसने देश में ______।
[RULE] Present perfect after 'one of the few people'

---

[Q1920] She was ___ the news about the accident.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) informed
(b) inform
(c) informing
(d) informing about
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The passive form with 'be' + past participle is required: 'was informed'.
उदाहरण: वह ___ समाचार के बारे में।
[RULE] Passive voice with past participle

---

[Q1921] The children were eager to ___ the new play ground.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) visit
(b) visiting
(c) visits
(d) visited
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'to' in an infinitive construction, the base form of the verb is used: 'visit'.
उदाहरण: वे ___ पार्क जाना चाहते हैं।
[RULE] Infinitive after 'to'

---

[Q1922] There is little ___ to be done before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) thing
(b) things
(c) anything
(d) something
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Little' with a countable noun requires singular: 'little thing'.
उदाहरण: बहुत कम ___ बचे हैं।
[RULE] Singular noun after 'little'

---

[Q1923] The manager asked the employees ___ their reports by Friday.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) submit
(b) submitted
(c) submitting
(d) to submit
[ANS] d
[SOL]
'Ask' followed by a verb is normally followed by an infinitive with 'to': 'to submit'.
उदाहरण: उसने कर्मचारियों को ___ रिपोर्ट जमा करने को कहा।
[RULE] Infinitive after 'ask'

---

[Q1924] I have never ___ such a beautiful sun rise
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) seen
(b) see
(c) seeing
(d) saw
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After 'have never', the present perfect tense is used: 'seen'.
उदाहरण: मैंने कभी ___ नहीं देखा।
[RULE] Present perfect after 'have never'

---

[Q1925] The new policy will be implemented ___ next month.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'In' is used with months and years to indicate a future time: 'in next month'.
उदाहरण: नीति ___ अगले महीने लागू होगी।
[RULE] Preposition 'in' with months

---

[Q1926] She was known ___ her sharp wit.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase 'known for' is idiomatic; it means 'reputed to have' a particular trait.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी चतुराई के लिए ___ जानी जाती है।
[RULE] Idiomatic phrase 'known for'

---

[Q1927] He has been working here ______ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Preposition **since** is used to indicate the starting point of an action that is still continuing.
उदाहरण: वह 2010 से यहाँ काम कर रहा है।
[RULE] Preposition 'since' for starting time

---

[Q1928] The book, ______ the author is unknown, is quite popular.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) whose
(b) where
(c) which
(d) that
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Relative pronoun **whose** is used to show possession in a relative clause.
उदाहरण: पुस्तक, जिसके लेखक अज्ञात हैं, बहुत लोकप्रिय है।
[RULE] Relative pronoun 'whose' for possession

---

[Q1929] She couldn't attend the meeting ______ a sudden illness.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because of
(b) due to
(c) on account of
(d) by the way
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Preposition **due to** is used to give a reason for something.
उदाहरण: वह अचानक बीमारी के कारण बैठक में नहीं जा सकी।
[RULE] Preposition 'due to' for giving reason

---

[Q1930] ______ are you collaborating with on this project?
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Whom
(b) Who
(c) Whose
(d) Which
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Relative pronoun **Whom** is used as the object of a verb or preposition in a relative clause.
उदाहरण: आप इस परियोजना पर किसके साथ सहयोग कर रहे हैं? (Whom are you collaborating with?)
[RULE] Relative pronoun 'Whom' as object

---

[Q1931] The company has been expanding its operations, ______ the new market.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) into
(b) in
(c) on
(d) to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition **into** indicates movement or transition into a place or situation.
उदाहरण: कंपनी ने नए बाजार में प्रवेश किया।
[RULE] Preposition 'into' for movement

---

[Q1932] He is known ______ his kindness.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition **for** is used after the verb *known* to indicate the reason or attribute.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी दयालुता के लिए जाना जाता है।
[RULE] Preposition 'for' after 'known'

---

[Q1933] The results were inconclusive, ______ the data was insufficient.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) but
(b) as
(c) so
(d) and
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Subordinating conjunction **as** introduces a reason or cause.
उदाहरण: परिणाम अनिश्चित थे, क्योंकि डेटा पर्याप्त नहीं था।
[RULE] Subordinating conjunction 'as' for cause

---

[Q1934] She was _______________ by the spectacular performance.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) awed
(b) awarded
(c) awarded by
(d) Pensive
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Verb **aw** (awed) means to impress greatly.
उदाहरण: वह शानदार प्रदर्शन से प्रभावित हुई।
[RULE] Verb 'aw' meaning 'to impress'

---

[Q1935] He always arrives at the office ______ 9 a.m.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) at
(c) on
(d) for
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Preposition **by** indicates the latest time by which something should happen.
उदाहरण: वह हमेशा 9 बजे तक कार्यालय पहुँचता है।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' for latest time

---

[Q1936] The manager asked the team to finish the task within ______ day.
[EXAM: SSC MTS 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) a
(b) an
(c) the
(d) one
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Determinant **one** is used before a singular countable noun to specify a single instance.
उदाहरण: प्रबंधक ने टीम से एक दिन के भीतर कार्य पूरा करने को कहा।
[RULE] Determinant 'one' for singular countable noun

[Q1937] She has been working here ______ five years.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) from
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used to indicate a duration of time.
उदाहरण: "She has been working here for five years."
[RULE] Preposition of duration after 'has been working'

---

[Q1938] They have been living in this town ______ 2003.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) from
(b) since
(c) for
(d) during
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use *since* to refer to the starting point of an action that continues up to now.
उदाहरण: "They have been living in this town since 2003."
[RULE] Preposition *since* for a starting point

---

[Q1939] He was very excited ______ the news.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) about
(b) of
(c) for
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *about* follows *excited* to indicate the subject of excitement.
उदाहरण: "He was very excited about the news."
[RULE] Preposition *about* after 'excited'

---

[Q1940] I have never seen such a beautiful flower ______.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) as
(b) like
(c) than
(d) so
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use *like* for comparisons in the form *such as*.
उदाहरण: "I have never seen such a beautiful flower like this."
[RULE] Comparison using *like* with *such a*

---

[Q1941] She ______ to the party if she had known about it.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) would have gone
(b) would go
(c) will go
(d) has gone
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The *third conditional* uses *would have* + past participle.
उदाहरण: "She would have gone to the party if she had known about it."
[RULE] Third conditional: would have + past participle

---

[Q1942] The manager was praised ______ his leader ship.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) to
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *praised* is followed by *for* when expressing the reason.
उदाहरण: "The manager was praised for his leadership."
[RULE] Preposition *for* after 'praised'

---

[Q1943] It is necessary ______ complete the paper work before the dead line
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) in
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The infinitive *to* follows the adjective *necessary*.
उदाहरण: "It is necessary to complete the paperwork before the deadline."
[RULE] Infinitive 'to' after adjective *necessary*

---

[Q1944] He apologized ______ the mistake.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) about
(c) to
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb *apologized* is followed by *for* when indicating the subject of apology.
उदाहरण: "He apologized for the mistake."
[RULE] Preposition *for* after 'apologized'

---

[Q1945] The painting was admired ______ all visitors.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) with
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The passive construction uses *admired* + *by* to indicate the doers of the action.
उदाहरण: "The painting was admired by all visitors."
[RULE] Passive voice with preposition *by*

---

[Q1946] He will not attend the meeting ______ he is ill.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) unless
(b) if
(c) before
(d) since
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The conjunction *unless* introduces a condition that must not be met for the main clause to happen.
उदाहरण: "He will not attend the meeting unless he is ill."
[RULE] Conditionals with *unless*

---

[Q1947] She has been working in the _____ position for five years.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) senior
(b) the senior
(c) a senior
(d) seniorly
[ANS] b
[SOL]
When a noun phrase follows the preposition *in*, it usually requires an article. Hence the correct form is *the senior*. 
उदाहरण: वह पाँच वर्षों से वरिष्ठ पद पर काम कर रही है।
[RULE] Preposition + Noun Phrase (Article required)

---

[Q1948] The manager will _____ the project by next week.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) finish
(b) finishes
(c) finished
(d) finishing
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After a modal verb like *will*, the base form of the verb is used. Therefore, *finish* is correct. 
उदाहरण: प्रबंधक अगले हफ्ते तक परियोजना समाप्त करेंगे।
[RULE] Modal + Base Verb

---

[Q1949] He was _____ to the news.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) surprised
(b) surprise
(c) surprising
(d) surprisedly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective *surprised* correctly modifies *he* in this context. 
उदाहरण: वह समाचार से हैरान हुआ।
[RULE] Adjective used as predicate adjective

---

[Q1950] They have been living in this city _____ 2010.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) before
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *since* is used to indicate the starting point of an action that continues to the present. 
उदाहरण: वे 2010 से इस शहर में रह रहे हैं।
[RULE] Since + Time Point

---

[Q1951] I will call you _____ I arrive.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) once
(b) when
(c) after
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adverbial conjunction *once* is commonly used to mean 'as soon as'. 
उदाहरण: मैं पहुँचते ही आपको कॉल करूँगा।
[RULE] Adverbial Conjunctions (once, when)

---

[Q1952] She was praised _____ her hard work.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) of
(c) with
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *for* expresses the reason for the praise. 
उदाहरण: उसके कठिन परिश्रम के लिए उसकी प्रशंसा की गई।
[RULE] Preposition of Reason (for)

---

[Q1953] He has been absent _____ several days.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) during
(d) at
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The preposition *for* is used with a duration of time. 
उदाहरण: वह कई दिनों से अनुपस्थित रहा है।
[RULE] Preposition for Duration

---

[Q1954] Please submit the report _____ Friday.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) before
(c) on
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *by* indicates a deadline. 
उदाहरण: रिपोर्ट शुक्रवार तक जमा करें।
[RULE] Deadline with By

---

[Q1955] The book is _____ the table.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) on
(b) in
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *on* is used to express position on a surface. 
उदाहरण: पुस्तक मेज़ पर है।
[RULE] Position Prepositions (on, in, at)

---

[Q1956] They went _____ the mountains for a week end
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) into
(c) in
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition *to* indicates movement towards a destination. 
उदाहरण: वे सप्ताहांत के लिए पहाड़ों गए।
[RULE] Direction Preposition (to)

---

[Q1957] If you _____ the instructions carefully, you will succeed.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) follow
(b) following
(c) follows
(d) followed
[ANS] a
[SOL]
After the conditional *if*, the base form of the verb is used. 
उदाहरण: यदि आप निर्देशों का ध्यानपूर्वक पालन करते हैं, तो आप सफल होंगे।
[RULE] Conditional + Base Verb

---

[Q1958] The project was completed ______ a month.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) in
(b) on
(c) at
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the preposition *in* to indicate a period of time.
उदाहरण: प्रोजेक्ट एक महीने में पूरा हुआ।
[RULE] Preposition for time duration

---

[Q1959] The manager was praised ______ his dedication to the project.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) by
(c) with
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use the preposition *for* to indicate the reason for praise.
उदाहरण: प्रबंधक को परियोजना के प्रति उसकी समर्पण के लिए सराहा गया।
[RULE] Preposition for reason

---

[Q1960] She is allergic ______ peanuts.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) with
(d) on
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *to* after the adjective *allergic*.
उदाहरण: वह मूंगफली से एलर्जिक है।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'to'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।

[Q1961] They have been married ______ fifteen years.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) since
(c) during
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *for* to express a duration of time.
उदाहरण: वे पंद्रह वर्षों से विवाहित हैं।
[RULE] Preposition for duration

---

[Q1962] I am looking forward ______ meeting you.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) to
(b) for
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *to* after the verb *looking forward*.
उदाहरण: मैं आपकी मुलाकात की प्रतीक्षा कर रहा हूँ।
[RULE] Looking forward to

[Q1963] The company is seeking a candidate ______ strong analytical skills.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) with
(b) who has
(c) having
(d) that has
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *with* to link a noun to its characteristic.
उदाहरण: कंपनी ऐसे उम्मीदवार की तलाश कर रही है जिसके पास मजबूत विश्लेषणात्मक कौशल हो।

[Q1964] Please submit the report ______________ Monday.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) on
(c) at
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *by* to indicate a deadline.
उदाहरण: कृपया रिपोर्ट सोमवार तक जमा करें।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'by'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] By for deadline

[Q1965] He was reprimanded ______________ his care less behavior.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) at
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *for* to state the reason for reprimand.
उदाहरण: उसे उसकी लापरवाह व्यवहार के लिए डांटा गया।
[RULE] Reprimanded for

---

[Q1966] The lecture will start ______________ 9 AM.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIV (2026)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) at
(b) by
(c) on
(d) in
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use *at* to indicate a specific time.
उदाहरण: व्याख्यान 9 बजे शुरू होगा।
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: रिक्त स्थान में 'at'  का प्रयोग वाक्य के संदर्भ और व्याकरण की दृष्टि से पूर्णतः सही है।
[RULE] At for specific time

[Q1967] She has been trying to improve her English language skills ______ she has taken an intensive course last year.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) since
(b) for
(c) by
(d) with
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use of 'since' to indicate the starting point of an action that continues to the present.
उदाहरण: वह अपनी अंग्रेजी कौशल में सुधार कर रही है **since** उसने पिछले साल एक गहन पाठ्यक्रम लिया था।
[RULE] Preposition 'since' for time

[Q1968] The committee is expected to announce its decision ______ Friday.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) by
(b) in
(c) of
(d) during
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'by' to denote a deadline or latest time before which something should happen.
उदाहरण: समिति का निर्णय **by** शुक्रवार तक घोषित किया जाना चाहिए।
[RULE] Preposition 'by' for deadlines

---

[Q1969] I would have gone to the party, but I was feeling ill ______ I could not make it.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) because
(b) although
(c) since
(d) unless
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Use 'although' to present a contrast between two clauses.
उदाहरण: मैंने पार्टी में जाना चाहा, **although** मैं बीमार था, इसलिए नहीं जा सका।
[RULE] Conjunction 'although' for contrast

[Q1970] The new policy will be implemented "_______" the results of the recent survey.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) following
(b) on
(c) during
(d) before
[ANS] a
[SOL]
When a policy is implemented after obtaining results, use 'following'.
उदाहरण: नई नीति **following** सर्वेक्षण के परिणामों के बाद लागू की जाएगी।
[RULE] Preposition 'following' to indicate sequence

[Q1971] The manager was praised ______ his ability to resolve conflicts.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2026 Tier-2]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) with
(d) at
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Use 'for' after a verb like 'praised' to indicate the reason.

[RULE] Preposition after 'praised'

[Q1972] He will not attend the meeting ______ he has a prior commitment.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) as
(b) if
(c) unless
(d) when
[ANS] c
[SOL]
'Unless' introduces a condition that negates the main clause.
उदाहरण: वह बैठक में नहीं आएगा **unless** उसकी पूर्व प्रतिबद्धता हो।
[RULE] Conjunction 'unless' for negative condition

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) if

(d) since

[Q1974] She ______ us with in form at i on about the new project during the meeting.

(c) is giving

Present perfect 'has given' is used for an action that occurred before another past event.
उदाहरण: उसने बैठक के दौरान हमें परियोजना के बारे में **has given** जानकारी दी।
[RULE] Present perfect for past actions with present relevance

(a) on

[RULE] Preposition 'on' for digital location

(a) because of

'Because of' is used to indicate the cause of an event.
उदाहरण: वह भारी ट्रैफ़िक के कारण **because of** देरी से स्टेशन पहुँचा।

(a) for

When an adjective modifies a noun, the adjective itself is used: *brief* means short.

(a) of

The noun *habit* is followed by *of* to indicate the action.

(a) to adopt

When a gerund is used as the object of *was*, the form is *giving*.

(b) attending
(c) attended

Future continuous uses *be + present participle*: *will be attending*.

(a) patients
(b) patient
(c) patience
(d) Ephemeral
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The noun *patience* is the correct word meaning calm acceptance. Example: The doctor was praised for his patience attitude.
उदाहरण: डॉक्टर को उसकी धैर्यपूर्ण रवैये के लिए प्रशंसा मिली।
[RULE] Patience – noun meaning tolerance

[Q2016] She will ____ to the conference tomorrow.
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) attend
(b) attends
(c) attending
(d) attended
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The future simple verb form for a scheduled action is the base form. Example: She will attend the conference tomorrow.
उदाहरण: वह कल सम्मेलन में उपस्थित होंगी।
[RULE] Future simple – will + base form
